dukkhasamudayādidhammassa aikattavaimattatā ācariyaina vibhattā, amhaihi ca ñātā, ‘‘tāya aikattavaimattatāya kattha pucchitai sati adhiṭṭhānaṃ vīmaṃsitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā suttādikai pucchitai sati vīmaṃsitabbanti dassaituṃ ‘‘aivaṃ suttai vā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha aivaṃ iminā vuttappakāraina suttai vā pucchitai, vaiyyākaraṇai vā pucchitai, gāthāyaṃ vā pucchitāyaṃ sati adhiṭṭhānaṃ vīmaṃsitabbaṃ| ‘‘kiṃ vīmaṃsitabba’’nti pucchaiyya ‘‘aikattatāya pucchati kiṃ, udāhu vaimattatāya pucchati ki’’nti vīmaṃsitabbanti yaujanā| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana – ‘‘idāni tāva aikattavaimattatāvisayai niyaujaitvā dassaituṃ ‘suttai vā vaiyyākaraṇai vā’tiādi vutta’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 48) vuttaṃ| ‘‘kathaṃ pucchitaṃ, kathaṃ vissaj‍jitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘yadi aikattatāyā’’tiādi  vuttaṃ| ‘‘taināhā’’tiādyānusandhyādikau ca ‘‘niyuttau adhiṭṭhānau hārau’’ti imassa anusandhyādikau ca vuttanayānusāraina vaiditabbau|

iti adhiṭṭhānahāravibhaṅgai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana aṭṭhakathāṭīkānusāraina gambhīratthau vitthāratau vibhajitvā gahaitabbauti|

15. parikkhārahāravibhaṅgavibhāvanā

49. yaina  yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina vibhaṅgaina dukkhasac‍cādīnaṃ aikattatādayau vibhattā, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau parikkhārahāravibhaṅgau nāmā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau parikkhārau hārau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha tatthāti taisu niddiṭṭhaisu sauḷasasu daisanāhārādīsu katamau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau parikkhārau hārau parikkhārahāravibhaṅgau nāmāti pucchati| ‘‘yai dhammā yaṃ dhammaṃ janayantī’’tiādiniddaisassa idāni mayā vuc‍camānau ‘‘yau dhammau yaṃ dhammaṃ janayati, tassa sau parikkhārau’’tiādikau parikkhārabhūtassa haitunau caiva pac‍cayassa ca vitthārasaṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau parikkhāravibhaṅgau nāma|

‘‘katamau saṃvaṇṇaitabbau parikkhārau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘yau dhammau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha yau haitupac‍cayappakārau dhammau yaṃ phalabhūtaṃ dhammaṃ janayati janaiti, tassa phaladhammassa sau haitupac‍cayappakārau dhammau parikkhārau nāma| ‘‘kiṃlakkhaṇau parikkhārau’’ti pucchitabbattā tathā pucchitvā lakkhaṇavisaisaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘kiṃlakkhaṇau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana – ‘‘tattha ‘yau dhammau yaṃ dhammaṃ janayati, tassa sau parikkhārau’ti saṅkhaipatau parikkhāralakkhaṇaṃ vatvā taṃ vibhāgaina dassaituṃ ‘kiṃlakkhaṇau’tiādi vutta’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 49) vuttaṃ| ‘‘kittakā dhammā janayantī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘dvai dhammā janayanti haitu ca pac‍cayau cā’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘haitupi kāraṇaṃ, pac‍cayaupi kāraṇaṃ, tasmā kāraṇāyaiva kaina lakkhaṇaina dvidhā vuttā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha kiṃlakkhaṇau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| janitabbaphalatau añ‍ñaihi phalaihi asādhāraṇalakkhaṇau haitu, sabbaphalaihi sādhāraṇalakkhaṇau pac‍cayau, iminā visaisalakkhaṇaina dvidhā vattabbāti atthau| ‘‘sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇavisaisau kīdisau bhavai’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘yathā kiṃ bhavai’’ti pucchitvā ‘‘yathā aṅkurassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yathā yau sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇavisaisau atthi, tathā sau sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇavisaisau kiṃ viya bhavaiti atthau| aṅkurassa nibbattiyā bījaṃ asādhāraṇaṃ yathā, tathā haitu phalassa nibbattiyā asādhāraṇau bhavai| pathavī ca āpau ca aṅkurassa nibbattiyā sādhāraṇā bhavanti yathā, tathā pac‍cayau phalassa nibbattiyā sādhāraṇau bhavai| sabbaphalassa pac‍cayattā aṅkurassa bījaṃ asādhāraṇaṃ janakaṃ haitu| ‘‘kathaṃ pathavī, āpau ca sādhāraṇā janakāti saddahitabbā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘aṅkurassa hī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| samaṃ samānaṃ phalaṃ bhavāpaitīti sabhāvau, kau sau? bījaṃ haituyaiva| ‘‘kiṃ haitupac‍cayānaṃ visaisau bījaṅkuraupamāyaiva dassaitabbau, udāhu añ‍ñūpamāyapi dassaitabbau’’ti vattabbattā añ‍ñāya upamāyapi  visaisaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘yathā vā panā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| imāya upamāyapi haitupac‍cayānaṃ visaisau vijānitabbauti adhippāyau|

bījaṅkurādīsu bāhiraisu parikkhārabhūtānaṃ haitupac‍cayānaṃ visaisau ācariyaina vibhattau, amhaihi ca ñātau, ‘‘kathaṃ ajjhattikaisu vibhattau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘ayañhi saṃsārau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| atha vā ‘‘bāhiraisu parikkhārabhūtau haitupac‍cayau yuttau hautu, kathaṃ ajjhattikaisu yuttau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘ayañhi saṃsārau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana – ‘‘aivaṃ bāhiraṃ haitupac‍cayavibhāgaṃ dassaitvā idāni ajjhattikaṃ dassaituṃ ‘ayañhi saṃsārau’tiādi vutta’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 49) vuttaṃ| haitupac‍cayaihi saha saṃsārau bhavatīti sahaitupac‍cayau| ayaṃ saṃsārau hi yasmā sahaitupac‍cayau hutvā nibbattau, tasmā ajjhattikaipi parikkhārabhūtau haitupac‍cayau yuttauyaivāti daṭṭhabbauti adhippāyau|

sau  imassa saṃsārassa sahaitupac‍cayattaṃ yadi bhagavatā vuttaṃ, aivaṃ sati amhaihi saddahitabbaṃ, ‘‘kathaṃ saddahitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘aivañhī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aivanti iminā avij‍jādinā haitupac‍cayaina sabbau paṭic‍casamuppādau saṃsārauti nibbattauti bhagavatā saṃsārassa sahaitupac‍cayattaṃ vuttaṃ, tasmā saddahitabbaṃ| avij‍jādayau saṅkhārādīnaṃ pac‍cayau hautu, ‘‘katamau avij‍jāya haitū’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘iti avij‍jā avij‍jāya haitū’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘katamau avij‍jāya pac‍cayau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘ayaunisau manasikārau pac‍cayau’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘katamā avij‍jā katamāya avij‍jāya haitū’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘purimikā avij‍jā pacchimikāya avij‍jāya haitū’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘katamā purimikā avij‍jā katamā pacchimikā avij‍jā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tatthā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānassa haitubhūtau purimau avij‍jānusayau samanantarauva kiṃ, udāhu paramparahaitupi hautī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘bījaṅkurau viyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bījaṅkurauti bījānaṃ aṅkurauti bījaṅkurau, bījānaṃ samanantarahaitutāya aṅkurau nibbattati viya| yattha rukkhādikai yaṃ phalaṃ nibbattati, tasmiṃ rukkhādikai nibbattassa assa phalassa idaṃ bījaṃ pana paramparahaitutāya haitubhūtaṃ bhavati|

‘‘bījaṃ pana aikaṃyaiva hauti, kathaṃ dvidhā vattabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘duvidhau hī’’tiādi vuttaṃ, samanantarakālaparamparakālabhaidaina haitupi duvidhau hautiyaivāti atthau| bījabhūtau haitu duvidhau yathā, aivaṃ avij‍jāya haitubhūtau avij‍jānusayaupi samanantarahaitu ca paramparahaitu cāti kālabhaidaina duvidhau bhavati, samanantarau avij‍jānusayau samanantarassa avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānassa samanantarahaitu  hauti| purimatarau avij‍jānusayau pacchimatarassa avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānassa paramparahaitu hauti| iti bījabhūtau asādhāraṇau haitu, pathavīāpādikau sādhāraṇau pac‍cayauti visaisau pākaṭau yathā, aivaṃ avij‍jānusayau asādhāraṇau haitu, ayaunisaumanasikārau sādhāraṇau pac‍cayauti visaisau daṭṭhabbau|

‘‘aittakainaiva haitupac‍cayānaṃ visaisau vattabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘yathā vā pana thālakañ‍cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana – ‘‘yathā vā panātiādināpi  haitupac‍cayavibhāgamaiva dassaitī’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha padīpassa pac‍cayabhūtaṃ thālakañ‍ca vaṭṭi ca tailañ‍ca padīpassa sabhāvahaitu samānahaitu na hautīti yaujanā| ‘‘padīpassa pac‍cayabhūtampi thālakādikaṃ sabhāvahaitu na hautīti kasmā saddahitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘na hi sak‍kā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| padīpassa pac‍cayabhūtaṃ anaggikaṃ aggirahitaṃ thālakañ‍ca vaṭṭiñ‍ca tailañ‍ca dīpaituṃ jālaituṃ hi yasmā na sak‍kā, tasmā purimau padīpau pacchimassa padīpassa sabhāvahaitu hauti viya, aivaṃ thālakādikaṃ sabhāvahaitu na hauti| iti aivaṃpakārau sabhāvau samānau padīpau haitu hauti yathā, parabhāvau asamānau thālakādikau pac‍cayau hauti yathā ca, tathā ajjhattikau sabhāvau haitu hauti, bāhirau asamānau pac‍cayau hauti| janakau avij‍jānusayau avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānassa haitu hauti, pariggāhakau upatthambhakau pac‍cayau hauti| añ‍ñaihi phalaihi asādhāraṇau haitu hauti, sabbaihi phalaihi sādhāraṇau pac‍cayau hautīti yaujaitvā padīpaupamāyapi haitupac‍cayānaṃ pākaṭau visaisau daṭṭhabbauti adhippāyau|

haitupac‍cayappabhaidaṃ kāraṇaṃ parikkhārauti ācariyaina vuttaṃ, tassa kāraṇassa kāraṇabhāvau ca phalāpaikkhau hauti, tasmā ‘‘yau kāraṇabhāvau yainākāraina hauti, katamau sau kāraṇabhāvau, katamau sau ākārau’’ti pucchitabbattā ca ‘‘yaṃ phalaṃ yaina visaisaina hauti, katamaṃ taṃ phalaṃ, katamau sau visaisau’’ti pucchitabbattā ca ‘‘kāraṇaphalānaṃ yau sambandhau hauti, katamau sau sambandhau’’ti pucchitabbattā ca taṃ sabbaṃ vibhāvaituṃ ‘‘avupacchaidatthau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ayamanusandhyatthau ca aṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘idāni yasmā kāraṇaṃ ‘parikkhārau’ti vuttaṃ, kāraṇabhāvau ca phalāpaikkhāya, tasmā kāraṇassa yau kāraṇabhāvau yathā ca sau hauti, yañ‍ca phalaṃ, yau ca tassa visaisau, yau ca kāraṇaphalānaṃ sambandhau, taṃ sabbaṃ vibhāvaituṃ ‘avupacchaidatthau’tiādi vutta’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 49) iminā vuttauti daṭṭhabbau|

tattha avupacchin‍nassa haitupac‍cayasaṅkhātassa kāraṇassa yau anupacchaidatthau atthi, sau anupacchaidatthau  santatiatthau hauti phalaina sambandhattā, yañ‍ca kāraṇaṃ attanau phalassa janakaṃ upatthambhakaṃ hutvā nirujjhati, sau anupacchin‍nau aiva nāma hautīti| yañ‍ca phalaṃ añ‍ñassa kāraṇaṃ hutvā nirujjhati, tasmiṃ anupacchin‍nai tassa ca santatiatthau hauti| yaṃ pana phalaṃ añ‍ñassa phalassa kāraṇaṃ ahutvā nirujjhati, taṃ upacchin‍naṃ hauti, yathā taṃ arahatau cuticittanti| kāraṇatau nibbattassa phalassa yau nibbattiatthau atthi, sau nibbattiatthau phalatthau hauti| paṭisandhikkhandhānaṃ yau paṭisandhiatthau paṭisandahanatthau atthi, sau paṭisandhiatthau punabbhavatthau punabbhavanatthau hauti| kilaisānaṃ yau palibaudhatthau santānai uppaj‍janatthau atthi, sau palibaudhatthau pariyuṭṭhānatthau hauti| kilaisānaṃ maggaina yau asamugghātatthau atthi, sau asamugghātatthau anusayatthau hauti| avij‍jāya catun‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ yau asampaṭivaidhatthau atthi, sau asampaṭivaidhatthau avij‍jatthau hauti| arahattamaggaina yau apariñ‍ñātatthau atthi, sau apariñ‍ñātatthau viñ‍ñāṇassa paṭisandhiviñ‍ñāṇassa bījatthau hauti|

aittāvatā kāraṇabhāvau ca kāraṇākārau ca phalañ‍ca phalavisaisau ca ācariyaina vibhattau, amhaihi ca ñātau, ‘‘katamau paramparahaitupac‍cayatthau, katamau ca sambandhatthau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘yattha avupacchaidau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yattha yassaṃ rūpārūpapavattiyaṃ avupacchin‍nassa haitupac‍cayappabhaidassa kāraṇassa yau avupacchaidau atthi, sau avupacchaidau tattha tissaṃ rūpārūpapavattiyaṃ santati hauti| yattha yassaṃ rūpārūpapavattiyaṃ yā santati atthi, sā santatitattha rūpārūpapavattiyaṃ nibbatti hautītiādinā yaujaitvā paramparahaituādikau viñ‍ñātabbau|

sīlakkhandhauti parisuddhasīlakkhandhau| samādhikkhandhassāti mahaggatakkhandhassa, samādhipaṭṭhānau hi mahaggatadhammau| pañ‍ñākkhandhauti maggaphalapañ‍ñāpadhānakkhandhau| sau hi vimuttiñāṇadassanasaṅkhātassa pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇakkhandhassa pac‍cayau hauti| titthañ‍ñutādīnaṃ atthau padaṭṭhānahāravibhaṅgavaṇṇanāyaṃ vuttauva|

sabhāvau haitūti ācariyaina vuttau, ‘‘kīdisau sau sabhāvau haitū’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘yathā vā pana cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuñ‍ca cakkhundriyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca nissayaṃ katvā rūpai paṭic‍ca ārammaṇaṃ katvā uppaj‍jati| tattha cakkhādīsu cakkhundriyaṃ ādhipataiyyapac‍cayatāya indriyapac‍cayatāya cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa pac‍cayau, rūpārammaṇaṃ puraijātārammaṇapac‍cayatāya  pac‍cayau, ālaukau san‍nissayatāya upanissayatāya pac‍cayau hauti| sau pac‍cayau hauntau phalaina cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaina asamānattā sabhāvau haitu na hauti, pac‍cayau ca hauti manasikārau| kiriyamanaudhātu pana phalaina cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaina viñ‍ñāṇabhāvaina samānattā sabhāvau haitu hauti yathā, aivaṃ saṅkhārā nāmakkhandhabhāvaina samānattā viñ‍ñāṇassa pac‍cayā hauntā sabhāvau haitu haunti| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nāmarūpaina aikasantativasaina samānattā nāmarūpassa pac‍cayau hauntaṃ sabhāvau haitu hauti| iminā nayaina ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ saḷāyatanassā’’tiādīsupi atthau vaiditabbau| aivaṃ vuttappakārau haitu, pac‍cayau janakau, upatthambhakau ca yau kauci upanissayau balavapac‍cayau hauti, sabbau sau haitupac‍cayau janakaupatthambhakau janitabbupatthambhiyassa phalassa parikkharaṇatau abhisaṅkharaṇatau nippariyāyatau parikkhārau nāma|

‘‘vuttappakārau haitupac‍cayau parikkhārau nāmāti kaina amhaihi saddahitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taināhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taina kāraṇabhūtaina sabbassa haitupac‍cayassa parikkhārabhāvaina āyasmā mahākac‍cānau ‘‘yai dhammā yaṃ dhammaṃ janayantī’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, taina vacanaina saddahitabbau, ‘‘yai dhammā yaṃ dhammaṃ janayantī’’ti vacanaṃ nissāya tumhaihi sal‍lakkhaitabbauti adhippāyau|

‘‘aittakauva parikkhārau hārau yuñ‍jitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau parikkhārau hārau’’ti vuttaṃ, yau yau parikkhārau hārau yuñ‍jitabbau, sau sau parikkhārau hārau nīharitvā yuttau yuñ‍jitabbauti|

iti parikkhārahāravibhaṅgai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana aṭṭhakathāṭīkānusāraina gambhīratthau vitthāratau vibhajitvā gahaitabbauti|

16. samāraupanahāravibhaṅgavibhāvanā

50. yaina  yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina parikkhārahāravibhaṅgaina suttatthānaṃ haitupac‍cayau vibhattau, sau…pai॰… vibhaṅgau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau samāraupanahāravibhaṅgau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau samāraupanau hārau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha taisu niddiṭṭhaisu sauḷasasu daisanāhārādīsu katamau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau samāraupanau hārau samāraupanahāravibhaṅgau nāmāti pucchati| ‘‘yai dhammā yaṃmūlā’’tiādiniddaisassa idāni mayā vuc‍camānau ‘‘aikasmiṃ padaṭṭhānai yattakāni padaṭṭhānāni autarantī’’tiādikau vitthārasaṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau samāraupanahāravibhaṅgau nāma|

‘‘kittakai padaṭṭhānai suttai vuttai kittakāni padaṭṭhānāni samāraupayitabbānī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘aikasmiṃ padaṭṭhānai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aikasmiṃ padaṭṭhānai suttai vuttai sati avuttāni yattakāni padaṭṭhānāni autaranti samausaranti, sabbāni tāni avuttāni padaṭṭhānāni suttai vuttāni viya niddhāraṇavasaina ānaitvā daisanāya āraupayitabbāni| ‘‘kāni viya samāraupayitabbānī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘yathā āvaṭṭai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| āvaṭṭai hārai aikasmiṃ padaṭṭhānai suttai vuttai sati suttai avuttāni bahukāni padaṭṭhānāni autaranti, tāni bahukāni padaṭṭhānāni pariyaisitabbāni yathā, aivaṃ samāraupanai hāraipi bahukāni padaṭṭhānāni daisanāya samāraupayitabbānīti atthau|

‘‘kaivalaṃ pana padaṭṭhānavasainaiva samāraupanā kātabbā kiṃ, udāhu añ‍ñavasaināpi samāraupanā kātabbā ki’’nti vattabbattā añ‍ñavasaināpi samāraupanā kātabbā; tasmā samāraupanā catubbidhā kātabbāti dassaintau ‘‘tattha samāraupanā catubbidhā’’tiādimāha| tattha tatthāti tāsu samāraupayitabbasamāraupanāsu padaṭṭhānaṃ padaṭṭhānasamāraupanā, vaivacanaṃ vaivacanasamāraupanā, bhāvanā bhāvanāsamāraupanā, pahānaṃ pahānasamāraupanā, iti iminā pabhaidaina samāraupanā catubbidhā kātabbā|

‘‘tāsu catubbidhāsu samāraupanāsu katamā padaṭṭhānasamāraupanā’’ti pucchitabbattā tathā pucchitvā padaṭṭhānasamāraupanaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha  katamā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| suttai vuttaina padaṭṭhānaina suttai avuttānaṃ padaṭṭhānānaṃ samāraupanā katamāti pucchati|

sabbapāpassa  akusalassa yaṃ akaraṇaṃ akaraṇahaitu sāsanaṃ atthi, aitaṃ sāsanaṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ auvādau hauti, atha vā akaraṇaṃ akaraṇatthāya yaṃ sāsanaṃ atthi, aitaṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ auvādau hauti, na yassa kassaci sāsananti atthau| akaraṇanti hi sampadānatthai pavattaṃ pac‍cattavacanaṃ yathā ‘‘kissa atthāya kimattha’’nti| kusalassa sampadā sampadāya yaṃ sāsanaṃ atthi, aitaṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ| sacittapariyaudāpanaṃ sacittapariyaudāpanatthaṃ yaṃ sāsanaṃ atthi, aitaṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ hauti|

iti aivaṃpakāraina vuttassa tassa sāsanassa kiṃ padaṭṭhānanti visaisassa visaisapadaṭṭhānaṃ puna pucchati| idaṃ sucaritattayaṃ sāsanassa auvādassa padaṭṭhānaṃ sucaritattayaina haitunā sāsanattāti daṭṭhabbaṃ yathā ‘‘an‍naina vasatī’’tiādi| ‘‘sucaritattayai padaṭṭhānai vuttai katamaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ samāraupayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yaṃ kāyikañ‍cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ khandhattayaṃ sāsanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ samāraupayitabbaṃ, ‘‘khandhattayai padaṭṭhānai samāraupayitai katamaṃ samāraupayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha sīlakkhandhau cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ samathavipassanādvayaṃ sāsanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ samāraupayitabbaṃ| ‘‘samathavipassanādvayai padaṭṭhānai samāraupayitai katamaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ samāraupayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha samathassa phala’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ phaladvayaṃ sāsanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ samāraupayitabbaṃ|

sāsanassa padaṭṭhānāni samāraupayitabbānīti ācariyaina niddhāraitvā vibhattāni, amhaihi ca ñātāni, ‘‘idāni katamassa katamaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ samāraupayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘vanaṃ vanathassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ kāmaguṇapañ‍cakaṃ vanaṃ taṇhābhūtassa vanathassa padaṭṭhānaṃ taṇhāvatthubhāvatau, ‘‘itthī’’ti vā ‘‘purisau’’ti vā nimittaggāhasaṅkhātaṃ idaṃ vanaṃ ‘‘ahau cakkhu, ahau sautaṃ, ahau ghānaṃ, ahau jivhā, ahau kāyau’’ti taisaṃ taisaṃ aṅgapac‍caṅgānaṃ anubyañ‍janaggāhasaṅkhātassa vanathassa padaṭṭhānaṃ samāraupayitabbaṃ| apariñ‍ñātaṃ dvādasāyatanasaṅkhātaṃ idaṃ vanaṃ saṃyaujanasaṅkhātassa vanathassa padaṭṭhānaṃ samāraupayitabbaṃ, āyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃyaujanuppaj‍janatau  anusayasaṅkhātaṃ idaṃ vanaṃ pariyuṭṭhānasaṅkhātassa vanathassa padaṭṭhānaṃ samāraupayitabbaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cakāmaguṇādīnaṃ vanabhāvau ca taṇhādīnaṃ vanathabhāvau ca kaina amhaihi saddahitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taināha bhagavā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taina pañ‍cakāmaguṇādīnaṃ vanabhāvaina ca taṇhādīnaṃ vanathabhāvaina ca bhagavā ‘‘chaitvā vanañ‍ca vanathañ‍cā’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, taina bhagavatau vacanaina vacanānusāraina saddahitabbauti| ayanti ayaṃ ‘‘aikasmiṃ padaṭṭhānai’’tiādisaṃvaṇṇanā| padaṭṭhānaināti aikaikaina padaṭṭhānaina| samāraupanāti tadañ‍ñapadaṭṭhānānaṃ samāraupanā| samāraupainti samāraupayitabbāni aitāya saṃvaṇṇanāyāti samāraupanāti viggahauti| (1)

51. padaṭṭhānaina samāraupanā ācariyaina niddiṭṭhā, amhaihi ca ñātā, ‘‘katamā vaivacanaina samāraupanā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamā vaivacanainā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti tāsu catūsu padaṭṭhānasamāraupanādīsu samāraupanāsu vaivacanaina aikaikaina rautadañ‍ñavaivacanānaṃ samāraupanā katamāti pucchati| ‘‘rāgavirāgā’’ti ca ‘‘caitauvimuttī’’ti ca ‘‘saikkhaphala’’nti ca idaṃ vacanattayaṃ anāgāmiphalatthattā anāgāmiphalassa vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘avij‍jāvirāgā’’ti ca ‘‘pañ‍ñāvimuttī’’ti ca ‘‘asaikkhaphala’’nti ca idaṃ vacanattayaṃ arahattaphalatthattā arahattaphalassa vaivacanaṃ| iminā nayaina saisaisu yaujanā kātabbā| (2)

vaivacanaina samāraupanā ācariyaina niddiṭṭhā, amhaihi ca ñātā, ‘‘katamā bhāvanāya samāraupanā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamā bhāvanāyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti tāsu catūsu padaṭṭhānasamāraupanādīsu katamāya daisitāya bhāvanāya katamaisānaṃ adaisitānaṃ bhāvanāraupanā katamāti pucchati| yathā yaina pakāraina yaṃ bhāvanaṃ bhagavā ‘‘tasmātiha, tvaṃ bhikkhu, kāyai kāyānupassī viharati, ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.369, 371, 395, 415) āha, tathā taina pakāraina tāya bhāvanāya tadañ‍ñabhāvanāpi samāraupayitabbāti atthau|

‘‘tasmātihā’’tiādipāṭhai  ‘‘kiṃ bhāvanaṃ bhagavā āhā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘ātāpī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘ātāpī’’ti vacanaina vīriyindriyaṃ bhagavā āha| ‘‘sampajānau’’ti vacanaina pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhagavā āha| ‘‘satimā’’ti vacanaina satindriyaṃ āha| ‘‘vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’’nti vacanaina samādhindriyaṃ āha| ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai katamā bhāvanā samāraupayitabbā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘aivaṃ kāyai kāyānupassinau viharatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchantī’’ti vuttaṃ| aivaṃ vuttāya vīriyindriyādibhāvanāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānā samāraupayitabbāti atthau| kaina kāraṇaina bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchantīti pucchati| catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ indriyabhāvaina, bhāvaitabbabhāvaina vā aikalakkhaṇattā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchantīti vissaj‍jaiti| ‘‘taisu samāraupitaisu katamai samāraupayitabbā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘catūsū’’tiādi vuttaṃ| catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu bhāviyamānaisu samāraupayitabbaisu cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchantīti samāraupayitabbāti atthau| saisaisupi aivamaiva samāraupayitabbā|

bhāvanāya  samāraupanā ācariyaina vibhattā, amhaihi ca ñātā, ‘‘katamā pahānaina samāraupanā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti tāsu catūsu padaṭṭhānasamāraupanādīsu katamaina daisitaina pahānaina katamaisaṃ adaisitānaṃ pahānānaṃ katamā samāraupanāti pucchati| ‘‘kāyai kāyānupassī viharantau asubhai ‘subha’nti vipal‍lāsaṃ pajahatī’’ti daisitaina ‘‘subha’’nti vipal‍lāsappahānaina kabaḷīkārāhārapariñ‍ñāya paribandhakilaisakāmupādānappahānādayaupi samāraupayitabbā|

‘‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharantau dukkhai ‘sukha’nti vipal‍lāsaṃ pajahatī’’ti daisitaina ‘‘sukha’’nti vipal‍lāsappahānaina phassāhārapariñ‍ñāya paribandhakilaisabhavupādānappahānādayaupi samāraupayitabbā|

‘‘cittai cittānupassī viharantau anic‍cai ‘nic‍ca’nti vipal‍lāsaṃ pajahatī’’ti daisitaina ‘‘nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsappahānaina viñ‍ñāṇāhārapariñ‍ñāya paribandhakilaisadiṭṭhupādānappahānādayaupi samāraupayitabbā|

‘‘dhammaisu  dhammānupassī viharantau anattani ‘attā’ti vipal‍lāsaṃ pajahatī’’ti daisitaina ‘‘attā’’ti vipal‍lāsappahānaina manausañ‍caitanāhārapariñ‍ñāya? manausañ‍caitanāhārapariñ‍ñāya paribandhakilaisaattavādupādānappahānādayaupi samāraupayitabbāti adhippāyau| (4)

pahānahārau pana lakkhaṇahāravibhaṅgavaṇṇanāyaṃ vuttauyaivāti idha na vadāma|

‘‘suttai daisitaina aikaikaina padaṭṭhānādikaina adaisitānaṃ padaṭṭhānādīnaṃ samāraupanabhāvau kaina amhaihi vijānitabbau saddahitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taināha āyasmā’’tiādimāha| taina tathā samāraupanabhāvaina āyasmā mahākac‍cānau –

‘‘yai dhammā yaṃmūlā, yai caikatthā pakāsitā muninā|

tai samāraupayitabbā, aisa samāraupanau hārau’’ti –

yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, taina vacanaina vacanānusāraina tathā samāraupanabhāvau tumhaihi vijānitabbau saddahitabbauti vuttaṃ hauti|

‘‘kiṃ  pana aittāvatā samāraupanau hārau paripuṇṇau, añ‍ñau niyuttau natthī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau samāraupanau hārau’’ti vuttaṃ| suttai daisitaina nayaina padaṭṭhānādikaina adaisitāni padaṭṭhānāni samāraupayitabbāni bhavanti, taina taina padaṭṭhānādikaina adaisitānaṃ padaṭṭhānādīnaṃ samāraupanau hārau niyuttau niddhāraitvā yuñ‍jitabbauti atthau daṭṭhabbau|

iti samāraupanahāravibhaṅgai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana aṭṭhakathāṭīkānusāraina gambhīratthau vitthāratau vibhajitvā gahaitabbau|

imai yathāvuttā sauḷasa saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisā saṃvaṇṇaitabbatthaisu añ‍ñāṇasaṃsayānaṃ haraṇatau apanayanatau hārā nāmāti|

niṭṭhitā hāravibhaṅgavāravibhāvanā|

1. daisanāhārasampātavibhāvanā

52. yaina  yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina daisanāhāravibhaṅgādihāravibhaṅgaina assādādayau nānāsuttatthā vibhattā, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau daisanāhāravibhaṅgādihāravibhaṅgau paripuṇṇau|

‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhamaṃ, disālaucanatau disā vilaukaitvā|

saṅkhipiya aṅkusaina hi, nayaihi tīhi niddisai sutta’’nti|| (naitti॰ 4 nayasaṅkhaipa) –

gāthā niddaisavārai ācariyaina vuttā, tassā gāthāya niddaisau hāravibhaṅgavārassa ādimhi na vibhattau, ‘‘kuhiñ‍ci vibhattau, hārasampātai vā vibhattau kiṃ, udāhu nayasamuṭṭhānahārai vā vibhattau  ki’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘hārasampātai vibhattau’’ti tumhaihi daṭṭhabbauti viñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ ‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhama’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

atha vā suparikammakatabhūmisadisaisu saṃvaṇṇaitabbaisu nānāsuttappadaisaisu nānāvaṇṇasugandhapupphasadisai sauḷasa hārai saṃvaṇṇanābhāvaina yaujaitvā sauḷasa hārā ācariyaina vibhattā, tathā susikkhitasippācariyasuvicāritajambunadābharaṇasadisaisu saṃvaṇṇaitabbaisu nānāsuttappadaisaisu nānāvidharaṃsijālavividhamaṇiratanasadisai sauḷasa hārai saṃvaṇṇanābhāvaina yaujaitvāva sauḷasa hārā vibhattā, mahāpathaviṃ parivattaitvā pappaṭakaujassa khādāpanaṃ atiduk‍karaṃ viya, nānāvidhaisu saṃvaṇṇaitabbaisu suttappadaisaisu paramatthaujāya sauḷasahi hāraihi atiduk‍karakhādāpanasadisaṃ viñ‍ñāpanaṃ karauntaina ca yaujanikamadhugaṇḍaṃ pīḷaitvā sumadhurasassa pāyāpanaṃ atiduk‍karaṃ viya nānāvidhaisu saṃvaṇṇaitabbaisu suttappadaisaisu paramatthamadhurasassa sauḷasahi hāraihi atiduk‍karaṃ pāyāpanasadisaṃ viñ‍ñāpanaṃ karauntaina ca ācariyaina anaikaisu saṃvaṇṇaitabbasuttappadaisaisu sauḷasa hārai saṃvaṇṇanābhāvaina yaujaitvā sauḷasa hārā vibhattā, amhaihi ca ñātā, ‘‘nānāvidhasuttappadaisaisu tai saṃvaṇṇanābhāvaina yaujaitvā sauḷasa hārā vibhattā kiṃ, udāhu aikasmimpi saṃvaṇṇaitabbasuttappadaisai saṃvaṇṇanābhāvaina yaujaitvā sauḷasa hārā vibhattā ki’’nti vattabbattā aikasmimpi saṃvaṇṇaitabbasuttappadaisai saṃvaṇṇanābhāvaina yaujaitvā sauḷasa hārā vibhattāyaivāti tathā vibhajantau ‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhama’’ntiādikaṃ hārasampātavāraṃ āha|

nanu hārasampātavāraṃ kathaitukāmaina ācariyaina ‘‘tattha katamau daisanāhārasampātau’’tiādivacanaṃ vattabbaṃ, atha kasmā ‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhama’’ntiādi vattabbanti cai? niddaisai vuttaṃ ‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhama’’ntiādikaṃ gāthaṃ hāravibhaṅgavārau nappayaujaiti vippakiṇṇavisayattā ca nayavicārassa ca antaritattā| hārasampātavārau pana taṃ gāthaṃ payaujaiti avikiṇṇavisayattā| tasmā taṃ gāthaṃ pac‍cāmasitvā hārasampātavārai tassā gāthāya niddaisau daṭṭhabbauti viñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ ‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhama’’ntiādi vattabbaṃyaivāti| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana –

aivaṃ suparikammakatāya bhūmiyā nānāvaṇṇāni muttapupphāni pakirantau viya, susikkhitasippācariyavicāritaisu surattasuvaṇṇālaṅkāraisu nānāvidharaṃsijālasamuj‍jalāni vividhāni maṇiratanāni bandhantau viya, mahāpathaviṃ parivattaitvā pappaṭakaujaṃ khādāpaintau viya, yaujanikamadhugaṇḍaṃ pīḷaitvā sumadhurasaṃ pāyaintau viya ca āyasmā mahākac‍cānau nānāsuttappadaisai udāharantau sauḷasa hārai vibhajitvā idāni tai aikasmiṃyaiva suttai yaujaitvā dassaintau hārasampātavāraṃ ārabhi| ārabhantau ca yāyaṃ niddaisavārai –

‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhamaṃ, disālaucanatau disā vilaukaitvā|

saṅkhipiya aṅkusaina hi, nayaihi tīhi niddisai sutta’’nti|| –

gāthā vuttā| yasmā taṃ hāravibhaṅgavārau nappayaujaiti vippakiṇṇavisayattā, nayavicārassa ca antaritattā| anaikaihi suttappadaisaihi hārānaṃ vibhāgadassanamaiva hi hāravibhaṅgavārau| hārasampātavārau pana taṃ payaujaiti aikasmiṃyaiva suttappadaisai  sauḷasa hārai yaujaitvāva tadanantaraṃ nayasamuṭṭhānassa kathitattā| tasmā ‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhama’’nti gāthaṃ pac‍cāmasitvā ‘‘tassā niddaisau kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbau? hārasampātaiti āhā’’ti –

vuttaṃ| gāthātthau niddaisavibhāvanāyaṃ vuttauva| ‘‘sauḷasa…pai॰… sutta’’nti yā gāthā niddaisai ācariyaina vuttā, tassā gāthāya niddaisau kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbau, hāravibhaṅgassa ādimhi ācariyaina na vibhattau, hārasampātai vā pac‍cāmasitvā vibhattauti daṭṭhabbau kiṃ, udāhu nayasamuṭṭhānai vā pac‍cāmasitvā vibhattauti daṭṭhabbau kinti pucchati| hārasampātai pac‍cāmasitvā vibhattauti daṭṭhabbauti vissaj‍janā|

hārasampātai tassā gāthāya niddaisau daṭṭhabbauti ācariyaina vuttau, sau hārasampātau daisanāhārasampātabhaidaina sauḷasavidhau, ‘‘tattha katamau hārasampātau daisanāhārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā imasmiṃ suttai saṃvaṇṇaitabbai saṃvaṇṇanābhāvaina mayā vibhajiyamānau hārasampātabhūtau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau daisanāhārasampātau nāmāti tathā vibhajituṃ ‘‘tattha katamau daisanāhārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti tasmiṃ sauḷasavidhai daisanāhārasampātādikai hārasampātai| katamau hārasampātabhūtau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau daisanāhārasampātau nāmāti pucchati|

‘‘arakkhitaina cittaina, micchādiṭṭhihataina ca|

thinamiddhābhibhūtaina, vasaṃ mārassa gacchatī’’ti|| –

suttai ‘‘arakkhitaina cittaināti kiṃdaisayatī’’ti pucchitvā ‘‘pamādaṃ daisayatī’’tiādisaṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau  daisanāhārasampātau nāmāti vuttaṃ hauti| gāthātthau aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 52) vibhattau| yaujanatthau pana arakkhitaina cittaina arakkhitacittasamaṅgī puggalau mārassa mac‍cunau vasaṃ gacchati| micchādiṭṭhihataina vipal‍lāsaina vipal‍lāsasamaṅgī puggalau mārassa vasaṃ gacchati| thinamiddhābhibhūtaina sasaṅkhārikacittaina kusītacittaina taṃcittasamaṅgī puggalau mārassa kilaisādimārassa vasaṃ gacchatīti|

‘‘arakkhitaina  cittainā’ti padaina daisitaṃ taṃ pamādadhammajātaṃ kassa pada’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘taṃ mac‍cunau pada’’nti vuttaṃ| ‘‘arakkhitaina cittainā’’ti iminā suttappadaisaina daisitau atthau ācariyaina vibhattau, amhaihi ca ñātau, ‘‘micchādiṭṭhihataina cāti suttappadaisaina daisitau atthau kathaṃ vibhattau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘micchādiṭṭhihataina cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yaina vipal‍lāsaina yadā anic‍cai ‘‘nic‍ca’’nti passati, tadā pavattau sau vipal‍lāsau ‘‘micchādiṭṭhihataṃ nāmā’’ti vuc‍cati| ‘‘sau pana vipal‍lāsau kiṃlakkhaṇau’’ti pucchitabbattā tathā pucchitvā ‘‘viparītaggāhalakkhaṇau vipal‍lāsau’’ti vuttaṃ|

viparītaggāhalakkhaṇauti asubhādīnaṃyaiva subhādiviparītaggāhalakkhaṇau vipal‍lāsau ‘‘vipal‍lāsayatī’’ti kāritatthasambhavatau| kiṃ vipal‍lāsau viparītaggāhalakkhaṇau? sau vipal‍lāsau sañ‍ñaṃ vipal‍lāsayati, cittampi vipal‍lāsayati, diṭṭhimpi vipal‍lāsayati| iti tayau dhammai vipal‍lāsayatīti vipal‍lāsaitabbānaṃ tividhattā vipal‍lāsāpi tividhā haunti| taisu sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau mudukau dubbalau subhādivasaina upaṭṭhitākāraggahaṇamattattā, cittavipal‍lāsau sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsatau balavā subhādivasaina upaṭṭhahantānaṃ rūpakkhandhādīnaṃ subhādivasaina san‍niṭṭhānaṃ katvā gahaṇatau| diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsacittavipal‍lāsaihi balavatarau, yaṃ yaṃ ārammaṇaṃ subhādiākāraina upaṭṭhāti| taṃ taṃ ārammaṇaṃ sassatādivasaina abhinivisitvā gahaṇatau| tasmā sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau paṭhamaṃ vuttau, tadanantaraṃ cittavipal‍lāsau, tadanantaraṃ diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau vuttau| vitthāratau pana aikaikassa subhasukhaattanic‍caggahaṇavasaina catubbidhattā dvādasavidhā haunti|

vipal‍lāsā ācariyaina vibhattā, amhaihi ca ñātā, ‘‘katamai vipal‍lāsapavattiṭṭhānavisayā’’ti pucchitabbattā imāni attabhāvavatthūni vipal‍lāsapavattiṭṭhānavisayānīti dassaituṃ ‘‘sau kuhiṃ vipal‍lāsayati catūsu attabhāvavatthūsū’’ti vuttaṃ| catūsu rūpakāyavaidanācittadhammasaṅkhātaisu attabhāvavatthūsu sau sabbau vipal‍lāsau sañ‍ñācittadiṭṭhiyau vipal‍lāsayati| ‘‘kathaṃ samanupassantassa vipal‍lāsayatī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘rūpaṃ attatau samanupassatī’’tiādi  vuttaṃ| yau puggalau rūpaṃ vā attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ vā attatau samanupassati, attani rūpaṃ vā attatau samanupassati| rūpasmiṃ attānaṃ vā attatau samanupassati, aivaṃ tassa samanupassantassa puggalassa vipal‍lāsau rūpakāyai sañ‍ñācittadiṭṭhiyau vipal‍lāsayati| aisaiva nayau vaidanādīsupi|

‘‘taisu rūpakāyādīsu katamaṃ katamassa vipal‍lāsassa vatthū’’ti pucchitabbattā aivaṃ pavattamānassa vipal‍lāsassa idaṃ imassa vatthūti vibhajituṃ ‘‘tattha rūpa’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu rūpādīsu catūsu paṭhamaṃ vipal‍lāsavatthu rūpaṃ ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti aivaṃ pavattamānassa vipal‍lāsassa vatthu hautīti vibhajitvā gahaitabbaṃ| aisa nayau saisaisupi| aivaṃ ‘‘asubhai subha’’ntiādippakāraina vipal‍lāsā catubbidhā bhavanti|

idaṃ imassa vatthūti ācariyaina vibhajitvā dassitā, amhaihi ca ñātā, ‘‘taisaṃ vipal‍lāsānaṃ katamai mūlakāraṇadhammā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘dvai dhammā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| cittassa saṃkilaisā, taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca – imai dvai dhammā vipal‍lāsānaṃ mūlakāraṇabhūtā bhavanti|

‘‘imai dvai dhammā aikatau vipal‍lāsānaṃ mūlakāraṇaṃ kiṃ haunti, udāhu visuṃ visu’’nti vattabbattā visuṃ visuṃ vibhajituṃ ‘‘taṇhānivuta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| avij‍jārahitā taṇhā nāma natthi, tasmā ‘‘taṇhāavij‍jānivuta’’nti vattabbanti? na, taṇhāya sātisayapac‍cayattā| sātisayāya hi taṇhāya asubhaipi ‘‘subha’’nti, dukkhaipi ‘‘sukha’’nti samanupassanti| ‘‘taṇhā ca avij‍jā cā’’ti vuttattā ‘‘avij‍jānivuta’’nti vattabbaṃ, kasmā ‘‘diṭṭhinivuta’’nti vuttanti? avij‍jāya diṭṭhi bhavatīti diṭṭhisīsaina avij‍jaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘diṭṭhinivuta’’nti vuttaṃ, avij‍jānivutanti atthau gahaitabbau| ‘‘avij‍jānivuta’’nti vuttai pana diṭṭhirahitā avij‍jāpi gahitā siyā, diṭṭhisahitāya hi avij‍jāya anic‍caipi ‘‘nic‍ca’’nti, anattaniyaipi ‘‘attā’’ti samanupassanti|

‘‘kathaṃ taṇhāmūlakau vipal‍lāsau pavattau, kathaṃ diṭṭhisahitāvij‍jāmūlakau vipal‍lāsau pavattau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha yau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu taṇhāmūlakadiṭṭhisahitāvij‍jāmūlakaisu| diṭṭhivipal‍lāsauti diṭṭhisahitāvij‍jāmūlakavipal‍lāsau| atītaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassatīti adabbabhūtaupi dabbabhūtau viya vuttau| taṇhāvipal‍lāsauti diṭṭhisahitataṇhāmūlakau vipal‍lāsau anāgataṃ  rūpaṃ diṭṭhibhinandanavasaina abhinandatīti| aivaṃ atītasamanupassanaanāgatābhinandanabhaidaina  pavattivisaisau daṭṭhabbau| ‘‘cittassa saṃkilaisau taṇhāavij‍jāyaiva dvai dhammā na haunti, atha khau dasa kilaisāpi, kasmā dvaiyaiva vuttā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘dvai dhammā cittassa upak‍kilaisā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca – imai dvaiyaiva dhammā paramasāvaj‍jassa vipal‍lāsassa mūlakāraṇattā| tāhi taṇhāavij‍jāhi visujjhantaṃ cittaṃ sabbaihi kilaisaihi visujjhati, tasmā ca visaisatau cittassa upak‍kilaisā hauntīti dvai dhammā vuttā| na hi tāsu taṇhāavij‍jāsu arahattamaggaina pahīnāsu kauci saṃkilaisau appahīnau nāma natthīti|

‘‘vuttappakārā taṇhāavij‍jā vuttappakārānaṃ vipal‍lāsānaṃyaiva mūlakāraṇaṃ haunti kiṃ, udāhu sakalassa vaṭṭassāpi mūlakāraṇaṃ haunti ki’’nti vattabbattā vuttappakārā taṇhāavij‍jā vuttappakārānaṃ mūlakāraṇaṃ haunti yathā, aivaṃ sakalassa vaṭṭassāpi mūlakāraṇaṃ hauntīti dassaituṃ ‘‘taisa’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha yaisaṃ puggalānaṃ cittaṃ arakkhitaṃ, micchādiṭṭhihatañ‍ca hauti, taisaṃ puggalānaṃ| yaisaṃ avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ pubbakauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati, taihi avij‍jānīvaraṇaihi taṇhāsaṃyaujanaihi saṃsārai sandhāvantānaṃ saṃsarantānaṃ puggalānaṃ sakiṃ nirayaṃ māravasagamanaina sandhāvanaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ hauti, sakiṃ tiracchānayauniṃ sandhāvanaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ hauti, sakiṃ paittivisayaṃ sandhāvanaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ hauti, sakiṃ asurakāyaṃ sandhāvanaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ hauti, sakiṃ daivai sandhāvanaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ hauti, sakiṃ manussai sandhāvanaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ hautīti atthau|

‘‘micchādiṭṭhihataina cā’’ti suttappadaisaina daisitau atthau ācariyaina vibhattau, amhaihi ca ñātau, ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhūtaināti suttappadaisaina daisitau atthau kathaṃ vibhattau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhūtainā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| cittassa viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa yā akal‍latā akammaniyatā atthi, idaṃ akal‍lattaṃ akammaniyattaṃ thinaṃ nāma| yaṃ kāyassa līnattaṃ vaidanādikkhandhattayalīnattaṃ atthi, idaṃ kāyassa līnattaṃ middhaṃ nāmāti thinamiddhasarūpamaiva vuttaṃ| taihi thinamiddhaihi cittassa abhibhūtabhāvādikau pana suviñ‍ñaiyyattā na vuttau, avuttaipi yaisaṃ puggalānaṃ cittaṃ  thinamiddhaihi abhibhūtaṃ, taisaṃ puggalānaṃ taina cittaina cittasīsaina saṃyaujanaina saṃsārai māravasagamanaina sandhāvanaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ pariyausānasabhāvau vitthāraitvā gahaitabbau|

‘‘vasaṃ mārassa gacchatīti suttappadaisaina daisitau atthau kathaṃ vibhattau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘vasaṃ mārassa gacchatī’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha kilaisamārassāti kilaisau dānādipuñ‍ñai māraiti nivāraitīti atthaina mārauti kilaisamārau| iminā kilaisamāraṃ nissāya pavattattā abhisaṅkhāramārakhandhamāramac‍cumārā  ca gahitā, ca-saddaina vā gahitāti vaiditabbā| sattamārassāti daivaputtamārassa| atha vā ‘‘daivaputtamārassā’’ti avatvā ‘‘sattamārassā’’ti vuttattā yau yau rājacaurādikau dānādīni vā issariyabhaugādīni vā māraiti, sau sau rājacaurādikaupi gahitau, tasmā yassa kassaci sattamārassāti atthau| vasanti icchaṃ laubhaṃ adhippāyaṃ ruciṃ ākaṅkhaṃ āṇaṃ āṇattiṃ| gacchatīti upagacchati upaiti anuvattati anugacchati nātik‍kamatīti atthau| ‘‘kasmā vasaṃ gacchatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘sau hī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yau sattau arakkhitacittaina ca micchādiṭṭhihatacittaina ca thinamiddhābhibhūtacittaina ca saman‍nāgatau hauti, sau sattau avij‍jānīvaraṇādīhi nivutau hutvā saṃsārābhimukhau hi yasmā hauti, na visaṅkhārābhimukhau, tasmā mārassa vasaṃ gacchatīti atthau|

‘‘arakkhitainātiādikassa yassa suttassa atthau vibhattau, taina ‘arakkhitainā’tiādikaina suttaina kittakāni sac‍cāni daisitānī’’tivattabbattā ‘‘imāni bhagavatā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘arakkhitainā’’tiādisuttaina bhagavatā imāni dvai sac‍cāni daisitāni dukkhaṃ, samudayau cāti| kathaṃ daisitāni? abhidhammanissitāya kathāya caiva suttantanissitāya kathāya ca daisitāni| tāsu kathāsu abhidhammanissitāya kathāya daisitai sati ‘‘arakkhitaina cittainā’’ti iminā padaina arakkhitaṃ rattampi cittaṃ, arakkhitaṃ duṭṭhampi cittaṃ, arakkhitaṃ mūḷhampi cittaṃ bhagavatā daisitaṃ ñāpitaṃ| tattha rattacittaṃ laubhasahagatacittuppādavasaina aṭṭhavidhaṃ, duṭṭhacittaṃ paṭighasampayuttacittuppādavasaina dubbidhaṃ, mūḷhacittaṃ maumūhacittuppādavasaina dubbidhanti vaiditabbaṃ| imaisañhi cittuppādānaṃ vasaina yā cakkhundriyādīnaṃ agutti anārakkhā uppan‍nā, tāya aguttiyā anārakkhāya cittaṃ arakkhitaṃ hauti phalūpacāraināti|

‘‘micchādiṭṭhihatainā’’ti  iminā padaina micchādiṭṭhisaṃsaṭṭhaṃ cittaṃ daisitaṃ, taṃ diṭṭhisampayuttacittuppādavasaina catubbidhanti vaiditabbaṃ| tañhi micchādiṭṭhiyā saṃsaṭṭhabhāvaina micchādiṭṭhivasānugatattā micchādiṭṭhihataṃ nāmāti| ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhūtainā’’ti iminā padaina thinamiddhaina saṃsaṭṭhaṃ cittaṃ daisitaṃ, taṃ sasaṅkhārikacittuppādavasaina pañ‍cavidhanti vaiditabbaṃ| tañhi thinamiddhaina saṃsaṭṭhabhāvaina thinamiddhavasānugatattā thinamiddhābhibhūtaṃ nāmāti aivaṃ yai dvādasākusalā cittuppādakaṇḍai ‘‘katamai dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hautī’’tiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 365) vitthāratau vattabbā, tai dvādasākusalacittuppādā tīhi padaihi bhagavatā daisitāti vaiditabbā| ‘‘mārassā’’ti padaina pañ‍ca mārā gahitā| taisu kilaisamārau  ‘‘cattārau āsavā, cattārau aughā, cattārau yaugā, cattārau ganthā, cattāri upādānāni, aṭṭha nīvaraṇā, dasa kilaisā’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ 1102 ādayau) daisitau| abhisaṅkhāramārau pana ‘‘kusalā caitanā (vibha॰ 226) akusalā caitanā (vibha॰ 226) kusalaṃ kammaṃ akusalaṃ kamma’’ntiādinā daisitau| khandhamārau pana ‘‘attabhāvau pañ‍cakkhandhā’’tiādinā daisitau| mac‍cumārau pana ‘‘cuti cavanatā’’tiādinā (vibha॰ 193) daisitau| aivaṃ tāvaittha abhidhammanissitāya kathāya daisitau atthau daṭṭhabbau|

suttantanissitāya pana kathāya daisitai sati ‘‘cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā nimittaggāhī hauti anubyañ‍janaggāhī, yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya na paṭipaj‍jati, na rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati…pai॰… sautindriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati… ghānindriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati… jivhindriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati… kāyindriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati… manindriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.347) aivaṃ puggalādhiṭṭhānaina yaṃ chadvārikacittaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ chadvārikacittaṃ ‘‘arakkhitaina cittainā’’ti iminā padaina daisitaṃ| yā micchādiṭṭhiyau pubbantakappanavasaina vā aparantakappanavasaina vā pubbantāparantakappanavasaina vā micchā abhinivisantassa ayaunisau ummuj‍jantassa ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti vā ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti vā ‘‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā uppan‍nā, tāhi  diṭṭhīhi vā, yā ca diṭṭhiyau ‘‘imā cattārau sassatavādā…pai॰… paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā’’ti brahmajālasuttādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.30 ādayau) vuttā, tāhi diṭṭhīhi vā sampayuttaṃ yaṃ cittaṃ ‘‘micchādiṭṭhihataina cā’’ti iminā padaina daisitaṃ|

‘‘thinaṃ nāma cittassa akammañ‍ñatā, middhaṃ nāma vaidanādikkhandhattayassa akammañ‍ñatā’’ti vā ‘‘thinaṃ anussāhasaṃsīdanaṃ, middhaṃ ussāhasattivighātau’’ti vā yāni thinamiddhāni vuttāni, taihi thinamiddhaihi yaṃ cittaṃ abhibhūtaṃ ajjhautthaṭaṃ, taṃ cittaṃ ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhūtainā’’ti iminā padaina daisitaṃ|

‘‘vasau nāma icchā laubhau adhippāyau ruci ākaṅkhā āṇā āṇattī’’ti yau vasau vuttau, sau vasau ‘‘vasa’’nti iminā padaina daisitau| ‘‘pañ‍ca mārā – khandhamārau abhisaṅkhāramārau mac‍cumārau daivaputtamārau kilaisamārau’’ti yau mārau vuttau, sau mārau ‘‘mārassā’’ti iminā daisitau| ‘‘gacchati, upagacchati, upaiti, anuvattati, anugacchati, nātik‍kamatī’’ti  yau puggalau vuttau, sau puggalau ‘‘gacchatī’’ti iminā daisitauti| aivaṃ daisitaisu dhammaisu akusalā samudayasac‍caṃ, ‘‘vasaṃ mārassa gacchatī’’ti iminā padaina yai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai upādāya pañ‍ñattau puggalau vuttau, tai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhasac‍canti dvai sac‍cāni daisitāni| ‘‘kimatthāya dvai sac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘taisaṃ bhagavā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taisaṃ dvin‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya ca pahānāya ca ‘‘arakkhitainā’’tiādi dhammaṃ bhagavā daisaiti, tāni dvai sac‍cāni ‘‘arakkhitainā’’tiādikaina ñāpaitīti atthau|

‘‘taisu dvīsu sac‍caisu kassa sac‍cassa pariñ‍ñāya, kassa sac‍cassa pahānāya daisaitī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāya, samudayassa pahānāyā’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘pariñ‍ñāpahānaihi katamāni sac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘yaina cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yaina arahattamaggaina parijānāti, yaina arahattamaggaina pajahati ca, ayaṃ arahattamaggau maggasac‍caṃ nāma| yaṃ nibbānadhammaṃ ārabbha taṇhāya, avij‍jāya ca pahānaṃ jātaṃ, ayaṃ nibbānadhammau niraudhau niraudhasac‍caṃ nāmāti| aivaṃ cattāri sac‍cāni bhagavatā daisitāni|

‘‘catun‍naṃ  sac‍cānaṃ daisitabhāvau kaina viñ‍ñātabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taināhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taisu catūsu sac‍caisu samudayasac‍caina assādau gahitau, dukkhasac‍caina ādīnavau gahitau, maggasac‍caniraudhasac‍caihi nissaraṇaṃ gahitaṃ, samudayappahānavasaina sabbagatipajahanaṃ jātaṃ, sabbagatipajahanaṃ phalanti gahitaṃ| yaina rakkhitacittatādikaina sabbagatipajahanaṃ jātaṃ, sau rakkhitacittatādikau upāyauti gahitau, arakkhitacittatādikassa paṭisaidhanamukhaina rakkhitacittatādikassa niyaujanaṃ bhagavatau āṇattīti gahitanti daisanāhāraina nānāsuttaisu dassitā assādādayau ‘‘arakkhitainā’’tiādikai aikasmiṃyaiva suttai nīharitvā dassitā|

‘‘assādādīnaṃ nīharitvā dassitabhāvau kaina viñ‍ñātabbau saddahitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taināhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aittha ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina nānāsuttaisu assādādayau nīharitvā dassitā, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau daisanāhāravibhaṅgau nāma| yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina aikasmiṃyaiva suttai assādādayau nīharitvā dassitā, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau daisanāhārasampātau nāmāti visaisau daṭṭhabbau|

‘‘aittakauva daisanāhārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau daisanāhārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| aikaikasmiṃyaiva suttai assādādayau yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina daisanāhārasampātaina nīharitvā  yathārahaṃ dassitā, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau daisanāhārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahitau|

iti daisanāhārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

2. vicayahārasampātavibhāvanā

53. yaina  daisanāhārasampātaina assādādayau ācariyaina vibhattā, amhaihi ca ñātā, sau daisanāhārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau vicayahārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau vicayau hārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu daisanāhārasampātādīsu sauḷasasu hārasampātaisu katamau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau vicayahārasampātau nāmāti pucchati| imaisu dhammaisu ayaṃ dhammau yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina vicayitabbau, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau vicayahārasampātau nāmāti niyamaitvā vibhajituṃ ‘‘tattha taṇhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana –

‘‘aivaṃ daisanāhārasampātaṃ dassaitvā idāni vicayahārasampātaṃ dassaintau yasmā daisanāhārapadatthavicayau vicayahārau, tasmā daisanāhārai vipal‍lāsahaitubhāvaina niddhāritāya taṇhāya kusalādivibhāgapavicayamukhaina vicayahārasampātaṃ dassaituṃ ‘tattha taṇhā duvidhā’tiādi āraddha’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 53) –

vuttaṃ| tattha tatthāti tasmiṃ ‘‘arakkhitaina cittainā’’tiādisuttatthai daisanāhārasampātaina saṃvaṇṇitai akusaladhammai ‘‘taṇhā’’ti niddhāritā sabbataṇhā| kusalāpīti catubhūmakai kusalai uddissa pavattā taṇhāpi| akusalāpīti akusaladhammai uddissa pavattā taṇhāpīti duvidhā hautīti vicayitabbā| taina vuttaṃ ṭīkāyaṃ – ‘‘kusaladhammārammaṇāti kusaladhammai uddissa pavattamattaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ, na taisaṃ ārammaṇapac‍cayataṃ, idha ‘kusalā dhammā’ti laukuttaradhammānampi adhippaitattā’’ti|

‘‘kusalā  taṇhā kiṃgāminī, akusalā taṇhā kiṃ gāminī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘akusalā saṃsāragāminī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| atha vā ‘‘katamau kusalākusalataṇhānaṃ visaisau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘akusalā saṃsāragāminī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘taṇhā nāma saṃsāragāminī hautu, kathaṃ apacayagāminī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘pahānataṇhā’’ti vuttaṃ, pahānassa haitubhūtā taṇhā pahānataṇhāti atthau, pahātabbataṇhaṃ āgamma yaṃ pahānaṃ pavattaitabbaṃ, taina pavattaitabbaina pahānaina apacayaṃ gacchatīti vuttaṃ hauti|

‘‘kiṃ  pana taṇhāyaiva kusalākusalāti dubbidhā, udāhu añ‍ñaupi kusalākusalāti dubbidhau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘mānaupī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘katamau mānau kusalau, katamau mānau akusalau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘yaṃ mānaṃ nissāya mānaṃ pajahati, ayaṃ mānau kusalau| yau pana mānau dukkhaṃ nibbattayati, ayaṃ mānau akusalau’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha yaṃ mānaṃ…pai॰… kusalauti yaṃ mānaṃ nissāya upanissāya pahānaṃ pavattitaṃ, taina pahānaina santānai uppaj‍janārahaṃ mānaṃ pajahati, ayaṃ upanissayapac‍cayabhūtau mānau phalūpacāraina kusalau| yau pana…pai॰… akusalauti yau pana mānau parahiṃsanādivasaina pavattamānau hutvā attanau ca parassa ca dukkhaṃ nibbattayati, ayaṃ mānau akusalauti vicayitvā vaiditabbau|

‘‘saṃsārāpacayagāminīsu tāsu taṇhāsu katamā apacayagāminī taṇhā kusalā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘kusalā’’ti vuttāya taṇhāya sarūpaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha yaṃ naikkhammasita’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti tāsu saṃsārāpacayagāminīsu taṇhābhūtāsu kusalākusalāsu| ayaṃ taṇhā kusalāti sambandhau| ‘‘ariyā puggalā santaṃ āyatanaṃ yaṃ ariyaphaladhammaṃ sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti, taṃ āyatanaṃ ariyaphaladhammaṃ ahaṃ kudāssu sacchikatvā viharissa’’nti patthayantassa tassa kulaputtassa tasmiṃ ariyaphalai pihā uppaj‍jati, pihāpac‍cayā yaṃ daumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, idaṃ daumanassaṃ ‘‘naikkhammasita’’nti vuc‍cati| ayaṃ ariyaphalai pihāsaṅkhātā taṇhā kusalā anavaj‍jā anavaj‍jaariyaphaladhammaṃ uddissa pavattattāti vicayitabbaṃ|

‘‘kathaṃ pavattā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘rāgavirāgā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yā rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti pattabbā| tadārammaṇā taṃ caitauvimuttiṃ āgamma pavattā taṇhā kusalā anavaj‍jā, yā avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti pattabbā, tadārammaṇā taṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ āgamma pavattā taṇhā kusalā anavaj‍jāti vicayitabbā| tāya pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā vasaina bhagavatā –

‘‘tasmā  rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau|

sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau, ñatvāna udayabbayaṃ|

thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhu, sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti|| (udā॰ 32; naitti॰ 31, 65, 78) –

gāthāyaṃ  ‘‘sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti padaṃ vuttaṃ, ‘‘tassā pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā yau pavicayau kātabbau, katamau sau pavicayau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tassā kau pavicayau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tassā pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā kau pavicayauti cai pucchaiyya ‘‘aṭṭha maggaṅgāni – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhī’’ti pavicayau vaiditabbau| ‘‘sau pavicayau kattha daṭṭhabbau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sau katthā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| sau pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā pavicayau kattha kasmiṃ dhammai daṭṭhabbauti pucchati| catutthai jhānai pāramitāya uk‍kaṃsagatāya catutthajjhānabhāvanāya sau pavicayau daṭṭhabbau|

‘‘catutthai jhānai pāramitāyā’’ti vuttamatthaṃ vivarituṃ ‘‘catutthai hi jhānai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yau sau catutthajjhānalābhī puggalau catutthai jhānai parisuddhaṃ pariyaudātaṃ anaṅgaṇaṃ vigatūpak‍kilaisaṃ mudu kammaniyaṃ ṭhitaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ, iti aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ cittaṃ bhāvayati, sau catutthajjhānalābhī puggalau tattha catutthai jhānai aṭṭhavidhaṃ vij‍jācaraṇaṃ adhigacchati| katamaṃ aṭṭhavidhaṃ? cha abhiñ‍ñā, dvai ca visaisai vā adhigacchatīti yaujanā| iddhividhādayau pañ‍ca, laukiyābhiñ‍ñā caiva arahattamaggapañ‍ñā cāti cha abhiñ‍ñā| manaumayiddhi caiva vipassanāñāṇañ‍cāti dvai ca visaisā haunti|

‘‘taṃ catutthajjhānacittaṃ kutau parisuddhaṃ…pai॰… kutau ānaiñ‍jappatta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘taṃ cittaṃ yatau parisuddha’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| taṃ cittaṃ yatau upaikkhāsatipārisuddhibhāvatau parisuddhaṃ, tatau upaikkhāsatipārisuddhibhāvatau pariyaudātaṃ hauti| sukhādīnaṃ pac‍cayaghātaina yatau vītarāgādianaṅgaṇabhāvatau anaṅgaṇaṃ, tatau vītarāgādianaṅgaṇabhāvatau vigatūpak‍kilaisaṃ| yatau subhāvitabhāvatau mudu, tatau subhāvitabhāvatau kammaniyaṃ| yatau parisuddhādīsu ṭhitabhāvatau ṭhitaṃ, tatau parisuddhādīsu ṭhitabhāvatau ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ hautītipi yaujanā yuttā aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 53) yugaḷatau āgatattā| saddhāvīriyasatisamādhipañ‍ñāaubhāsaihi pariggahitabhāvatau ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ| saddhāya hi pariggahitaṃ cittaṃ paṭipakkhai assaddhiyai na iñ‍jati na calati, vīriyaina pariggahitaṃ  cittaṃ paṭipakkhai kausaj‍jai na iñ‍jati, satiyā pariggahitaṃ  cittaṃ paṭipakkhai pamādai na iñ‍jati, samādhinā pariggahitaṃ cittaṃ paṭipakkhai uddhac‍cai na iñ‍jati, pañ‍ñāya pariggahitaṃ cittaṃ paṭipakkhāya avij‍jāya na iñ‍jati, aubhāsagataṃ cittaṃ kilaisandhakārai na iñ‍jati| iti imaihi chahi dhammaihi pariggahitaṃ catutthajjhānacittaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ hauti| aivaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatattā catutthajjhānacittaṃ chan‍naṃ abhiñ‍ñāñāṇānañ‍ca manaumayiddhivipassanāñāṇānañ‍ca adhigamūpāyau hauti, tasmā sau pañ‍ñāvimuttiparicayau catutthajjhānai daṭṭhabbauyaivāti saṅkhaipatthau| vitthāratau pana aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 53) ‘‘tattha upaikkhāsatipārisuddhibhāvainā’’tiādinā vā ‘‘aparau nayau’’tiādinā vā vuttauyaivāti amhaihi na vuttau|

‘‘yaisaṃ rāgādiaṅgaṇānaṃ abhāvaina anaṅgaṇaṃ, yaisaṃ abhijjhādiupak‍kilaisānaṃ abhāvaina vigatūpak‍kilaisaṃ, yāya cittassa ṭhitiyā abhāvaina ṭhitaṃ, iñ‍janāya abhāvaina ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ, tai rāgādiaṅgaṇādayau katamāya pakkhā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha aṅgaṇā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu rāgādiaṅgaṇādīsu aṅgaṇā ca rāgādiaṅgaṇā ca upak‍kilaisā abhijjhādiupak‍kilaisā ca santi, tadubhayaṃ rāgādiaṅgaṇaabhijjhādiupak‍kilaisadvayaṃ taṇhāpakkhau rāgādiaṅgaṇānaṃ taṇhāsabhāvattā, abhijjhādiupak‍kilaisānañ‍ca taṇhāya anulaumattā| yā iñ‍janā phandanā yā ca cittassa aṭṭhiti anavaṭṭhānaṃ atthi, ayaṃ iñ‍janā aṭṭhiti diṭṭhipakkhau iñ‍janāya ca aṭṭhitiyā ca micchābhinivaisahaitubhāvatauti pavicayau kātabbau|

‘‘kiṃ pana catutthajjhānacittaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatattāyaiva chan‍naṃ abhiñ‍ñāñāṇānañ‍ca manaumayiddhivipassanāñāṇānañ‍ca adhigamūpāyau hautī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘cattāri indriyānī’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘tassa catutthajjhānalābhinau, dukkhindriyaṃ daumanassindriyaṃ sukhindriyaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ iti cattārindriyāni catutthajjhānai nirujjhanti, tassa catutthajjhānalābhinau upaikkhindriyaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ bhavati, tasmāpi catutthajjhānacittaṃ vuttappakārānaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ ñāṇānaṃ adhigamūpāyau hauti, sau ca adhigamūpāyabhāvau ciṇṇavasībhāvassaiva bhavaiyya, kathaṃ catutthajjhānamattalābhinau ciṇṇavasībhāvau siyā’’ti vattabbattā sau catutthajjhānalābhī catutthajjhānaiyaiva aṭṭhatvā arūpasamāpattiyaupi  aivaṃ katvā nibbattaiti bhāvaiti, tasmā ciṇṇavasībhāvau hautīti dassaituṃ ‘‘sau uparimaṃ samāpatti’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tassatthau aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 53) vitthāratau vuttau, tasmā yaujanamattaṃ karissāma|

sau rūpāvacaracatutthajjhānalābhī yaugāvacarau rūpāvacaracatutthajjhānasamāpattitau uparimaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ  santatau santataratau manasi karauti| yathāvuttaṃ uparimaṃ samāpattiṃ santatau manasi karautau tassa catutthajjhānalābhinau yaugāvacarassa catutthajjhānai sañ‍ñā sañ‍ñāpadhānā samāpatti auḷārikā viya hutvā saṇṭhahati, paṭighasañ‍ñā ca uk‍kaṇṭhā anabhirati hutvā saṇṭhahati, sau yathāvuttaina vidhinā manasi karauntau yaugāvacarau sabbasau niravasaisatau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘‘ākāsaṃ ananta’’nti manasi katvā pavattamānaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| rūpasañ‍ñā rūpāvacarasañ‍ñā pañ‍cavidhaabhiñ‍ñābhinīhārau hauti| nānattasañ‍ñā nānārammaṇaisu vaukārau akusalau pavattati| aivaṃ rūpāvacarajjhānai ādīnavadassī hutvā tā rūpasañ‍ñānānattasañ‍ñāyau ārammaṇai samatik‍kamati, assa yaugāvacarassa paṭighasañ‍ñā ca abbhatthaṃ gacchati| aivaṃ iminā vuttanayaina samatik‍kamaina samāhitassa, santavuttinā arūpāvacarasamādhinā samāhitassa yaugāvacarassa aubhāsau rūpāvacarajjhānaubhāsau antaradhāyati| rūpānaṃ kasiṇarūpānaṃ jhānacakkhunā dassanañ‍ca antaradhāyatīti yaujanā|

‘‘yaina samādhinā samāhitassa, samāhitassa aubhāsau ca rūpānaṃ dassanañ‍ca antaradhāyati, sau samādhi kittakaihi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau, kathaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sau samādhī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| sau samādhīti yaina rūpārūpāvacarasamādhinā samāhitau, sau duvidhaupi samādhi anabhijjhābyāpādavīriyārambhaihi tīhi upakārakaṅgaihi ca passaddhisatīhi dvīhi parikkhāraṅgaihi ca avikkhittaina aikaina sabhāvaṅgaina ca chahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatauti pac‍cavaikkhitabbau punappunaṃ cintaitabbau sal‍lakkhaitabbau| ‘‘kathaṃ kattha pac‍cavaikkhitabbau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘anabhijjhāsahagataṃ mai mānasaṃ sabbalaukai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘taisu chasu aṅgaisu kittakau samathau, kittakā vipassanā’’ti pucchitabbattā aittakau samathau, aittakā vipassanāti vibhajituṃ ‘‘tattha yañ‍cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

54. ‘‘pañ‍ñāvimuttī’’ti vuttassa arahattaphalassa samādhissa samathavipassanāsaṅkhātā pubbabhāgapaṭipadā samādhimukhaina ācariyaina vibhattā, ‘‘tāya paṭipadāya labhitabbau arahattaphalasamādhi kittakaina vaiditabbau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sau samādhi pañ‍cavidhaina vaiditabbau’’ti vuttaṃ| yau arahattaphalasamādhi samathavipassanāpaṭipadāya labhitabbau, sau arahattaphalasamādhi pañ‍cavidhaina ñāṇadassanaina vaiditabbau|

‘‘kathaṃ pañ‍cavidhañāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘ayaṃ samādhī’’tiādi  vuttaṃ| ayaṃ arahattaphalasamādhi appitappitakkhaṇai phalasamāpattisukhattā pac‍cuppan‍nasukhau hauti, iti pac‍cavaikkhantassa assa arahatau pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavati| ayaṃ arahattaphalasamādhi āyatiṃ samāpaj‍jitabbassa arahattaphalasamādhissa upanissayapac‍cayattā āyatiṃ sukhavipākau hauti, iti pac‍cavaikkhantassa…pai॰… bhavati| ayaṃ arahattaphalasamādhi kilaisaarīhi ārakattā ariyau, kāmāmisavaṭṭāmisalaukāmisānaṃ abhāvatau nirāmisau ca hauti, iti pac‍cavaikkhantassa…pai॰… bhavati| ayaṃ arahattaphalasamādhi akāpurisaihi sammāsambuddhapac‍caikabuddhasāvakabuddhaihi saivitabbattā akāpurisasaivitau hauti, iti pac‍cavaikkhantassa…pai॰… bhavati| ayaṃ arahattaphalasamādhi aṅgasantakilaisadarathasantattā santau caiva divasampi samāpaj‍jantassa atittikaraṇatau paṇītau ca paṭippassaddhakilaisaina arahatā puggalaina laddhattā paṭippassaddhiladdhau ca arahattamaggasamādhisaṅkhātaina aikaudibhāvaina adhigatattā aikaudibhāvādhigatau ca sasaṅkhāraina sapayaugaina adhigatattā, nīvaraṇādipac‍canīkadhammai niggayha anadhigatattā, añ‍ñai kilaisai vāraitvā anadhigatattā, arahattamaggaphalabhāvainaiva pavattattā nasasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritagatau hauti, iti pac‍cavaikkhantassa assa arahatau pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavatīti pañ‍cavidhaina ñāṇadassanaina sau arahattaphalasamādhi vicayitvā vaiditabbauti|

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nasukhādīsu  samādhīsu kittakau samathau, kittakā vipassanā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yau ca samādhi pac‍cuppan‍nasukhau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| samathau, vipassanāti ca arahattaphalasamathavipassanāva adhippaitā, na pubbabhāgasamathavipassanāti| arahattaphalasamādhi pañ‍cavidhaina vaiditabbauti ācariyaina vuttau, ‘‘tassa arahattaphalasamādhissa pubbabhāgapaṭipadāyaṃ vuttau samādhi kittakaina vaiditabbau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sau samādhi pañ‍cavidhaina vaiditabbau’’ti vuttaṃ| yau rūpāvacaracatutthajjhānasamādhi pubbabhāgapaṭipadāyaṃ vuttau, sau rūpāvacaracatutthajjhānasamādhi pañ‍cavidhaina pakāraina vaiditabbau| ‘‘katamainā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘pītipharaṇatā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| paṭhamadutiyajjhānaisu pañ‍ñā pītipharaṇatā hauti| paṭhamadutiyatatiyajjhānaisu pañ‍ñā sukhapharaṇatā hauti| catutthajjhānai caitaupariyapañ‍ñā caitaupharaṇatā hauti| dibbacakkhupañ‍ñā ālaukapharaṇatā hauti| jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā pavattamānapañ‍ñā pac‍cavaikkhaṇānimittaṃ hauti| iti pañ‍cavidhaina pañ‍ñāpakāraina vicayitvā vaiditabbauti| ‘‘taisu pañ‍cavidhaisu pakāraisu kittakau samathau, kittakā vipassanā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yau ca pītipharaṇau’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

55. sampayaugavasaina samādhi ācariyaina vibhattau, amhaihi ca ñātau, ‘‘kathaṃ ārammaṇavasaina  vibhattau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘dasa kasiṇāyatanānī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| imaihi dasahi ārammaṇaihi kasiṇaihipi samādhi vicinitvā vaiditabbauti| ‘‘taisu dasasu kittakau samathau, kittakā vipassanā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yañ‍cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| kasiṇanti ca kasiṇamaṇḍalampi parikammampi paṭibhāganimittampi tasmiṃ paṭibhāganimittai uppan‍najjhānampi vuc‍cati, idha pana sasampayuttajjhānamaiva adhippaitaṃ|

‘‘kiṃ pana vuttappakārau samādhiyaiva samathavipassanāya yaujaitabbau, udāhu añ‍ñaupi yaujaitabbau’’ti vattabbattā satipaṭṭhānādipubbabhāgapaṭipadābhaidaina anaikabhaidabhin‍nau niravasaisau ariyamaggaupi vicayitvā yaujaitabbauti dassaituṃ ‘‘aivaṃ sabbau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha aivanti mayā vuttanayaina vuttanayānusāraina sabbau niravasaisau satipaṭṭhānādipubbabhāgapaṭipadābhaidaina anaikabhaidabhin‍nau ariyau maggau yaujaitabbau| kathaṃ? yaina yaina anabhijjhādiākāraina pac‍cuppan‍nasukhatādiākāraina  samādhi mayā vuttau, taina taina anabhijjhādiākāraina pac‍cuppan‍nasukhatādiākāraina yau yau ariyamaggau samathaina yaujaituṃ sambhavati, sau sau ariyamaggau samathaina vicayitvā yaujayitabbau| yau yau ariyamaggau vipassanāya yaujaituṃ sambhavati, sau sau ariyamaggau vipassanāya yaujayitvā yaujayitabbauti atthau gahaitabbau|

‘‘yaihi samathādhiṭṭhānaihi vipassanādhammaihi yaujayitabbau, tai samathādhiṭṭhānā vipassanādhammā katamaihi dhammaihi saṅgahitā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tai tīhi dhammaihi saṅgahitā anic‍catāya dukkhatāya anattatāyā’’ti vuttaṃ, tai samathādhiṭṭhānā vipassanādhammā ‘‘anic‍catāya pañ‍ñāya dukkhatāya pañ‍ñāya anattatāya pañ‍ñāyā’’ti tīhi anupassanādhammaihi saṅgahitā gaṇhitāti atthau| anic‍catādinā sahacaraṇatau anupassanāpañ‍ñāpi ‘‘anic‍catā dukkhatā anattatā’’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘yau yaugī puggalau samathādhiṭṭhānaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvayamānau hauti, sau yaugī puggalau kiṃ bhāvayatī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sau samathavipassanaṃ bhāvayamānau’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

samathavipassanādīni bhāvayamānau puggalau rāgacaritau dausacaritau mauhacaritauti tividhau, ‘‘tattha katamau puggalau katamaina katamaina vimaukkhamukhaina niyyāti, katamāyaṃ katamāyaṃ sikkhantau, katamaṃ katamaṃ pajahantau, katamaṃ katamaṃ anupagacchantau, katamaṃ katamaṃ parijānantau, katamaṃ katamaṃ pavāhaintau, katamaṃ katamaṃ niddhunantau, katamaṃ katamaṃ vamaintau, katamaṃ katamaṃ nibbāpaintau, katamaṃ katamaṃ uppāṭaintau, katamaṃ katamaṃ vijaṭaintau niyyātī’’ti pucchitabbattā  ‘‘rāgacaritau puggalau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘idāni yaisaṃ puggalānaṃ yattha sikkhantānaṃ visaisatau niyyānamukhāni, yaisañ‍ca kilaisānaṃ paṭipakkhabhūtāni tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni, taihi saddhiṃ tāni dassaituṃ ‘rāgacaritau’tiādi vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| tassatthaupi aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 55) vuttauyaiva|

‘‘kasmā  tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni bhāvayantau tayau khandhai bhāvayatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha suñ‍ñatavimaukkhamukha’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu tīsu vimaukkhamukhaisu| suñ‍ñatavimaukkhamukhaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhau anattānupassanāya pañ‍ñāpadhānattā| animittavimaukkhamukhaṃ samādhikkhandhau anic‍cānupassanāya samādhipadhānattā| appaṇihitavimaukkhamukhaṃ sīlakkhandhau dukkhānupassanāya sīlapadhānattā| iti tīhi vimaukkhamukhaihi tiṇṇaṃ khandhānaṃ saṅgahitattā tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni bhāvayantau sau yaugī puggalau tayau khandhai bhāvayatiyaivāti pañ‍ñāpadhānādibhāvau aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 55) vuttau| tayau khandhai bhāvayantau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayati| ‘‘kasmā bhāvayatī’’ti vattabbattā kāraṇaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha yā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

tiṇṇaṃ khandhānaṃ ariyaaṭṭhaṅgikamaggabhāvau vibhattau, amhaihi ca jānitau, ‘‘kathaṃ samathavipassanābhāvau jānitabbau’’ti vattabbattā tiṇṇaṃ khandhānaṃ samathavipassanābhāvaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha sīlakkhandhau cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘yau yaugī puggalau samathavipassanaṃ bhāvaiti, tassa yauginau puggalassa bhavaṅgāni katamaṃ bhāvanaṃ gacchantī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘yau samathavipassanaṃ bhāvaiti, tassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| kāyau ca cittañ‍ca dvai bhavaṅgāni upapattibhavassa aṅgāni bhāvanaṃ vaḍḍhanaṃ gacchanti| sīlañ‍ca samādhi ca dvai padāni dvai pādā bhavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā bhāvanaṃ vaḍḍhanaṃ gacchanti|

‘‘kathaṃ gacchantī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘sau hauti bhikkhū’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhāvitakāyauti bhāvitau kāyau kāyaābhisamācārikau, kāyasaṃvarau vā yaina bhikkhunāti bhāvitakāyau| saisaisupi aisa nayau| kāyai kāyaābhisamācārikai, kāyasaṃvarai vā bhāviyamānai sati sammākammantau, sammāvāyāmau ca dvai dhammā bhāvanaṃ gacchanti kāyasamācārasīlattā| sīlai vācāsaṃvaraājīvasaṃvaravasaina pavattai sīlai bhāviyamānai sati sammāvācā ca sammāājīvau ca dvai dhammā bhāvanaṃ gacchanti vācādisaṃvarasīlattā| cittai cittasaṃvaravasaina pavattai cittai bhāviyamānai sati sammāsati ca sammāsamādhi ca dvai dhammā bhāvanaṃ gacchanti cittasaṃvarasīlattā| pañ‍ñāya bhāviyamānāya sati  sammādiṭṭhi ca sammāsaṅkappau  ca dvai dhammā bhāvanaṃ gacchanti samānattā, upakārakattā ca| sammāsaṅkappaina hi punappunaṃ saṅkappantassa pañ‍ñā vaḍḍhatīti|

‘‘sammākammantau ca sammāvāyāmau ca dvai dhammā kāyavasainaiva vibhattā vicaitabbā kiṃ, udāhu cittavasaina vibhattā vicaitabbā ki’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yau ca sammākammantau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| kāyasucaritacaitanābhūtau yau ca sammākammantau, taṃsahitau yau ca sammāvāyāmau siyā kāyikau, viratibhūtau yau ca sammākammantau, taṃsahitau yau ca sammāvāyāmau siyā caitasikau, tattha taisu kāyikacaitasikabhūtaisu sammākammantasammāvāyāmaisu yau sammākammantasammāvāyāmau kāyasaṅgahau, sau sammākammantasammāvāyāmau kāyai kāyaābhisamācārikai, kāyasaṃvarai vā bhāvitai sati bhāvanaṃ gacchati| yau sammākammantasammāvāyāmau cittasaṅgahau, sau sammākammantasammāvāyāmau cittai cittasaṃvarai bhāvitai sati bhāvanaṃ gacchatīti yaujanā|

‘‘samathavipassanaṃ bhāvayantau sau yaugī puggalau kittakaṃ adhigamaṃ gacchatī’’ti vicayitabbattā ‘‘sau samathavipassanaṃ bhāvayantau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| pañ‍cavidhaṃ ariyamaggādhigamaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘khippādhigamau cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tassatthau aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 55) vibhattau| ‘‘kaina katamau adhigamau hautī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha samathainā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

56. ‘‘arakkhitaina cittainā’’tiādisuttatthau vainaiyyānaṃ arahattaphalavimuttimukhaina ācariyaina vicayitau vibhattau, amhaihi ca ñātau, ‘‘daisakassa dasabalasaman‍nāgatassa dasa balāni kathaṃ vicayitabbānī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yau daisayati, sau dasabalasaman‍nāgatau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana –

‘‘iti mahāthairau ‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’ti gāthāya vasaina arahattaphalavimuttimukhaina vicayahārasampātaṃ niddisantau, daisanākusalatāya anaikaihi suttappadaisaihi tassā pubbabhāgapaṭipadāya bhāvanāvisaisānaṃ bhāvanānisaṃsānañ‍ca vibhajanavasaina nānappakāratau vicayahāraṃ dassaitvā, idāni dasan‍naṃ  tathāgatabalānampi vasaina taṃ dassaituṃ ‘tattha yau daisayatī’tiādimāhā’’ti –

vuttaṃ| tattha tatthāti taisu samathavipassanaṃ bhāvayantaisu sāsitabbasāsakaisu| dasabalasaman‍nāgatau yau  daisakau satthā ‘‘arakkhitaina cittainā’’tiādidhammaṃ daisaiti, auvādaina sāvakai na visaṃvādayati, tassa daisakassa satthunau dasa balāni vicayitabbānīti yaujanā|

‘‘kinti daisaitī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sau tividha’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| taisu dasasu tathāgatabalaisu ṭhānāṭṭhānañāṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ nāma, ‘‘taṃ balaṃ kathaṃ vicayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sau tathā auvaditau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| atthau aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 56) vuttau, pāḷivasainapi pākaṭau| ‘‘aitaṃ ṭhānaṃ na vij‍jatī’’ti jānanaṃ aṭṭhānañāṇaṃ nāma, ‘‘aitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti jānanaṃ ṭhānañāṇaṃ nāmāti ṭhānāṭṭhānānaṃ jānanañāṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti adhippāyau vaiditabbau| (1)

57. ṭhānāṭṭhānañāṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ ācariyaina vicayitaṃ vibhattaṃ amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘kathaṃ sabbatthagāminipaṭipadāñāṇaṃ dutiyatathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘iti ṭhānāṭṭhānatā’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘ayaṃ paṭipadā imasmiṃ bhavai gāminī, ayaṃ paṭipadā imasmiṃ bhavai gāminī’’ti sabbattha gāminiyā paṭipadāya jānanañāṇaṃ sabbatthagāminipaṭipadāñāṇaṃ nāmāti sabbatthagāminipaṭipadāñāṇaṃ dutiyaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti adhippāyau| (2)

59. sabbatthagāminipaṭipadāñāṇaṃ dutiyaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ ācariyaina vicayitaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘kathaṃ anaikadhātunānādhātuñāṇaṃ tatiyaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘iti sabbatthagāminī paṭipadā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘ayaṃ dhātu ca ayaṃ dhātu ca anaikadhātu nāma, ayaṃ dhātu ca ayaṃ dhātu ca nānādhātu nāmā’’ti anaikadhātunānādhātūnaṃ jānanañāṇaṃ anaikadhātunānādhātuñāṇaṃ nāmāti anaikadhātunānādhātuñāṇaṃ tatiyaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti| (3)

60. anaikadhātunānādhātuñāṇaṃ  tatiyaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ ācariyaina vicayitaṃ vibhattaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘kathaṃ sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikatāñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘iti anaikadhātunānādhātukassa laukassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘imai sattā aivaṃ adhimuttā, imai sattā aivaṃ adhimuttā’’ti sattānaṃ adhimuc‍canānaṃ jānanañāṇaṃ sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikatāñāṇaṃ nāmāti sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikatāñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti| (4)

sattānaṃ  nānādhimuttikatāñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ ācariyaina vibhattaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘kathaṃ vipākavaimattatāñāṇaṃ pañ‍camaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘iti tai yathādhimuttā cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ adhimuttānaṃ sattānaṃ idaṃ kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ, imassa kaṇhakammassa ayaṃ vipākau| idaṃ kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ, imassa suk‍kakammassa ayaṃ vipākau’’ti aivamādīhi vipākānaṃ nānattajānanañāṇaṃ vipākavaimattatāñāṇaṃ nāmāti vipākavaimattatāñāṇaṃ pañ‍camaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti|

62. vipākavaimattatāñāṇaṃ pañ‍camaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ ācariyaina vicayitaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘kathaṃ jhānānaṃ saṃkilaisavaudānavuṭṭhānañāṇaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘iti tathā samādin‍nāna’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ samādin‍nānaṃ kammānaṃ jhānānaṃ vimaukkhānaṃ samādhīnaṃ samāpattīnaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau, idaṃ vaudānaṃ, idaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ, aivaṃ saṃkilissati, aivaṃ vaudāyati, aivaṃ vuṭṭhahatī’’ti jhānānaṃ saṃkilaisavaudānavuṭṭhānānaṃ anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ jhānānaṃ saṃkilaisavaudānavuṭṭhānañāṇaṃ nāmāti jhānānaṃ saṃkilaisavaudānavuṭṭhānañāṇaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti| (5)

63. jhānānaṃ saṃkilaisañāṇaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ ācariyaina vicayitaṃ, ‘‘kathaṃ indriyaparaupariyattavaimattatāñāṇaṃ sattamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘iti tassaiva samādhissā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina indriyāni, aivaṃ akampiyaṭṭhaina balānī’’ti jānanaina saha ‘‘ayaṃ mudindriyau, ayaṃ majjhindriyau, ayaṃ tikkhindriyau’’ti parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyabalānaṃ amudumajjhādhimattatājānanañāṇaṃamudumajjhādhimattatājānanañāṇaṃ indriyaparaupariyattavaimattatāñāṇaṃ nāmāti indriyaparaupariyattavaimattatāñāṇaṃ sattamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti| (6)

64. indriyaparaupariyattañāṇaṃ  sattamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ ācariyaina vicayitaṃ, ‘‘kathaṃ pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbaṃ, kathaṃ dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ navamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘iti tattha yaṃ anaikavihita’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘aikaṃ jāti’’ntiādinā jātivasaina vā ‘‘aivaṃnāmau’’tiādinā nāmagauttavaṇṇāhārasukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvaidanāya pariyantavasaina vā sākārassa sauddaisassa anaikavihitapubbainivāsassa taṃtaṃbhavassa asaisatau jānanañāṇaṃ pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ nāmāti pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti| (7)

cavamānaupapaj‍jamānahīnapaṇītasuvaṇṇadubbaṇṇasugataduggatayathākammūpagānaṃ  sattānaṃ asaisatau cutūpapātānaṃ jānanañāṇaṃ dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ nāmāti dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ navamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti| (8-9)

pubbainivāsādiaṭṭhamanavamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ ācariyaina vicayitaṃ vibhattaṃ, ‘‘kathaṃ sabbāsavakkhayañāṇaṃ dasamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘iti tattha ya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| baudhimūlai saṃkilaisamāranihanaṃ ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ, idaṃ kilaisamāranihanaṃ ñāṇaṃ sabbāsavakkhayañāṇaṃ nāmāti sabbāsavakkhayañāṇaṃ dasamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ vicayitabbanti ayaṃ saṅkhaipatthau| vitthāratau pana pāḷitau ca aṭṭhakathātau ca yatipautānampi pākaṭau bhavaiyyāti mañ‍ñitvā na dassitau|(10)

‘‘aittakauva vicayahārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau vicayau hārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yai yai suttappadaisatthā vuttā, tai tai suttappadaisatthā yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina vicayahārasampātaina vicayitabbā, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau vicayahārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ nīharitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbauti|

iti vicayahārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

3. yuttihārasampātavibhāvanā

65. yaina  yaina vicayahārasampātaina suttappadaisatthā ācariyaina vicayitabbā, amhaihi ca ñātā, sau vicayahārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau yuttihārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau yuttihārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana – ‘‘aivaṃ nānānayaihi vicayahārasampātaṃ vitthāraitvā idāni yuttihārasampātādīni dassaituṃ ‘tattha katamau yuttihārasampātau’tiādi āraddha’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 65) vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu daisanāhārasampātādīsu  sauḷasasu hārasampātaisu katamau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau yuttihārasampātau nāmāti pucchati, pucchitvā yasmiṃ suttappadaisai vuttānaṃ atthānaṃ yuttibhāvau vicāraitabbau, taṃ suttappadaisaṃ nīharituṃ –

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau|

sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau, ñatvāna udayabbayaṃ|

thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhu, sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti|| (udā॰ 32; naitti॰ 31, 78) –

vuttaṃ| tassaṃ gāthāyaṃ tasmā arakkhitacittassa māravasānugatattā satisaṃvaraindriyasaṃvarādivasaina bhikkhu rakkhitacittau assa bhavaiyya, tasmā kāmavitak‍kādimicchāsaṅkappagaucarassa māravasānugatattā naikkhammasaṅkappādivasaina bhikkhu sammāsaṅkappagaucarau assa bhavaiyya, tasmā micchādiṭṭhihatacittassa māravasānugatattā yaunisaumanasikāraina bhikkhu udayabbayaṃ ñatvāna sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau assa bhavaiyya, tasmā thinamiddhaina hatacittassa māravasānugatattā vīriyavasaina bhikkhu thinamiddhābhibhū assa bhavaiyya, tādisau bhikkhu sabbā duggatiyau jahai jahissatīti atthau vaiditabbau|

‘‘tassaṃ gāthāyaṃ kathaṃ yuttibhāvau vicāraitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| rakkhitacittassa sammāsaṅkappagaucarau bhavissatīti atthau yuj‍jati aiva, nau na yuj‍jati| micchāsaṅkappānampi jahitattā sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau hutvā viharantau udayabbayaṃ paṭivijjhissatīti atthau yuj‍jati sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārassa udayabbayānupassanāsambhavatau| udayabbayaṃ paṭivijjhantau sabbā duggatiyau jahissatīti atthau  yuj‍jati udayabbayānupassanānuk‍kamaina ariyamaggasambhavatau| sabbā duggatiyau jahantau sabbāni duggativinipātabhayāni samatik‍kamissatīti atthau yuj‍jati sabbaisaṃ duggativinipātabhayānaṃ anuppaj‍janatau|

‘‘aittakauva yuttihārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau yuttihārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina yuttihārasampātaina suttappadaisatthānaṃ yuttibhāvau vicāraitabbau, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau yuttihārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbauti|

iti yuttihārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

4. padaṭṭhānahārasampātavibhāvanā

66. yaina yaina yuttihārasampātaina suttappadaisatthānaṃ yuttibhāvau ācariyaina vibhāvitau, amhaihi ca ñātau, sau yuttihārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau padaṭṭhānahārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau padaṭṭhānau hārasampātau’’ti pucchati|

pucchitvā yasmiṃ suttappadaisai vuttāni padaṭṭhānāni nīharitāni, taṃ suttappadaisaṃ nīharituṃ ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarauti gāthā’’ti vuttā| gāthātthau vuttauva| ‘‘katamai gāthātthā katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ padaṭṭhānānī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’ti suttappadaisassa atthabhūtā indriyaisu guttadvāratā tiṇṇaṃ sucaritānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ nāma sucaritapāripūriyā āsan‍nakāraṇattā| ‘‘sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti suttappadaisassa atthabhūtā naikkhammasaṅkappādayau sammāsaṅkappā samathassa padaṭṭhānaṃ nāma kāmacchandaādinīvaraṇavikkhambhanassa āsan‍nakāraṇattā| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti suttappadaisassa atthabhūtā kammassakatāsammādiṭṭhi ca sappac‍cayanāmarūpadassanasammādiṭṭhi  ca vipassanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ nāma anic‍cānupassanādīnaṃ visaisakāraṇattā| ‘‘ñatvāna udayabbaya’’nti suttappadaisassa atthabhūtā udayabbayānupassanāpañ‍ñā dassanabhūmiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ nāma paṭhamamaggādhigamassa āsan‍nakāraṇattā| ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhū’’ti suttappadaisassa atthabhūtaṃ thinamiddhābhibhavanaṃ vīriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ nāma āsan‍nakāraṇattā| ‘‘sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti suttappadaisassa atthabhūtā pahātabbajahanabhāvanāya ariyamaggabhāvanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ nāma pahātabbappahānaina ariyamaggabhāvanāpāripūrisambhavatau|

‘‘aittakauva  padaṭṭhānahārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau padaṭṭhānau hārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina padaṭṭhānahārasampātabhūtaina suttappadaisatthāni padaṭṭhānāni nīharitāni, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau padaṭṭhānahārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbauti|

iti padaṭṭhānahārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

5. lakkhaṇahārasampātavibhāvanā

67. yaina yaina padaṭṭhānahārasampātaina suttappadaisatthāni padaṭṭhānāni ācariyaina niddhāritāni, amhaihi ca ñātāni, sau padaṭṭhānahārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau lakkhaṇahārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau lakkhaṇau hārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha taisu daisanāhārasampātādīsu sauḷasasu hārasampātaisu katamau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau lakkhaṇahārasampātau nāmāti pucchati|

‘‘katamaihi suttatthaihi samānalakkhaṇā katamai dhammā gahitā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tasmā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti suttappadaisaina vuttaṃ idaṃ rakkhaṇaṃ satindriyaṃ gahitaṃ, satindriyai  gahitai saddhādipañ‍cindriyāni gahitāni bhavanti indriyaṭṭhaina samānalakkhaṇattā| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti suttappadaisaina vuttā sammādiṭṭhi gahitā, sammādiṭṭhiyā gahitāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau gahitau bhavati| taṃ kissa haitūti kāraṇaṃ pucchati| pucchitvā kāraṇamāha ‘‘sammādiṭṭhitau hī’’tiādinā| sammādiṭṭhihaitutau sammāsaṅkappau hi yasmā pabhavati, tasmā, sammāsaṅkappatau sammāvācā hi yasmā pabhavati, tasmā, sammāvācātau sammākammantau hi yasmā pabhavati, tasmā, sammākammantatau sammāājīvau hi yasmā pabhavati, tasmā, sammāājīvatau sammāvāyāmau hi yasmā pabhavati, tasmā, sammāvāyāmatau sammāsatihi yasmā pabhavati, tasmā, sammāsatitau  sammāsamādhi hi yasmā pabhavati, tasmā, sammāsamādhitau sammāvimutti hi yasmā pabhavati, tasmā, sammāvimuttitau sammāvimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ hi yasmā pabhavati, tasmā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau gahitau bhavatīti|

‘‘aittakauva lakkhaṇahārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau lakkhaṇau hārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina lakkhaṇahārasampātaina suttappadaisatthā samānalakkhaṇaina gahitā bhavanti, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau lakkhaṇahārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahitauti|

iti lakkhaṇahārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

6. catubyūhahārasampātavibhāvanā

68. yaina yaina lakkhaṇahārasampātaina suttappadaisatthā samānalakkhaṇaina gahitā, sau lakkhaṇahārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau catubyūhahārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau catubyūhau hārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha taisu daisanāhārasampātādīsu sauḷasasu hārasampātaisu katamau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau catubyūhahārasampātau nāmāti pucchati|

‘‘katamasmiṃ  suttai katamai nirutyādhippāyanidānapubbāparānusandhayau niddhāritā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tasmā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’ti suttappadaisai ‘‘rakkhīyatai rakkhita’’nti niruttiṃ ‘‘paripālīyatī’’ti iminā pariyāyaina dassaiti, itisaddassa ādyatthattā ‘‘cintaitīti cittaṃ, attanau santānaṃ cinautīti cittaṃ, pac‍cayaihi citanti cittaṃ, cittavicittaṭṭhaina cittaṃ, cittakaraṇaṭṭhaina cittaṃ, rakkhitaṃ cittaṃ yassāti rakkhitacittau| sammā saṅkappaitīti sammāsaṅkappau, gāvau caranti aitthāti gaucarau, gaucarau viyāti gaucarau, sammāsaṅkappau gaucarau assāti sammāsaṅkappagaucarau| sammā passatīti sammādiṭṭhi, sammādiṭṭhi puraikkhārau  assāti sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau| jānātīti ñatvāna| udayau ca vayau ca udayabbayaṃ| thinañ‍ca middhañ‍ca thinamiddhaṃ, abhibhavatīti abhibhū, thinamiddhaṃ abhibhūti thinamiddhābhibhū| bhikkhatīti bhikkhū’’ti niruttipi nīharitā| taināha – ‘‘iti-saddau ādyatthau’’ti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 68)| aisā vuttappakārā pañ‍ñatti nirutti nāmāti nīharitā|

idha suttappadaisai bhagavatau kau adhippāyauti cai pucchaiyya, yai sappurisā sabbāhi duggatīhi parimuc‍citukāmā bhavissanti, tai sappurisā dhammacārinau rakkhitacittā bhavissantīti ayaṃ adhippāyau| aittha ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’tiādisuttappadaisai bhagavatau adhippāyauti nīharitabbau|

‘‘katamaṃ nidāna’’nti cai pucchaiyya, kaukālikau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu thairaisu cittaṃ arakkhitvā padausayitvā mahāpadumanirayai yasmā upapan‍nau, yasmā bhagavā ca satiārakkhaina saman‍nāgatau sabbā duggatiyau jahati, tasmā ca sabbā duggatiyau jahitukāmau bhikkhu sappurisau rakkhitacittau assa bhavaiyyāti nidānaṃ nīharitabbaṃ|

‘‘katamau pubbāparasandhī’’ti cai pucchaiyya, suttamhi ‘‘satiyā cittaṃ rakkhitabba’’nti yaṃ vacanaṃ vuttaṃ, taina pubbavacanaina ayaṃ ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa…pai॰… sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti suttappadaisau anusandhi saṃsandati samaitīti pubbaparānusandhi niddhāritabbauti|

‘‘aittakauva  catubyūhahārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau catubyūhau hārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina catubyūhahārasampātaina nirutyādhippāyanidānapubbāparānusandhi niddhāritabbau, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau catubyūhahārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbauti|

iti catubyūhahārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

7. āvaṭṭahārasampātavibhāvanā

69. yaina  yaina catubyūhahārasampātaina nirutyādhippāyanidānapubbāparānusandhayau vibhattā, sau catubyūhahārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau āvaṭṭahārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau āvaṭṭau hārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha katamau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau āvaṭṭahārasampātau nāmāti pucchati|

‘‘katamai suttatthā kathaṃ āvaṭṭaitabbā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tasmā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| naikkhammasaṅkappasaṅkhātasammāsaṅkappabahulau kasiṇādivasaina, avihiṃsāsaṅkappasaṅkhātasammāsaṅkappabahulau maittādivasaina adhigatāya cittaikaggatāya cittaṃ ṭhapaintau saṃkilaisatau rakkhitacittau nāma hauti, ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti iminā rakkhitacittai vuttai sati yā aikaggatā āvaṭṭaitabbā, sā ayaṃ aikaggatā samathau| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti iminā sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārai vuttai sati yā pañ‍ñā āvaṭṭaitabbā, sā ayaṃ pañ‍ñā vipassanā| ‘‘ñatvāna udayabbaya’’nti iminā udayabbayañāṇasaman‍nāgatai vuttai sati yā dukkhaparijānanā āvaṭṭaitabbā, sā ayaṃ dukkhaparijānanā dukkhapariñ‍ñā| ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhū’’ti iminā puggalādhiṭṭhānaina yaṃ thinamiddhābhibhavanaṃ vuttaṃ, idaṃ thinamiddhābhibhavanaṃ samudayappahānaṃ| ‘‘sammā duggatiyau jahai’’ti iminā yau sabbaduggatijahanasaṅkhātau anuppādau vuttau, sau ayaṃ anuppādau  niraudhau| iti dukkhapariñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñaitabbaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ āvaṭṭaitabbaṃ, samudayappahānaina pahātabbaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ āvaṭṭaitabbaṃ, niraudhaina niraudhasac‍caṃ āvaṭṭaitabbaṃ, samathavipassanāhi maggasac‍caṃ āvaṭṭaitabbanti imāni cattāri sac‍cāni āvaṭṭaitabbānīti|

‘‘aittakauva āvaṭṭau hārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau āvaṭṭau hārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina āvaṭṭahārasampātaina samathādayau āvaṭṭaitabbā, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau āvaṭṭahārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbauti|

iti āvaṭṭahārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

8. vibhattihārasampātavibhāvanā

70. yaina yaina āvaṭṭahārasampātaina suttatthā āvaṭṭaitabbā, sau āvaṭṭahārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau vibhattihārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau vibhattihārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha katamau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau vibhattihārasampātau nāmāti pucchati|

‘‘katamai suttatthā kattha vibhattā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yā ‘‘tasmā…pai॰… gaucarau’’ti gāthā vuttā, tissaṃ gāthāyaṃ vuttau kusalapakkhau dhammau kusalapakkhaina dhammaina satisaṃvarau dhammau niddisitabbau vibhajitabbau, akusalapakkhaina dhammaina niddisitabbau vibhajitabbau|

kathaṃ? ‘‘rakkhitacittassā’’ti padaina vuttau kusalapakkhau satisaṃvarau dhammau ‘‘cakkhudvārasaṃvarau…pai॰… manaudvārasaṃvarau’’ti chabbidhaina kusalapakkhaina dhammaina niddisitabbau vibhajitabbau| ‘‘sammāsaṅkappau’’ti padaina vuttau kusalapakkhau sammāsaṅkappau dhammau ‘‘naikkhammasaṅkappau abyāpāda-saṅkappau avihiṃsāsaṅkappau’’ti tividhaina kusalapakkhaina dhammaina  vibhajitabbau| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti padaina vuttā kusalapakkhā dhammajāti ‘‘dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminipaṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ, pubbantai ñāṇaṃ, aparantai ñāṇaṃ, pubbantāparantai ñāṇaṃ, idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu ñāṇa’’nti (dha॰ sa॰ 1063) aṭṭhavidhaina kusalapakkhaina dhammaina vibhajitabbā| ‘‘ñatvāna udayabbaya’’nti padaina vuttaṃ kusalapakkhaudayabbayañāṇaṃ dhammajātaṃ pañ‍ñāsavidhaina udayabbayañāṇaina kusalapakkhaina vibhajitabbaṃ| ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhū’’ti padaina vuttaṃ thinamiddhābhibhavanaṃ kusalapakkhaṃ dhammajātaṃ ‘‘sautāpattimaggābhibhavanaṃ  sakadāgāmimaggābhibhavanaṃ anāgāmimaggābhibhavanaṃ arahattamaggābhibhavana’’nti catubbidhaina kusalapakkhaina vibhajitabbaṃ|
