satisaṃvarau kusalapakkhau ‘‘laukiyau satisaṃvarau, laukuttarau satisaṃvarau’’ti dubbidhaina vibhajitabbauti| laukiyau satisaṃvarau kāmāvacarauvāti aikavidhaina vibhajitabbau| laukuttarā satisaṃvarau ‘‘dassanabhūmi, bhāvanābhūmī’’ti dubbidhaina vibhajitabbau| kāmāvacarau satisaṃvarau ‘‘kāyānupassanāsatisaṃvarau vaidanānupassanāsatisaṃvarau cittānupassanāsatisaṃvarau dhammānupassanāsatisaṃvarau’’ti catubbidhaina vibhajitabbau| laukuttarau satisaṃvaraupi tathā catubbidhaina vibhajitabbau| sammāsaṅkappasammādiṭṭhiyaupi laukiyalaukuttaravasaina dubbidhādibhaidaina vibhajitabbā| padaṭṭhānainapi padaṭṭhānahārasampātai vuttanayaina vibhajitabbā|

akusalapakkhaina ‘‘arakkhitaina cittainā’’ti padaina vuttau asaṃvarau ‘‘cakkhuasaṃvarau …pai॰… kāyaasaṃvarau, caupanakāyaasaṃvarau, vācāasaṃvarau, manauasaṃvarau’’ti aṭṭhavidhaina vibhajitabbau| ‘‘micchādiṭṭhihatainā’’ti padaina gahitau micchāsaṅkappau ‘‘kāmavitak‍kau byāpādavitak‍kau vihiṃsāvitak‍kau’’ti tividhaina vibhajitabbau| ‘‘micchādiṭṭhī’’ti padaina vuttā micchādiṭṭhi ‘‘dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰…idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu añ‍ñāṇa’’nti aṭṭhavidhaina vibhajitabbā, dvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhividhainapi vibhajitabbā| thinamiddhaṃ pañ‍cavidhaina sasaṅkhārikavidhaina vibhajitabbaṃ|

‘‘aittakauva vibhattihārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau vibhattihārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina vibhattihārasampātaina suttappadaisatthā vibhattā, sau sau  saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau vibhattihārasampātau niyuttauti yathāraha niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbauti|

iti vibhattihārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

9. parivattanahārasampātavibhāvanā

71. yaina  yaina vibhattihārasampātaina suttappadaisatthā vibhattā, sau vibhattihārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau parivattanahārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau parivattanau hārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

‘‘katamai suttappadaisatthā kathaṃ parivattaitabbā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tasmā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yā ‘‘tasmā…pai॰… gaucarau’’ti gāthā vuttā, tāya gāthāya yā samathavipassanā niddhāritā, tāya samathavipassanāya bhāvitāya akusalānaṃ niraudhau phalaṃ payaujanaṃ hauti, pariñ‍ñātaṃ dukkhaṃ hauti, samudayau pahīnau hauti, maggau bhāvitau hautīti parivattaitabbau| paṭipakkhaina pana samathavipassanāya abhāvitāya akusalānaṃ aniraudhau, apariñ‍ñātaṃ dukkhaṃ, samudayau appahīnau, maggau abhāvitau hautīti parivattaitabbau|

‘‘aittakauva parivattanahārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau parivattanau hārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina parivattanahārasampātaina suttappadaisatthā parivattaitabbā, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau parivattanahārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ nīharitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbauti|

iti parivattanahārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

10. vaivacanahārasampātavibhāvanā

72. yaina  yaina parivattanahārasampātaina suttappadaisatthā parivattaitabbā, sau parivattanau hārasampātauti paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau vaivacanau hārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau vaivacanau hārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

‘‘katamaisaṃ  suttappadaisatthānaṃ, suttapadānaṃ vā katamāni vaivacanānī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tasmā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yā ‘‘tasmā…pai॰… gaucarau’’ti gāthā vuttā, tāya gāthāya ‘‘rakkhitacittassā’’ti padaina vuttassa cittassa, ‘‘rakkhitacittassā’’ti aittha cittassa padassa vā ‘‘cittaṃ…pai॰… vijānitatta’’nti yaṃ vacanaṃ vuttaṃ, idaṃ vacanaṃ vaivacanaṃ, ‘‘mānasaṃ hadaya’’ntiādivacanampi (dha॰ sa॰ 17, 63) cittassa vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti aittha sammāsaṅkappassa ‘‘naikkhammasaṅkappau abyāpādasaṅkappau avihiṃsāsaṅkappau’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ vuttaṃ, idaṃ vacanaṃ vaivacanaṃ, ‘‘tak‍kau vitak‍kau’’tiādi (dha॰ sa॰ 7) vacanampi sammāsaṅkappassa vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti aittha sammādiṭṭhipadassa ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi nāma pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñākhaggau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ pañ‍ñāpataudau pañ‍ñāpāsādau’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ vuttaṃ, idaṃ vacanaṃ vaivacanaṃ, ‘‘pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau’’tiādi (dha॰ sa॰ 16) vacanampi sammādiṭṭhipadassa vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘thinaṃ thiyanā thiyitattaṃ cittassa, cittassa akal‍latā akammañ‍ñatā aunāhau pariyaunāhau antausaṅkaucau’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ 1162-1163) yaṃ vacanaṃ vuttaṃ, idaṃ vacanaṃ thinassa vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘kāyassa akal‍latā akammañ‍ñatā kāyālasiyaṃ sauppaṃ supanā suppitatta’’nti (dha॰ sa॰ 1163) yaṃ vacanaṃ vuttaṃ, idaṃ vacanaṃ middhassa vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘bhikkhakau bhikkhū’’tiādikaṃ (pārā॰ 45; vibha॰ 510) yaṃ vacanaṃ vuttaṃ, idaṃ vacanaṃ bhikkhupadassa vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘duggati apāyau vinipātau vaṭṭadukkhaṃ saṃsārau’’tiādikaṃ yaṃ vacanaṃ vuttaṃ, idaṃ vacanaṃ duggatipadassa vaivacanaṃ| iti vaivacanāni nīharitāni|

‘‘aittakauva vaivacanahārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau vaivacanau hārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina vaivacanahārasampātaina vaivacanāni nīharitāni, sau sau  saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau vaivacanahārasampātau niyuttau yathāsambhavaṃ nīharitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahitauti|

iti vaivacanahārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

11. pañ‍ñattihārasampātavibhāvanā

73. yaina  yaina vaivacanahārasampātaina vaivacanāni niddhāritāni, sau vaivacanahārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau pañ‍ñattihārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau pañ‍ñattihārasampātau’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

‘‘katamā suttappadaisabhūtā pañ‍ñatti katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñattī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tasmā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’ti pañ‍ñatti satiyā padaṭṭhānassa rakkhitabbassa cittassa pañ‍ñāpanatau satiyā padaṭṭhānapañ‍ñatti nāma| satiyā hi rakkhitabbaṃ cittaṃ satiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ nāma| taināha aṭṭhakathāyaṃ – ‘‘adhiṭṭhahitvā rakkhantiyā satiyā rakkhiyamānaṃ cittaṃ tassā adhiṭṭhānaṃ viya hautī’’ti| ‘‘sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti pañ‍ñatti samathassa bhāvanāya pañ‍ñāpanatau samathassa bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti nāma| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau, ñatvāna udayabbaya’’nti pañ‍ñatti dassanabhūmiyā nikkhaipassa pañ‍ñāpanatau nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti nāma| ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhū’’ti samudayassa anavasaisappahānassa pañ‍ñāpanatau anavasaisappahānapañ‍ñatti nāma| ‘‘sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti pañ‍ñatti maggassa ariyamaggassa bhāvanāya pañ‍ñāpanatau bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti nāma|

‘‘aittakauva pañ‍ñattihārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau pañ‍ñattihārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina pañ‍ñattihārasampātaina pañ‍ñattippabhaidā niddhāritā, sau sau  saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau pañ‍ñattihārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbauti|

iti pañ‍ñattihārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

12. autaraṇahārasampātavibhāvanā

74. yaina  yaina pañ‍ñattihārasampātaina pañ‍ñattippabhaidā niddhāritā, sau pañ‍ñattihārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamau autaraṇau hārasampātau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamau autaraṇau hārasampātau’’tiādimāha|

‘‘tattha tissaṃ gāthāyaṃ katamāni indriyāni niddhāraitvā katamaihi niddhāritaihi dhammaihi autaratī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tasmā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yā ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’tiādigāthā vuttā, tissaṃ gāthāyaṃ ‘‘tasmā…pai॰… puraikkhārau’’ti suttappadaisaina sammādiṭṭhi gahitā, sammādiṭṭhiyā gahitāya pañ‍cindriyāni saddhādipañ‍cindriyāni gahitāni bhavanti|

tāniyaiva saddhādipañ‍cindriyāni vij‍jāya upakārattā vā padaṭṭhānattā vā vij‍jā bhavanti, vij‍juppādā tādisāya vij‍jāya uppādā uppādahaitutau avij‍jāniraudhau sambhavati, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau sambhavati…pai॰… jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau sambhavatīti| ayaṃ autaraṇā paṭic‍casamuppādaina pañ‍cindriyānaṃ autaraṇā nāma|

tāniyaiva pañ‍cindriyāni sīlakkhandhaina samādhikkhandhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina tīhi khandhaihi saṅgahitāni bhavanti saddhāvīriyaihi sīlasambhavatau, satiyā ca pañ‍ñānuvattakattā| ayaṃ autaraṇā khandhaihi pañ‍cindriyānaṃ autaraṇā nāma|

tāniyaiva pañ‍cindriyāni saṅkhārapariyāpan‍nāni bhavanti| yai saṅkhārā anāsavā bhavanti, bhavaṅgā bhavahaitū nau bhavanti, tai anāsavā saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā bhavanti dhammadhātuyā antaugadhattā| ayaṃ autaraṇā dhātūhi pañ‍cindriyānaṃ autaraṇā nāma|

sā  anāsavā dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā bhavati, yaṃ āyatanaṃ anāsavaṃ bhavati, bhavaṅgaṃ bhavahaitu nau bhavati| ayaṃ autaraṇā āyatanaihi dhammadhātuyā autaraṇā nāma|

‘‘aittakauva autaraṇau hārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau autaraṇau hārasampātau’’ti  vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina autaraṇahārasampātaina suttappadaisatthā autaritabbā, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau autaraṇau hārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbauti|

iti autaraṇahārasampātai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana…pai॰… gahaitabbauti|

itau paṭṭhāya ‘‘tattha katamau’’tiādianusandhyatthau ca pariyausānai ‘‘niyuttau’’tiādianusandhyatthau ca vuttanayānusārainaiva viñ‍ñātabbau| katthaci katthaci pāṭhai apākaṭaṃyaiva yathābalaṃ kathayissāma|

13. saudhanahārasampātavibhāvanā

75. yatthāti yasmiṃ pañhai| ārambhau atthau suddhau paripuṇṇau, sau pañhau niravasaisatau vissaj‍jitau bhavati| yattha pañhai pana ārambhau atthau na suddhau aparipuṇṇau kauci vissaj‍jaitabbau avasiṭṭhau, sau pañhau tāva vissaj‍jitau na bhavati|

15. parikkhārahārasampātavibhāvanā

77. ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’ti padaina yau satisaṃvarau vuttau, ayaṃ satisaṃvarau samathassa parikkhārau| ‘‘sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti padaina yau sammāsaṅkappau vuttau, sau sammāsaṅkappau vipassanāya parikkhārauti vibhajitvā yaujaitabbau|

16. samāraupanahārasampātavibhāvanā

78. ‘‘tasmā  rakkhitacittassā’’ti padaina yaṃ rakkhitacittaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ rakkhitacittaṃ tiṇṇaṃ sucaritānaṃ kammānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| sammādiṭṭhiyā bhāvitāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvitau bhavati aikatau bhāvitabbattā, padaṭṭhānakāraṇattā ca| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘sammādiṭṭhitau hī’’tiādi| yassa arahatau samādhivimutti bhavati, ayaṃ arahā anupādisaisau puggalau, ayaṃ samādhivimutti anupādisaisā nibbānadhātu|

‘‘daisanāhārasampātādikau hārasampātau yaina sauḷasappabhaidabhāvaina suttappadaisatthai niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitau, sauḷasappabhaidabhāvau kaina amhaihi saddahitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taināhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taina sauḷasappabhaidabhāvaina āyasmā mahākac‍cānau ‘‘sauḷasa…pai॰… sutta’’nti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, taina vacanaina sauḷasappabhaidabhāvau tumhaihi saddahitabbau|

‘‘aittakauva hārasampātau paripuṇṇau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttau hārasampātau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina daisanāhārasampātaina suttappadaisatthā niddhāritā, sau sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau daisanāhārasampātādihārasampātau niyuttau yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbauti atthau gahaitabbau, aṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 73, 76) tathā niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitauti|

iti daisanāhārasampātādihārasampātai sattibalānurūpā

racitā vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana aṭṭhakathāṭīkānusāraina gambhīratthau vitthāratau vibhajitvā gahaitabbauti|

nayasamuṭṭhānavibhāvanā

79. yaina yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtaina daisanāhārasampātādinā hārasampātaina aikasuttappadaisatthā niddhāraitvā vibhattā, sau  saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisabhūtau daisanāhārasampātādihārasampātau paripuṇṇau, ‘‘katamaṃ nayasamuṭṭhāna’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ nayasamuṭṭhāna’’ntiādi āraddhaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana –‘‘aivaṃ nānāsuttavasaina, aikasuttavasaina ca  hāravicāraṃ dassaitvā idāni nayavicāraṃ dassaituṃ ‘tattha katamaṃ nayasamuṭṭhāna’ntiādi āraddha’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 79) vuttaṃ| ‘‘tattha katamau nandiyāvaṭṭanayau’’tiādiṃ anārabhitvā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ nayasamuṭṭhāna’’ntiādiārambhanai kāraṇaṃ aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttamaiva| tattha tatthāti taisu hārādīsu yau atthanayau saṃvaṇṇanānayaina niddiṭṭhau, tassaiva atthanayassa samuṭṭhānaṃ bhūmiṃ pucchati ‘‘katamaṃ nayasamuṭṭhāna’’nti|

kiñ‍cāpi saṃvaṇṇanānayā niddiṭṭhā, tathāpi atthanayasaṃvaṇṇanānayānaṃ visaisassa pākaṭaṃ kātuṃ puna kathayissāma| taṇhāavij‍jāhi saṃkilaisapakkhassa suttatthassa catusac‍cayaujanamukhaina nayanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau caiva samathavipassanāhi vaudānapakkhassa suttatthassa catusac‍cayaujanamukhaina nayanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca nandiyāvaṭṭau nayau nāma| tīhi akusalamūlaihi laubhādīhi saṃkilaisapakkhassa suttatthassa catusac‍cayaujanamukhaina nayanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau caiva tīhi kusalamūlaihi alaubhādīhi vaudānapakkhassa suttatthassa catusac‍cayaujanamukhaina nayanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca tipukkhalau nayau nāma| catūhi subhasañ‍ñādīhi vipal‍lāsaihi sakalasaṃkilaisapakkhassa suttatthassa catusac‍cayaujanamukhaina nayanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau caiva catūhi asubhasañ‍ñādīhi avipal‍lāsaihi satipaṭṭhānaihi, saddhindriyaihi vā vaudānapakkhassa suttatthassa catusac‍cayaujanamukhaina nayanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca sīhavik‍kīḷitau nayau nāmāti vuttā nayā saṃvaṇṇanānayā nāma| taisaṃ saṃvaṇṇanānayānaṃ samuṭṭhānaṃ pucchati ‘‘katamaṃ nayasamuṭṭhāna’’nti|

nayā pana nānāsuttatau niddhāritaihi taṇhāavij‍jādīhi mūlapadaihi catusac‍cayaujanāya nayatau anubujjhiyamānau dukkhādiatthau| sau hi maggañāṇaṃ nayati sampāpaitīti ‘‘nayau’’ti vuttau, sau atthanayau nāma| tassaiva atthanayassa samuṭṭhānaṃ pucchati ‘‘katamaṃ nayasamuṭṭhāna’’nti? tasmā  catusac‍cayaujanāya nayaggāhatau nīyati anupucchīyatīti nayau, kau sau? suttatthabhūtau dukkhādikau atthau| nayati maggañāṇaṃ pāpaitīti vā nayau, dukkhādikau atthauva| taināha – ‘‘anubujjhiyamānau dukkhādiatthau| sau hi maggañāṇaṃ nayati sampāpaitīti nayau’’ti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 79)| samuṭṭhahanti nayā aitaināti samuṭṭhānaṃ, kiṃ taṃ? avij‍jātaṇhādikāraṇaṃ, taṃdīpanā saṃvaṇṇanā ca| avij‍jātaṇhādinā hi dukkhādiatthanayā sambhavanti| atha vā avij‍jātaṇhādīhi catusac‍cayaujanā samuṭṭhānaṃ nāma| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘kiṃ pana taṃ? taṃtaṃmūlapadaihi catusac‍cayaujanā’’ti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 79)| aivaṃ atthai gayhamānai sati saṃvaṇṇanānayāpi gahitā haunti, nayānaṃ samuṭṭhānaṃ nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ, tasmiṃ nayasamuṭṭhānai|

‘‘katamaṃ  nandiyāvaṭṭanayasamuṭṭhāna’’nti pucchitabbattā paṭhamaṃ nandiyāvaṭṭanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyatī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| nandiyāvaṭṭanayasamuṭṭhānabhūtāya avij‍jāya ca taṇhāya ca pubbā kauṭi ‘‘asukassa buddhassa bhagavatau uppaj‍janakālai uppan‍nā, asukassa cak‍kavattinau uppaj‍janakālai uppan‍nā’’ti na pañ‍ñāyati kauṭiyā abhāvatauti yaujanā| ‘‘avij‍jātaṇhāsu katamā nīvaraṇaṃ, katamā saṃyaujana’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha avij‍jā nīvaraṇaṃ taṇhā saṃyaujana’’nti vuttaṃ| tatthāti tāsu avij‍jātaṇhāsu| ādīnavapaṭicchādikattā avij‍jā nīvaraṇaṃ| bhavaisu saṃyaujanatau taṇhā saṃyaujanaṃ| ‘‘avij‍jānīvaraṇā sattā kathaṃ vicaranti, kathaṃ vuc‍canti, taṇhāsaṃyaujanā sattā kathaṃ vicaranti kathaṃ vuc‍cantī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘avij‍jānīvaraṇā sattā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| avij‍jānīvaraṇamaitaisamatthīti avij‍jānīvaraṇā| avij‍jāya saṃyuttā viya pavattā avij‍jāya abhinivisavatthūsu saṃyuttā viya pavattā sattā avij‍jāpakkhaina vipal‍lāsaina nic‍cādiabhinivaisavatthubhūtai rūpādiārammaṇai vicaranti vividhā caranti pavattanti, tai nic‍cādiabhinivisantā vicarantā sattā ‘‘diṭṭhicaritā’’ti vuc‍canti| taṇhāsaṃyaujanamaitaisanti taṇhāsaṃyaujanā| taṇhāya saṃyuttā viya pavattā, taṇhāya vā ārammaṇabhūtai vatthukāmai saṃyuttā viya pavattā sattā taṇhāpakkhaina aṭṭhasatataṇhāvicaritaina ārammaṇabhūtai vatthusmiṃ vicaranti pavattantīti atthau|

‘‘diṭṭhicaritā  sattā kaṃ paṭipattiṃ anuyuttā viharanti, taṇhācaritā sattā kaṃ paṭipattiṃ anuyuttā viharantī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘diṭṭhicaritā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| diṭṭhicaritā sattā itau sāsanatau bahiddhā pabbajitā hauntāpi ‘‘sukhaina adhigantabbaṃ sukhaṃ natthi, dukkhaina adhigantabbaṃ sukhaṃ pana atthī’’ti manasi karauntā attakilamathānuyaugaṃ pañ‍cātapādipaṭipattiṃ anuyuttā viharanti| taṇhācaritā sattā itau sāsanatau bahiddhā pabbajitā hauntāpi ‘‘kāmai paṭisaivantā laukaṃ vaḍḍhāpaintā bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ vaḍḍhāpaintī’’ti manasi karauntā kāmaisu kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugaṃ paṭipattiṃ anuyuttā viharanti|

‘‘kasmā diṭṭhicaritā tathāvidhaṃ paṭipattiṃ anuyuttā viharanti, kasmā taṇhācaritā tathāvidhaṃ paṭipattiṃ anuyuttā viharantī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha kiṃ kāraṇa’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti tattha taisu diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritaisu| yaṃ yasmā kāraṇā viharanti, taṃ kāraṇaṃ kinti pucchati| itau sāsanatau bahiddhā yaisaṃ puggalānaṃ sac‍cavavatthānaṃ natthi, catusac‍cappakāsanā kutau ca atthi, samathavipassanākausal‍laṃ vā upasamasukhappatti vā kutau atthi, tai puggalā upasamasukhassa anabhiñ‍ñā viparītacaitā hutvā aivaṃ āhaṃsu ‘‘sukhaina adhigantabbaṃ sukhaṃ natthi, dukkhaina adhigantabbaṃ sukhaṃ nāma atthī’’ti| tai aivaṃsañ‍ñī aivaṃdiṭṭhī dukkhaina sukhaṃ patthayamānā hutvā attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti| itau sāsanatau bahiddhā yaisaṃ puggalānaṃ sac‍cavavatthānaṃ natthi, catusac‍cappakāsanā kutau ca atthi, samathavipassanākausal‍laṃ vā upasamasukhappatti vā kutau atthi, tai puggalā upasamasukhassa anabhiñ‍ñā viparītacaitā hutvā aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘yau kāmai paṭisaivati, sau laukaṃ vaḍḍhayati, yau laukaṃ vaḍḍhayati, sau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| tai aivaṃsañ‍ñī aivaṃdiṭṭhī kāmaisu sukhasañ‍ñī hutvā kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā ca viharantīti yaujanā kātabbā|

‘‘tathā viharantā kiṃ vaḍḍhayantī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tai tadabhiñ‍ñā santā raugamaiva vaḍḍhayantī’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘tathā vaḍḍhayantā raugādīnaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ samathavipassanaṃ vaḍḍhayanti ki’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tai raugābhitun‍nā gaṇḍapaṭipīḷitā sal‍lānuviddhā nirayatiracchānayaunipaitāsuraisu ummuj‍janimuj‍jāni karauntā ugghātanigghātaṃ pac‍canubhauntā raugagaṇḍasal‍labhaisaj‍jaṃ na  vindantī’’ti vuttaṃ| atthau pana aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 79) vuttau| ‘‘katamai saṃkilaisavaudānā, katamai raugādayau, katamaṃ bhaisaj‍ja’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha attakilamathānuyaugau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu saṃkilaisavaudānaraugabhaisaj‍jādīsu| attakilamathānuyaugau ca kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugau ca saṃkilaisau hauti, samathavipassanā vaudānaṃ hauti, attakilamathānuyaugau ca raugau hauti, samathavipassanā rauganigghātakabhaisaj‍jaṃ…pai॰.. samathavipassanā sal‍luddhāraṇabhaisaj‍jaṃ hauti|

‘‘katamau katamaṃ sac‍ca’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha saṃkilaisau dukkha’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu saṃkilaisādīsu saṃkilaisau aikadaisavasaina dukkhaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ hauti| tadabhisaṅgauti tasmiṃ saṃkilaisai abhisaṅgau viya pavattau laukiyadhammau niravasaisavasaina dukkhasac‍caṃ hauti| atha vā tasmiṃ dukkhai abhisaṅgau viya pavattā taṇhā dukkhasamudayau samudayasac‍caṃ hauti| taṇhāniraudhau dukkhaniraudhau niraudhasac‍caṃ hauti| samathavipassanā dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā maggasac‍caṃ hauti| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni niddhāraitvā yaujaitabbāni| ‘‘taisu catūsu sac‍caisu katamaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, katamau pahātabbau, katamau bhāvaitabbau, katamau sacchikātabbau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

80. diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritānaṃ attakilamathānuyaugādivasaina cattāri sac‍cāni niddhāritāni, ‘‘kathaṃ diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritānaṃ sak‍kāyadassanai pavattibhaidavasaina cattāri sac‍cāni niddhāritānī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha diṭṭhicaritā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| atha vā ‘‘diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritānaṃ  sak‍kāyadassanai katamau pavattibhaidau’’ti pucchitabbattā imaisaṃ sak‍kāyadassanai ayaṃ pavattibhaidauti vibhajitvā dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha diṭṭhicaritā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘idāni diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritānaṃ sak‍kāyadiṭṭhidassanai pavattibhaidaṃ dassaituṃ ‘diṭṭhicaritā’tiādi vutta’’nti| tatthāti taisu diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritaisu| diṭṭhicaritā puggalā rūpaṃ ‘‘attā’’ti attatau upagacchanti…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘‘attā’’ti attatau upagacchanti diṭṭhicaritānaṃ attābhinivaisassa balavabhāvatau, taṇhācaritā pana rūpaṃ vā  ‘‘attā’’ti rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ upagacchanti attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā ‘‘attā’’ti attānaṃ upagacchanti…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā ‘‘attā’’ti attānaṃ upagacchanti taṇhācaritānaṃ attaniyābhinivaisassa balavabhāvatau| pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu aikaikaṃ nissāya catubbidhattā vīsativatthukā ayaṃ micchādiṭṭhi ‘‘sak‍kāyadiṭṭhī’’ti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritānaṃ sak‍kāyadassanai pavattibhaidau viñ‍ñātabbauti atthau|

‘‘sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā katamau paṭipakkhau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tassā paṭipakkhau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tassā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā pajahanavasaina laukuttarā sammādiṭṭhi paṭipakkhau, tassā sammādiṭṭhiyā anvāyikā anuguṇabhāvaina pavattanakā dhammā ca sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā pajahanavasaina paṭipakkhā bhavanti| ‘‘katamai dhammā anvāyikā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sammāsaṅkappau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| sammāsaṅkappau…pai॰… sammāsamādhi imai dhammā anvāyikā haunti| ayaṃ sammādiṭṭhiādikau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau tassā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā paṭipakkhau hauti pahāyakattā| ‘‘tai sammādiṭṭhiyādayau dhammā khandhatau kittakā hauntī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tai tayau khandhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘katamau khandhau samathau, katamau khandhau vipassanā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sīlakkhandhau samādhikkhandhau ca samathau, pañ‍ñākkhandhau vipassanā’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘sak‍kāyādīsu katamau katamaṃ sac‍caṃ, katamau katamaṃ sac‍ca’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha sak‍kāyau’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

‘‘diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritānaṃ sak‍kāyadiṭṭhitappaṭipakkhavasaina cattāri sac‍cāni niddhāritāni, kathaṃ antadvayamajjhimapaṭipadā niddhāritā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha yai rūpaṃ attatau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritaisu yai diṭṭhicaritā puggalā rūpaṃ ‘‘attā’’ti attatau upagacchanti…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘‘attā’’ti attatau upagacchanti| imai diṭṭhicaritā puggalā ‘‘rūpādayau ca attā, rūpādīnañ‍ca anic‍cattā, attassāpi anic‍cattā attā ucchij‍jati, attā vinassati, attā paraṃ maraṇā na hautī’’ti abhinivisanatau ‘‘ucchaidavādinau’’ti  vuc‍canti| yai taṇhācaritā puggalā rūpaṃ vā ‘‘attā’’ti rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ upagacchanti…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vā ‘‘attā’’ti viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ attānaṃ upagacchanti| attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ upagacchanti| imai taṇhācaritā puggalā ‘‘rūpādīhi attā añ‍ñau  añ‍ñattā attā nic‍cau sassatau’’ti abhinivisanatau ‘‘sassatavādinau’’ti vuc‍canti|

tattha taisu ucchaidavādīsassatavādīpuggalaisu pavattā ucchaidavādasassatavādā ubhau antā antadvayapaṭipadā haunti| ayaṃ antadvayapaṭipadā saṃsārapavattanassa haitubhāvatau saṃsārapavatti hauti, tassa antadvayassa paṭipaj‍janassa pajahanavasaina majjhimapaṭipadāsaṅkhātauva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau paṭipakkhau hauti pahāyakattā| ayaṃ maggau saṃsāranivattanassa haitubhāvatau saṃsāranivatti hauti| tattha saṃsārapavattisaṃsāranivattīsu pavatti saṃsārapavatti dukkhaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ, tadabhisaṅgau tasmiṃ dukkhai abhisaṅgau taṇhā samudayau samudayasac‍caṃ, taṇhāniraudhau dukkhaniraudhau niraudhasac‍caṃ, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā maggasac‍caṃ, iti imāni cattāri sac‍cāni niddhāritāni| ‘‘dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyya’’ntiādimhi vuttanayauva atthau|

‘‘ucchaidasassatassa kittakau pabhaidau, maggassa kittakau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha ucchaidasassata’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha ucchaidasassataariyamaggaisu ucchaidasassatadassanaṃ samāsatau saṅkhaipatau vīsativatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi| ucchaidau pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai nissāya pavattattā pañ‍cavidhau, sassatadassanaṃ aikaikasmiṃ tidhā uppaj‍janatau pan‍narasavidhanti vīsatividhaṃ hauti| vitthāratau dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni| katamāni? cattārau sassatavādā, cattārau aikac‍casassatavādā, cattārau antānantavādā, cattārau amarāvikkhaipavādā, dvai adhic‍casamuppan‍navādā, sauḷasa sañ‍ñīvādā, aṭṭha asañ‍ñīvādā, aṭṭha naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā, satta ucchaidavādā, pañ‍ca diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādāti dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni vaiditabbāni| vitthāratau pana brahmajālasuttai (dī॰ ni॰ 1.30 ādayau) āgatāni| taisaṃ ucchaidasassatadassanānaṃ taicattālīsaṃ baudhipakkhiyadhammā paṭipakkhau maggau| katamai taicattālīsaṃ? ‘‘anic‍casañ‍ñā dukkhasañ‍ñā anattasañ‍ñā’’ti tissau sañ‍ñā ca ‘‘pahānasañ‍ñā virāgasañ‍ñā niraudhasañ‍ñā’’ti tissau sañ‍ñā cāti cha sañ‍ñā ca ‘‘cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, aṭṭha maggaṅgānī’’ti taicattālīsaṃ baudhipakkhiyadhammā vipassanāvasaina paṭipakkhau maggau nāma|

‘‘samathavasaina  katamau paṭipakkhau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘aṭṭha vimaukkhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aṭṭha vimaukkhā pākaṭā| dasa kasiṇāyatanāni samathavasaina paṭipakkhau maggau nāma| ‘‘katamaṃ ñāṇaṃ katamassa dhammassa padālana’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatānī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| diṭṭhicaritai pavattāni diṭṭhigatāni, mauhau| taṇhācaritai pavattāni diṭṭhigatāni, jālaṃ| anādivasaina pavattau mauhau| anidhanavasaina pavattaṃ jālaṃ| aṭṭha samāpattiyau samāpaj‍jitvā taijaitvā tikkhaṃ vipassanāñāṇañ‍ca ariyamaggañāṇañ‍cañāṇavajiraṃ nāma baudhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ ñāṇapadaṭṭhānattā| mauhau ca jālañ‍ca mauhajālaṃ| padālaitīti padālanaṃ, kattari yupac‍cayau, mauhajālassa padālananti mauhajālapadālanaṃ| padālanañhi duvidhaṃ vikkhambhanapadālanaṃ samucchaidapadālananti| pubbabhāgai samathavipassanāvasaina vikkhambhanapadālanaṃ, maggakkhaṇai samucchaidapadālanaṃ aiva daṭṭhabbaṃ| tatthāti tasmiṃ mauhajālai avij‍jā mauhau, bhavataṇhā jālaṃ| attanau ādhāraṃ puggalaṃ dukkhādīsu aṭṭhasu ṭhānaisu mauhaitīti mauhau| paṭhamaṃ jālaṃ jaṭaṃ lāyitvā jaṭāvasaina lāti pavattatīti jālaṃ, attani jātaṃ macchasakuṇādikaṃ lāti gaṇhāti, lāpaiti gaṇhāpaitīti vā jālaṃ, jālaṃ viyāti jālaṃ| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘atītādibhaidabhin‍naisu rūpādīsu, sakaattabhāvādīsu ca saṃsibbanavasaina pavattanatau jālaṃ bhavataṇhā’’ti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 80)| ‘‘avij‍jātaṇhāhi attakilamathānuyaugādīnaṃ kilaisapakkhānaṃ niddhāraṇaṃ kataṃ kathaṃ kaina saddahitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘taina vuc‍cati ‘pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jāya ca bhavataṇhāya cā’ti’’ vuttaṃ|

81. ‘‘itau sāsanatau bahiddhā diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritānaṃ paṭipadādayau niddhāritā, sāsanai diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritānaṃ paṭipadādayau kathaṃ niddhāritabbā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘saṃkilaisapakkhā suttatthā ācariyaina dassitā, vaudānapakkhasuttatthā kathaṃ dassitabbā’’ti vattabbattā vā ‘‘tattha diṭṭhicaritau asmiṃ sāsanai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu diṭṭhicaritataṇhācaritaisu| diṭṭhicaritau puggalau asmiṃ sāsanai pabbajitau hutvā catūsu pac‍cayaisu sal‍laikhānusantatavutti bhavati| kasmā? yasmā sal‍laikhai tibbagāravau, tasmā sal‍laikhai tibbagāravattā| taṇhācaritau puggalau asmiṃ sāsanai pabbajitau hutvā sikkhānusantatavutti bhavati| kasmā? yasmā sikkhāya tibbagāravau, tasmā sikkhāya tibbagāravattā| diṭṭhicaritau puggalau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamantau hutvā dhammānusārī puggalau bhavati| kasmā? diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivisayai pañ‍ñāsadisapavattanatau| taṇhācaritau puggalau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamantau hutvā saddhānusārī bhavati| kasmā? taṇhāvasaina micchādhimaukkhattā| diṭṭhicaritau puggalau sukhāya paṭipadāya, dandhābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyāti, sukhāya  paṭipadāya, khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyāti sukhaina kilaisaihi vikkhambhituṃ samatthattā| taṇhācaritau puggalau dukkhāya paṭipadāya, dandhābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyāti, dukkhāya paṭipadāya, khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyāti dukkhaina kilaisaihi vikkhambhituṃ samatthattā|

‘‘tathā kasmā niyyātī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha kiṃ kāraṇaṃ ya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| yaṃ yaina kāraṇaina niyyāti, taṃ kāraṇaṃ kinti pucchati| tassa taṇhācaritassa kāmā sukhaina aparic‍cattā hi yasmā bhavanti, tasmā kāmānaṃ sukhaina aparic‍cattattā tathā niyyāti| sau taṇhācaritau kāmaihi vatthukāmakilaisakāmaihi vivaiciyamānau dukkhaina paṭinissarati, dandhañ‍ca dhammaṃ catusac‍cadhammaṃ ājānāti| yau pana ayaṃ puggalau diṭṭhicaritau hauti, sau ayaṃ diṭṭhicaritau puggalau āditau ādimhiyaiva kāmaihi kilaisakāmavatthukāmaihi anatthikau bhavati| sau diṭṭhicaritau tatau taihi kāmaihi vivaiciyamānau khippañ‍ca sukhaina paṭinissarati, khippañ‍ca dhammaṃ ājānāti|

‘‘taṇhācaritau dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, diṭṭhicaritau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyātīti aikaikāya paṭipadāya bhavitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘dukkhāpi paṭipadā duvidhā dandhābhiñ‍ñā ca khippābhiñ‍ñā ca, sukhāpi paṭipadā duvidhā dandhābhiñ‍ñā ca khippābhiñ‍ñā cā’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ sati aikauva dvīhi dvīhi paṭipadāhi niyyātīti āpaj‍jatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘sattāpī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taṇhācaritā sattāpi duvidhā mudindriyāpi tikkhindriyāpi, diṭṭhicaritā sattāpi duvidhā mudindriyāpi tikkhindriyāpīti yaujanā kātabbā| yai taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritā mudindriyā bhavanti, tai taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritā dandhañ‍ca paṭinissaranti, dandhañ‍ca dhammaṃ ājānanti| yai taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritā tikkhindriyā bhavanti, tai taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritā khippañ‍ca paṭinissaranti, khippañ‍ca dhammaṃ ājānanti, tasmā aikaikassaiva aikaikā paṭipadā  yuttāvāti| ‘‘imāhi paṭipadāhi niyyantiyaiva, na niyyiṃsu niyyissantīti āpaj‍jaiyya vattamānavibhattiyā niddiṭṭhattā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘imā catassau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| atītaipi imāhi catūhi paṭipadāhi niyyiṃsu, pac‍cuppan‍naisupi niyyanti, anāgataipi niyyissantīti atthau gahaitabbau yathā ‘‘pabbatau tiṭṭhatī’’ti|

aivanti aivaṃ vuttappakāraina| ariyapuggalā catuk‍kamaggaṃ paṭipadaṃ pañ‍ñāpainti| ‘‘kimatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpaintī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘abudhajanasaivitāyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa bhūmīti taṇhāavij‍jānaṃ vasaina saṃkilaisapakkhai dvidisā catusac‍cayaujanāpi samathavipassanānaṃ  vasaina vaudānapakkhai dvidisā catusac‍cayaujanāpi dassitā| ayaṃ catubbidhā catusac‍cayaujanā nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa samuṭṭhānaṃ bhūmi samuṭṭhānabhāvatauti| ‘‘tathāvidhāya catusac‍cayaujanāya nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa samuṭṭhānabhūmibhāvau kaina saddahitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taināhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

82. ‘‘nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa samuṭṭhānabhūtā bhūmi dassitā, tassa nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa kattha katamā disā kittakaina upaparikkhitabbā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘vaiyyākaraṇaisu hī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| hi-saddau pakkhantaratthau| yai disābhūtā āhārādayau dhammā vaiyyākaraṇaisu ‘‘kusalākusalā’’ti vuttaṃ, tai disābhūtā āhārādayau dhammā duvidhaina ‘‘imai akusalā dhammā saṃkilaisadhammā, imai kusalā vaudānadhammā’’ti duvidhaina upaparikkhitabbā ālaucitabbā| ‘‘katamaina duvidhainā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘laukavaṭṭānusārī ca laukavivaṭṭānusārī cā’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘katamaṃ vaṭṭaṃ, katamaṃ vivaṭṭa’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘vaṭṭaṃ nāma saṃsārau, vivaṭṭaṃ nibbāna’’nti vuttaṃ| ‘‘saṃsārassa katamau haitū’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘kammakilaisā haitu saṃsārassā’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘katamaṃ kammaṃ nāma, caitanāyaiva kammaṃ nāma kiṃ, udāhu caitasikañ‍ca phaladānasamatthāsamatthampi kammaṃ ki’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha kamma’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu kammakilaisaisu| ‘‘taṃ kammaṃ kathaṃ kaina pakāraina daṭṭhabba’’nti cai vadaiyya, yathā yaina upacayaina kataṃ kammaṃ phaladānasamatthaṃ hauti, tasmiṃ upacayai taṃ kammaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘tai kilaisā katamaihi dhammaihi niddisitabbā’’ti cai pucchaiyya, sabbaipi kilaisā catūhi vipal‍lāsaihi niddisitabbā| ‘‘tai kilaisā kattha  daṭṭhabbā’’ti cai pucchaiyya, dasavatthukai kilaisapuñ‍jai tai kilaisā daṭṭhabbā|

‘‘katamāni dasa vatthūnī’’ti cai pucchaiyya, cattārau āhārā, cattārau vipal‍lāsā, cattāri upādānāni, cattārau yaugā, cattārau ganthā, cattārau āsavā, cattārau aughā, cattārau sal‍lā, catassau viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau, cattāri agatigamanānīti dasa vatthūnīti vaiditabbāni catun‍naṃ kic‍cavasaina aikattā| aittha ca kilaisānaṃ pac‍cayau vatthukāmaupi kilaisaupi kilaisavatthu hauti purimānaṃ purimānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pacchimānaṃ pacchimānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pac‍cayabhāvatau|

‘‘katamau katamau katamassa katamassa vatthū’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘paṭhamai āhārai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ālambitabbai paṭhamai kabaḷīkārāhārai paṭhamau ‘‘rūpaṃ subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina pavattati| ālambitabbai dutiyai phassāhārai dutiyau ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā sukhā’’ti  vipal‍lāsau pavattati| ālambitabbai tatiyai cittāhārai tatiyau ‘‘cittaṃ nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsau pavattati, ālambitabbai catutthai manausañ‍caitanāhārai ‘‘dhammau attā’’ti vipal‍lāsau ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina pavattati| ālambitabbai paṭhamai ‘‘rūpaṃ subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau paṭhamaṃ kāmupādānaṃ ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina pavattati| saisaisupi yathārahaṃ nayānusāraina yaujaitvā atthau vaiditabbau|

83. ‘‘taisu āhārādīsu katamau katamassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha yau cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu āhārādīsu, taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritaisu vā| taṇhācaritassa rūpavaidanāsu tibbacchandarāgassa uppaj‍janatau yau ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau, yau ca phassāhārau pavattati, imai kabaḷīkārāhāraphassāhārā taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā bhavanti| diṭṭhicaritassa dhammacittaisu balavaattanic‍cābhinivaisassa uppaj‍janatau yau ca manausañ‍caitanāhārau, yau ca viñ‍ñāṇāhārau pavattati, imai manausañ‍caitanāhāraviñ‍ñāṇāhārā diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā bhavanti| ‘‘tattha yau ca asubhai subha’’ntiādīsupi vuttanayānusāraina atthau gahaitabbauti| purimānaṃ  dvin‍naṃ dvin‍naṃ taṇhāpadhānattā caiva taṇhāsabhāvattā ca, pacchimānaṃ dvin‍naṃ dvin‍naṃ diṭṭhipadhānattā caiva diṭṭhisabhāvattā cāti|

84. ‘‘katamasmiṃ paṭhamādikai āhārai katamau paṭhamādikau vipal‍lāsau pavattatī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha kabaḷīkārai āhārai’’tiādinā nāmavasaina niyamaitvā pubbai vuttatthamaiva dassaiti| ‘‘katamasmiṃ vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau katamaṃ upādiyatī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘paṭhamai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| paṭhamai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau puggalau kāmai yaina upādānaina upādiyati, idaṃ upādānaṃ ‘‘kāmupādānaṃ nāmā’’ti vuc‍cati| saisaisu iminā nayaina yaujanā kātabbā|

‘‘yaina kāmupādānaina kāmaihi puggalau saṃyuj‍jati, ayaṃ kāmupādānadhammau ‘kāmayaugau’ti vuc‍catī’’tiādinā yaujanā kātabbā| saisānaṃ yaujanatthādayau pāḷitau, aṭṭhakathātau ca pākaṭā|

85. ‘‘āhārādīsu katamai katamā disā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha imā catassau disā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha taisu āhāracatuk‍kādīsu dasasu catuk‍kaisu paṭhamau paṭhamau kabaḷīkārāhārādikau  paṭhamā disā, dutiyau dutiyau phassāhārādikau dutiyā disā, tatiyau tatiyau viñ‍ñāṇāhārādikau tatiyā disā, catutthau catutthau manausañ‍caitanāhārādikau catutthā disāti vaiditabbā|

‘‘tāsu catūsu disāsu taisu kabaḷīkārāhārādīsu dhammaisu katamai dhammā katamassa upak‍kilaisā’’ti pucchitabbattā imai āhārādayau dhammā imassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisāti vibhattāti dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha yau ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau…pai॰… imai diṭṭhicaritassa udattassa upak‍kilaisā’’ti vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana – ‘‘kabaḷīkārāhārau āhārautiādi āhārādīsu yai yassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā, taṃ vibhajitvā dassaituṃ āraddha’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 85) vuttaṃ| dasan‍naṃ suttānanti aikadaisabhūtānaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ| atthauti sabhāvadhammau| saddatthau hi asamānauti| ‘‘byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti aitaina ca saddatthassa nānattaṃ dassaiti|

‘‘kabaḷīkārāhārādīsu  katamai āhārādayau katamaina vimaukkhamukhaina pariñ‍ñaṃ pahānaṃ gacchantī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yau ca kabaḷīkārau’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

itīti aivaṃ vuttappakārā| sabbaiti sabbai āhārādayau dhammā laukavaṭṭānusārinau bhavanti| taiti tai sabbai āhārādayau dhammā| laukāti laukatau vaṭṭatau| tīhi vimaukkhamukhaihi anic‍cānupassanādīhi niyyanti|

86. saṃkilaisapakkhai disābhūtā āhāracatuk‍kādayau dasa catuk‍kā dhammā ācariyaina niddhāraitvā dassitā, amhaihi ca ñātā, ‘‘vaudānapakkhai katamai disābhūtā dhammā’’ti pucchitabbattā vaudānapakkhai disābhūtai dhammai dassaituṃ ‘‘catassau paṭipadā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| cattārau vihārāti dibbavihārā brahmavihārā ariyavihārā ānaiñ‍javihārāti cattārau vihārā| taisu rūpāvacarasamāpattiyau dibbavihārā, catassau appamañ‍ñāyau brahmavihārā, catassau phalasamāpattiyau ariyavihārā, catassau arūpasamāpattiyau ānaiñ‍javihārā cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammāti mānapahānaṃ ālayasamugghātau avij‍jāpahānaṃ bhavūpasamauti cattārau acchariyaabbhutadhammā| cattāri adhiṭṭhānānīti sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ upasamādhiṭṭhānanti cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni| catassau samādhibhāvanāti chandasamādhibhāvanā vīriyasamādhibhāvanā cittasamādhibhāvanā vīmaṃsāsamādhibhāvanāti catassau samādhibhāvanā| cattārau  sukhabhāgiyāti indriyasaṃvarau tapau baujjhaṅgau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggauti cattārau sukhabhāgiyā| avasaisā pākaṭā|

‘‘catūsu catūsu paṭipadādīsu katamau paṭipadādikau paṭhamau satipaṭṭhānādikau bhavatī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘paṭhamā paṭipadā paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhāna’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘paṭipadādayau pañ‍ñāpadaṭṭhānādikā, satipaṭṭhānādayau pana satipadaṭṭhānādikā, tasmā ‘paṭhamā paṭipadā paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhāna’ntiādiyaujanā kātabbā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘paṭhamā paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ pūraitī’’tiādi vuttaṃ, pūrakapūraitabbabhāvatau tathā yaujanā kātabbāti adhippāyau| atha  vā ‘‘bhāvitā bahulīkatā katamai paṭipadādayau katamai satipaṭṭhānādikai pūraintī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘paṭhamā paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ pūraitī’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

87. ‘‘taisu dasasu paṭipadācatuk‍kādīsu katamai dhammā katamā disā, katamai dhammā katamā disā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha imā catassau disā paṭhamā paṭipadā, paṭhamau satipaṭṭhānau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu dasasu paṭipadācatuk‍kādīsu| imā mayā vuc‍camānā paṭhamā paṭipadādayau disā catassau disā bhavanti|

‘‘taisu catūsu disābhūtaisu paṭipadācatuk‍kādīsu katamau disābhūtau atthau katamassa puggalassa bhaisaj‍ja’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha paṭhamā paṭipadā…pai॰… diṭṭhicaritassa udattassa bhaisaj‍ja’’nti vuttaṃ|

‘‘taisu dasasu paṭipadādicatuk‍kaisu dhammaisu katamau katamau katamaṃ katamaṃ vimaukkhamukha’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha dukkhā ca paṭipadā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| atthau pana ṭīkāyaṃ vitthāraina vuttau pākaṭau| saṃkilaisapakkhai disābhūtā āhāracatuk‍kādayau dasa catuk‍kā samatik‍kamitabbapahātabbabhāvaina niddhāritā, vaudānapakkhai pana disābhūtā paṭipadācatuk‍kādayau dasa catuk‍kā samatik‍kamapahāyakabhāvaina niddhāritā|

‘‘taisaṃ āhāracatuk‍kādīnaṃ dasan‍naṃ catuk‍kānaṃ samatik‍kamanapahānasaṅkhātaṃ yaṃ vik‍kīḷitañ‍ca taisaṃ paṭipadācatuk‍kādīnaṃ dasan‍naṃ catuk‍kānaṃ bhāvanāsaṅkhātaṃ yaṃ vik‍kīḷitañ‍ca sacchikiriyāsaṅkhātaṃ yaṃ vik‍kīḷitañ‍ca atthi, taṃ tividhaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ katamaisaṃ puggalānaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ  bhavatī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘taisaṃ vik‍kīḷita’’nti vuttaṃ| atha vā ‘‘yathāvuttappakāraihi vimaukkhamukhaihi yai buddhapac‍caikabuddhasāvakā vimuc‍ciṃsu, taisu buddhassaiva vik‍kīḷitaṃ bhavati kiṃ, pac‍caikabuddhassaiva vik‍kīḷitaṃ bhavati kiṃ, sāvakassaiva vik‍kīḷitaṃ bhavati kiṃ, udāhu sabbaisaṃ buddhapac‍caikabuddhasāvakānaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ bhavati ki’’nti vicāraṇāya sambhavatau ‘‘taisaṃ vik‍kīḷita’’nti vuttaṃ| yathāvuttappakāraihi vimaukkhamukhaihi yai buddhapac‍caikabuddhasāvakā muc‍ciṃsu, taisaṃ buddhapac‍caikabuddhasāvakānaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ| yaṃ āhāracatuk‍kādīnaṃ dasan‍naṃ catuk‍kānaṃ  saparasantānai samatik‍kamanapahānañ‍ca yā paṭipadācatuk‍kādīnaṃ dasan‍naṃ catuk‍kānaṃ saparasantānai bhāvanāsampādanā, sacchikiriyāsampādanā ca atthi, idaṃ sabbaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ nāma bhavatīti atthau|

‘‘sabbaisaṃ āhāracatuk‍kādīnaṃ dasan‍naṃ catuk‍kānaṃ sabbai paṭipadācatuk‍kādayau dasa catuk‍kā paṭipakkhā haunti kiṃ, udāhu yathāk‍kamaṃ catuk‍kānaṃ catuk‍kā paṭipakkhā haunti ki’’nti vicāraṇāya sambhavatau yathāk‍kamaṃ catuk‍kānaṃ catuk‍kā paṭipakkhā haunti pahātabbapahāyakabhāvaināti dassaintau ‘‘cattārau āhārā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau catassau paṭipadā’’tiādimāha| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana – ‘‘idāni āhārādīnaṃ paṭipadādīhi yaina samatik‍kamanaṃ, taṃ naisaṃ paṭipakkhabhāvaṃ dassaintau ‘cattārau āhārā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau catassau paṭipadā’tiādimāhā’’ti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 87) vuttaṃ| tattha saṃkilaisapakkhai cattārau yai āhārā niddhāritā, taisaṃ catun‍naṃ āhārānaṃ vaudānapakkhai yā catassau paṭipadā niddhāritā, tā catassau paṭipadā paṭipakkhau āhārānaṃ pahātabbattā, paṭipadānaṃ pana pahāyakattā| satipi āhārānaṃ appahātabbabhāvai vipassanārammaṇattā āhārapaṭibaddhachandarāgappahānavasaina pahātabbabhāvau vuttauti aivamādiyaujanā kātabbā|

sīhāti sīhasadisā buddhā ca sīhasadisā pac‍caikabuddhā ca sīhasadisā sāvakā cāti sīhasadisā visuṃ visuṃ yaujaitabbā| sāvakā pana ahatarāgadausamauhāpi santi, taipi ‘‘sīhā’’ti mañ‍ñaiyyunti taṃ nivattāpanatthaṃ ‘‘hatarāgadausamauhā’’ti vuttaṃ| hanitabbāti hatā, rāgau ca dausau ca mauhau ca rāgadausamauhā, hatā rāgadausamauhā aitaihi sāvakaihīti hatarāgadausamauhā, sāvakāti yaujanā kātabbā sāvakānaṃyaiva byabhicārasambhavatauti| taisaṃ sīhānaṃ buddhānaṃ, taisaṃ sīhānaṃ pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ, taisaṃ sīhānaṃ sāvakānaṃ bhāvanā vaudānapakkhai bhāvitabbānaṃ baudhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ bhāvanā vaḍḍhanā ca, sacchikiriyā vaudānapakkhaiyaiva sacchikātabbānaṃ  phalanibbānānaṃ sacchikiriyā ca, byantīkiriyā saṃkilaisapakkhai pahātabbānaṃ pahānasaṅkhātā byantīkiriyā ca vik‍kīḷitaṃ nāma bhavati|

indriyādhiṭṭhānanti  saddhindriyādīnaṃ indriyānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ pavattanaṃ bhāvanā, sacchikiriyā ca| vipariyāsānadhiṭṭhānanti vipal‍lāsānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ pahānavasaina appavattanaṃ, anuppādanañ‍ca saṅkhaipatau vik‍kīḷitaṃ nāmāti gahitaṃ| indriyāni saddhindriyādīni saddhammagaucarau saddhammassa vaudānapakkhassa gaucarau pavattanahaitūti adhippaitāni| vipariyāsā vipal‍lāsā kilaisagaucarau saṃkilaisapakkhassa gaucarau pavattihaitūti adhippaitā|

‘‘cattārau āhārātiādinā saṃkilaisapakkhai āhāracatuk‍kādīnaṃ dasan‍naṃ catuk‍kānaṃ taṇhācaritādīnaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ upak‍kilaisavibhāvanāmukhaina yā niddhāraṇā ācariyaina katā, ‘catassau paṭipadā’tiādinā ca vaudānapakkhai paṭipadācatuk‍kādīnaṃ dasan‍naṃ catuk‍kānaṃ taṇhācaritādīnaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ vaudānavibhāvanāmukhaina yā niddhāraṇā ācariyaina katā, sā ayaṃ niddhāraṇā katamassa nayassa samuṭṭhānaṃ bhūmīti vuc‍catī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘ayaṃ vuc‍cati sīhavik‍kīḷitassa nayassa bhūmī’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha ayanti yā ayaṃ vuttanayaina dvippakārā niddhāraṇā katā, sā ayaṃ dvippakārā niddhāraṇā sīhavik‍kīḷitassa nayassa bhūmi pavattiṭṭhānaṃ samuṭṭhānanti nayasamuṭṭhānakausal‍laihi puggalaihi vuc‍catīti yaujanā kātabbāti|

‘‘vuttappakārāya niddhāritāya nayassa bhūmibhāvau kasmā viñ‍ñāyatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taināhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taina vuttappakārāya niddhāraṇāya nayabhūmibhāvaina ‘‘yau naiti…pai॰… kusalāti cā’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ ācariyau āha, taina vacanaina viñ‍ñāyatīti|

sīhavik‍kīḷitanayabhūmi ācariyaina vibhāvitā, amhaihi ca ñātā, ‘‘katamā tipukkhalanayabhūmī’’ti pucchitabbattā tipukkhalanayabhūmiṃ vibhāvaitukāmau ‘‘tattha yai dukkhāya paṭipadāyā’’tiādimāha| aivaṃ sati ‘‘ayaṃ saṃkilaisau, tīṇi akusalamūlānī’’tiādivacanamaiva ācariyaina vattabbaṃ, kasmā pana ‘‘tattha yai dukkhāya paṭipadāyā’’tiādivacanaṃ vattabbanti? sac‍caṃ, tipukkhalanayabhūmibhāvanā pana ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādipuggalattayavasaina pavattā, tasmā ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādipuggalattayaṃ vibhāvaituṃ ‘‘tattha yai dukkhāya paṭipadāyā’’tiādivacanaṃ vuttaṃ| aivamapi ‘‘tattha yau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya  niyyāti, ayaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñū’’tiādivacanamaiva vattabbaṃ, kasmā pana ‘‘tattha yai dukkhāya paṭipadāyā’’tiādivacanaṃ vattabbanti? sac‍caṃ, ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādipuggalattayaṃ pana sīhavik‍kīḷitanayatau tipukkhalanayassa niggacchanatau nikkhamanatau sīhavik‍kīḷitanayabhūmivibhāvanāyaṃ paṭipadāvibhāgatau vibhāvitapuggalacatuk‍katau niddhāritaṃ, sīhavik‍kīḷitanayabhūmivibhāvanāyaṃ paṭipadāvibhāgatau vibhāvitaṃ puggalacatuk‍kaṃ paṭhamaṃ vibhāvaituṃ ‘‘tattha yai dukkhāya paṭipadāyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti yau paṭipadācatuk‍kau sīhavik‍kīḷitanayabhūmivibhāvanāyaṃ niddhāritau, tasmiṃ paṭipadācatuk‍kai| yaiti yai dandhaudattā diṭṭhicaritapuggalā yathāk‍kamaṃ sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya ca sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti, iti niyyakā dvai puggalā ca niddhāritā| ‘‘taisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ katamau saṃkilaisau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘taisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau’’ti vissaj‍jaituṃ ‘‘taisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| cattārau āhārā niddhāritā…pai॰… cattāri agatigamanāni niddhāritāni, iti ayaṃ dasavidhau āhāracatuk‍kādicatuk‍kau taisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ saṃkilaisau hauti| taisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ saṃkilaisau ācariyaina niddhāritau, ‘‘katamaṃ vaudāna’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘taisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ idaṃ vaudāna’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| catassau paṭipadā niddhāritā, catassau appamāṇā niddhāritā, iti idaṃ dasavidhaṃ paṭipadācatuk‍kādicatuk‍kabhūtaṃ dhammajātaṃ taisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ vaudānaṃ hauti|

88. ‘‘taisu catūsu puggalaisu katamau puggalau ugghaṭitañ‍ñū, katamau puggalau vipañ‍citañ‍ñū, katamau puggalau naiyyau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘pubbaipi ‘tattha yai’tiādinā cattārau puggalā niddhāritā, kasmā pana puna ‘‘tattha yai’tiādinā cattārau puggalā niddhāritā’’ti cai vadaiyya? pubbai saṃkilaisavaudānaṃ sāmibhāvaina niddhāritā, pacchā pana ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādīnaṃ avayavānaṃ samūhabhāvaina niddhāritāti visaisatthau gahaitabbau| tattha yai…pai॰… imai dvai puggalāti aittha yaujanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttasadisauva| tatthāti taisu catūsu puggalaisu| yauti udattau diṭṭhicaritau| ayanti ayaṃ niyatau udattau diṭṭhicaritau| puna yauti udattauva taṇhācaritau ca mandau diṭṭhicaritau ca| sādhāraṇāyāti dukkhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya ca|

‘‘ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādayau  tayau puggalā ācariyaina niddhāritā, taisu tīsu puggalaisu katamassa katamassa katamaṃ katamaṃ bhagavā upadisatī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha bhagavā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| atha vā ‘‘paṭipadābhaidaina puggalabhaidau ācariyaina vibhāvitau, kathaṃ daisanābhaidaina  puggalabhaidau vibhāvitau’’ti vattabbattā daisanābhaidainapi puggalabhaidaṃ vibhāvaituṃ ‘‘tattha bhagavā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu tīsu ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādīsu puggalaisu| ‘‘samathadaisanāvipassanādaisanābhaidainaiva puggalabhaidau vibhāvitau’’ti vattabbattā mududhammadaisanātikkhadhammadaisanābhaidainapi puggalabhaidaṃ vibhāvaituṃ ‘‘tattha bhagavā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| saisaisupi aivamaiva anusandhyatthau vattabbau|

visuṃ visuṃ paṭipadābhaidaina cattārau hutvā visuṃ ca sampiṇḍitā ca paṭipadābhaidaina caiva daisanābhaidaina ca tayau hauntīti vibhāvaituṃ ‘‘tattha yai’’tiādiṃ puna vatvā ‘‘iti khau cattāri hutvā tīṇi bhavantī’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha cattāri tīṇīti liṅgavipal‍lāsaniddaisau, ‘‘cattārau tayau’’ti pana pakatiliṅganiddaisau kātabbauva|

‘‘taisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ katamau saṃkilaisau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘taisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha ayaṃ saṃkilaisauti ‘‘tīṇi akusalamūlāni…pai॰… sīlavipatti diṭṭhivipatti ācāravipattī’’ti niddhāritānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ iti ayaṃ samūhau saṃkilaisau hauti| ‘‘taisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ saṃkilaisau ācariyaina niddhāritau, kathaṃ vaudānaṃ niddhāritabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘taisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ idaṃ vaudāna’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha idaṃ vaudānanti ‘‘tīṇi kusalamūlāni…pai॰… tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni suñ‍ñataṃ animittaṃ appaṇihita’’nti niddhāritānaṃ kusaladhammānaṃ samūhabhūtaṃ iti idaṃ dhammajātaṃ vaudānaṃ hauti|

‘‘vuttappakāraina cattārau hutvā tayau puggalā bhavantīti ācariyaina vibhāvitā, tayau hutvā kittakā puggalā bhavantī’’ti vattabbabhāvatau ‘‘iti khau cattāri hutvā tīṇi bhavanti, tīṇi hutvā dvai bhavanti taṇhācaritau ca diṭṭhicaritau cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| itīti haiṭṭhā vuttappakāraina cattāri cattārau hutvā tīṇi tayau bhavanti| tīṇi tayau hutvā taṇhācaritau ca diṭṭhicaritau cāti dvai puggalā bhavanti|

‘‘taisaṃ  dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ katamau saṃkilaisau’’ti vattabbabhāvatau ‘‘taisaṃ dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha ayaṃ saṃkilaisauti ‘‘taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca…pai॰… sassatadiṭṭhi ca ucchaidadiṭṭhi cā’’ti niddhāritānaṃ akusaladhammānaṃ iti ayaṃ samūhau saṃkilaisau hauti|

‘‘taisaṃ  dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ saṃkilaisau ācariyaina niddhāritau, kathaṃ vaudāna’’nti vattabbabhāvatau ‘‘taisaṃ dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ idaṃ vaudāna’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ vaudānanti ‘‘samathau ca vipassanā ca…pai॰… saupādisaisā ca nibbānadhātu, anupādisaisā ca nibbānadhātū’’ti niddhāritānaṃ kusaladhammānaṃ samūhabhūtaṃ iti idaṃ dhammajātaṃ vaudānaṃ hauti|

‘‘tīṇi akusalamūlānī’’tiādinā saṃkilaisapakkhai akusalamūlatikādīnaṃ dvin‍naṃ dvādasan‍naṃ tikānaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādipuggalānaṃ saṃkilaisavibhāvanāmukhaina yā niddhāraṇā katā, ‘‘tīṇi kusalamūlānī’’tiādinā vaudānapakkhai kusalamūlatikādīnaṃ dvin‍naṃ dvādasan‍naṃ tikānaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādipuggalānaṃ vaudānavibhāvanāmukhaina yā niddhāraṇā katā, ayaṃ vuttappakāraniddhāraṇā tipukkhalassa ca nayassa, aṅkusassa ca nayassa bhūmi samuṭṭhānaṃ pavattihaitu nāmāti yaujaitvā ‘‘taṇhā ca avij‍jā cā’’tiādinā saṃkilaisapakkhai taṇhāavij‍jādukādīnaṃ pan‍narasan‍naṃ dukānaṃ, dvin‍naṃ taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritānaṃ puggalānaṃ vaudānavibhāvanāmukhaina yā niddhāraṇā katā, ‘‘samathau ca vipassanā cā’’tiādinā vaudānapakkhai samathavipassanādukādīnaṃ aikūnavīsatidukānaṃ, dvin‍naṃ taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritānaṃ puggalānaṃ vaudānavibhāvanāmukhaina yā niddhāraṇā katā, ayaṃ vuttappakārā nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa bhūmītipi nīharitvā yaujaitabbā| puggalādhiṭṭhānavasaina hi nandiyāvaṭṭanayatau sīhavik‍kīḷitanayassa sambhavau, sīhavik‍kīḷitanayatau ca tipukkhalanayassa sambhavau hauti| dhammādhiṭṭhānavasaina pana sīhavik‍kīḷitanayatau tipukkhalanayassa sambhavau, tipukkhalanayatau ca nandiyāvaṭṭanayassa sambhavau hauti| taināha aṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘antai ‘taṇhā ca avij‍jā cā’tiādinā samathassa nayassa bhūmi dassitā| tainaiva hi ‘cattāri hutvā tīṇi bhavanti, tīṇi hutvā dvai bhavantī’ti vutta’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 88)|

‘‘kasmā  pana ayaṃ vuttappakārāya niddhāraṇāya tipukkhalassa ca nayassa, aṅkusassa ca nayassa bhūmibhāvau viñ‍ñāyatī’’ti vattabbabhāvatau ‘‘taināhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taina yathāvuttassa niddhāraṇāya bhūmibhāvaina ācariyau ‘‘yau akusalai…pai॰… disālaucanaināti cā’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, taina vacanaina vuttappakārāya niddhāraṇāya tipukkhala…pai॰… yassa bhūmibhāvau viñ‍ñāyatīti atthau|

‘‘aittāvatā nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ hauti, añ‍ñaṃ natthī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttaṃ nayasamuṭṭhāna’’nti vuttaṃ| yaina yaina nayasamuṭṭhānaina saṃkilaisapakkhai vā akusalā dhammā niddhāritā, vaudānapakkhai vā kusalā dhammā niddhāritā, taṃ taṃ nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ niyuttaṃ yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuj‍jitabbanti atthau gahaitabbau|

nayak‍kamaina pana saṅkhaipatau dassayissāmi – dvai puggalā, tayau puggalā, cattārau puggalāti puggalā tikauṭṭhāsā bhavanti, catudisā, chadisā, aṭṭhadisāti disāpi tikauṭṭhāsā bhavanti| tattha dvai puggalāti taṇhācaritau puggalau, diṭṭhicaritau puggalauti dvai puggalā bhavanti| tayau puggalāti ugghaṭitañ‍ñupuggalau, vipañ‍citañ‍ñupuggalau, naiyyapuggalauti tayau puggalā bhavanti| cattārau puggalāti dukkhāpaṭipadādandhābhiñ‍ñādibhaidaina bhin‍nā mudindriyau taṇhācaritau puggalau, mudindriyau diṭṭhicaritau puggalau, tikkhindriyau taṇhācaritau puggalau, tikkhindriyau diṭṭhicaritau puggalauti cattārau puggalā bhavanti| catudisāti saṃkilaisapakkhai dvai dvai disā, vaudānapakkhai dvai dvai disāti catudisā bhavanti| chadisāti saṃkilaisapakkhai tissau tissau disā, vaudānapakkhai tissau tissau disāti chadisā bhavanti| aṭṭhadisāti saṃkilaisapakkhai catassau catassau disā, vaudānapakkhai catassau catassau disāti aṭṭhadisā bhavanti| taisu dvai dvai taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritai puggalai, catudisā ca nissāya nandiyāvaṭṭanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati| tayau ugghaṭitañ‍ñuvipañ‍citañ‍ñunaiyyapuggalai ca chadisā ca nissāya tipukkhalanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati| dukkhāpaṭipadādandhābhiñ‍ñādibhaidaina bhin‍nai cattārau mudindriyataṇhācaritatikkhindriyataṇhācaritamudindriyadiṭṭhicaritatikkhindriyadiṭṭhicaritai puggalai ca aṭṭhadisā ca nissāya sīhavik‍kīḷitanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati| aikaikasmiṃ nayasamuṭṭhānai vibhajitai disālaucanaaṅkusanayasamuṭṭhānānipi vibhajitāni bhavanti|

‘‘kathaṃ nandiyāvaṭṭanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavatī’’ti cai vadaiyya? ‘‘taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca ahirikañ‍ca anauttappañ‍ca assati ca asampajañ‍ñañ‍ca ayaunisaumanasikārau ca kausaj‍jañ‍ca dauvacassañ‍ca ahaṃkārau ca mamaṃkārau ca assaddhā ca pamādau ca asaddhammassavanañ‍ca asaṃvarau ca abhijjhā ca byāpādau ca nīvaraṇañ‍ca saṃyaujanañ‍ca kaudhau ca upanāhau ca makkhau ca paḷāsau ca issā ca macchairañ‍ca māyā ca sāṭhaiyyañ‍ca sassatadiṭṭhi ca ucchaidadiṭṭhi cā’’ti (naitti॰ 88) dukadukavasaina daisitau ayaṃ disābhūtau akusaladhammasamūhau dvin‍naṃ taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritānaṃ puggalānaṃ saṃkilaisau hautīti saṃkilaisapakkhai saṃkilaisasāmañ‍ñabhāvaina yaujaitvā ‘‘imaisu pan‍narasasu dukadukavasaina daisitaisu disābhūtaisu akusaladhammaisu katamau akusaladhammau katamassa puggalassa disā’’ti manasāva disādhammabhāvaina aulaukaitvā ‘‘ayaṃ ayaṃ paṭhamau paṭhamau akusaladhammau taṇhācaritassa puggalassa saṃkilaisapakkhai  paṭhamā disā nāma, ayaṃ ayaṃ dutiyau dutiyau akusaladhammau diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa saṃkilaisapakkhai dutiyā disā nāmā’’ti visuṃ visuṃ yaujaitvā samudayasac‍cadukkhasac‍cāni yathārahaṃ nīharitvā vibhajitabbadhammasabhāvau ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitau, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca nandiyāvaṭṭanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati, tassa aulaukanā ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitā, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca disālaucananayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati, tathā aulaukaitvā disāvisaisabhūtassa dhammavisaisassa puggalānaṃ nayanañ‍ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina nayati, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca aṅkusanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati|

‘‘samathau ca vipassanā ca, hirī ca auttappañ‍ca, sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, yaunisaumanasikārau ca vīriyārambhau ca, sauvacassañ‍ca dhammai ñāṇañ‍ca anvayai ñāṇañ‍ca, khayai ñāṇañ‍ca anuppādai ñāṇañ‍ca, saddhā ca appamādau ca, saddhammassavanañ‍ca saṃvarau ca, anabhijjhā ca abyāpādau ca, rāgavirāgā ca caitauvimutti, avij‍jāvirāgā ca pañ‍ñāvimutti, abhisamayau ca appicchatā ca, santuṭṭhi ca ak‍kaudhau ca, anupanāhau ca amakkhau ca, apaḷāsau  ca issāpahānañ‍ca macchariyappahānañ‍ca vij‍jā ca, vimutti ca saṅkhatārammaṇau ca vimaukkhau, asaṅkhatārammaṇau ca vimaukkhau, saupādisaisā ca nibbānadhātu, anupādisaisā ca nibbānadhātū’’ti (naitti॰ 88) tikadukavasaina daisitaṃ idaṃ disābhūtaṃ kusalasamūhadhammajātaṃ dvin‍naṃ taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritānaṃ puggalānaṃ vaudānaṃ hautīti vaudānapakkhai vaudānasāmañ‍ñabhāvaina yaujaitvā ‘‘imaisu aikūnavīsatiyā dukadukavasaina vā daisitaisu disābhūtaisu dhammaisu katamau katamau kusaladhammau katamassa katamassa puggalassa disā’’ti manasāva disādhammabhāvaina aulaukaitvā ‘‘ayaṃ paṭhamau paṭhamau kusaladhammau taṇhācaritassa puggalassa vaudānapakkhai paṭhamā disā nāma, ayaṃ dutiyau dutiyau kusaladhammau diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa vaudānapakkhai dutiyā disā nāmā’’ti visuṃ visuṃ yaujaitvā maggasac‍caniraudhasac‍cāni yathārahaṃ nīharitvā vibhajitabbadhammabhāvau ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitau, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca nandiyāvaṭṭanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati, tassa aulaukanā ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitā, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca disālaucananayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati, tathā aulaukaitvā disāvisaisabhūtassa dhammavisaisassa puggalānaṃ nayanañ‍ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina nayati, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca aṅkusanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati|

‘‘kathaṃ tipukkhalanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavatī’’ti cai pucchaiyya, ‘‘tīṇi akusalamūlāni – laubhau akusalamūlaṃ, dausau akusalamūlaṃ, mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| tīṇi duc‍caritāni – kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ| tayau akusalavitak‍kā – kāmavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tissau akusalasañ‍ñā – kāmasañ‍ñā, byāpādasañ‍ñā, vihiṃsāsañ‍ñā| tissau viparītasañ‍ñā – nic‍casañ‍ñā, sukhasañ‍ñā, attasañ‍ñā| tissau vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| tissau dukkhatā – dukkhadukkhatā, saṅkhāradukkhatā, vipariṇāmadukkhatā| tayau aggī – rāgaggi, dausaggi, mauhaggi| tayau sal‍lā – rāgasal‍lau, dausasal‍lau, mauhasal‍lau| tissau jaṭā – rāgajaṭā, dausajaṭā, mauhajaṭā| tissau akusalūpaparikkhā – akusalaṃ kāyakammaṃ, akusalaṃ vacīkammaṃ, akusalaṃ manaukammaṃ| tissau vipattiyau – sīlavipatti, diṭṭhivipatti, ācāravipattī’’ti (naitti॰ 88) tikavasaina daisitau ayaṃ disābhūtau akusaladhammasamūhau tiṇṇaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñuvipañ‍citañ‍ñunaiyyapuggalānaṃ saṃkilaisau hautīti saṃkilaisapakkhai saṃkilaisasāmañ‍ñabhāvaina yaujaitvā ‘‘imaisu dvādasasu tikatikavasaina daisitaisu disābhūtaisu akusaladhammaisu katamau katamau akusalau dhammau katamassa katamassa puggalassa disā’’ti manasāva disādhammabhāvaina aulaukaitvā ‘‘ayaṃ ayaṃ paṭhamau paṭhamau akusaladhammau ugghaṭitañ‍ñupuggalassa saṃkilaisapakkhai paṭhamā disā nāma| ayaṃ ayaṃ dutiyau dutiyau akusaladhammau vipañ‍citañ‍ñupuggalassa saṃkilaisapakkhai dutiyā disā nāma| ayaṃ ayaṃ tatiyau tatiyau akusaladhammau naiyyassa puggalassa saṃkilaisapakkhai tatiyā disā nāmā’’ti visuṃ visuṃ yaujaitvā samudayasac‍cadukkhasac‍cāni yathārahaṃ nīharitvā vibhajitabbadhammasabhāvau ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitau| sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca tipukkhalanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati| tassa aulaukanā ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitā, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca disālaucananayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati tathā aulaukaitvā disāvisaisabhūtassa dhammavisaisassa puggalānaṃ nayanañ‍ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina nayati, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau aṅkusanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati|

‘‘tīṇi kusalamūlāni – alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ, adausau kusalamūlaṃ, amauhau kusalamūlaṃ| tīṇi sucaritāni – kāyasucaritaṃ, vacīsucaritaṃ, manausucaritaṃ| tayau kusalavitak‍kā – naikkhammavitak‍kau, abyāpādavitak‍kau, avihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tayau samādhī – savitak‍kau savicārau samādhi, avitak‍kau vicāramattau samādhi, avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi| tissau kusalasañ‍ñā – naikkhammasañ‍ñā, abyāpādasañ‍ñā, avihiṃsāsañ‍ñā| tissau aviparītasañ‍ñā – anic‍casañ‍ñā, dukkhasañ‍ñā, anattasañ‍ñā| tissau kusalūpaparikkhā – kusalaṃ kāyakammaṃ, kusalaṃ vacīkammaṃ, kusalaṃ manaukammaṃ| tīṇi saucaiyyāni – kāyasaucaiyyaṃ, vacīsaucaiyyaṃ, manausaucaiyyaṃ| tissau sampattiyau – sīlasampatti, samādhisampatti, pañ‍ñāsampatti| tissau sikkhā – adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| tayau khandhā – sīlakkhandhau, samādhikkhandhau, pañ‍ñākkhandhau| tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni – suñ‍ñataṃ, animittaṃ, appaṇihita’’nti (naitti॰ 88) tikatikavasaina daisitaṃ idaṃ disābhūtaṃ kusalasamūhadhammajātaṃ tiṇṇaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñuvipañ‍citañ‍ñunaiyyapuggalānaṃ vaudānaṃ hautīti vaudānapakkhai vaudānasāmañ‍ñabhāvaina yaujaitvā ‘‘imaisu dvīsu dvādasasu tikatikavasaina daisitaisu disābhūtaisu kusaladhammaisu katamau katamau kusaladhammau katamassa katamassa puggalassa disā’’ti manasāva disādhammabhāvaina aulaukaitvā ‘‘ayaṃ ayaṃ paṭhamau paṭhamau kusaladhammau ugghaṭitañ‍ñupuggalassa vaudānapakkhai paṭhamā disā nāma| ayaṃ ayaṃ dutiyau dutiyau kusalau dhammau vipañ‍citañ‍ñupuggalassa vaudānapakkhai dutiyā disā nāma| ayaṃ ayaṃ tatiyau tatiyau kusaladhammau naiyyapuggalassa vaudānapakkhai tatiyā disā nāmā’’ti visuṃ visuṃ disābhāvaina yaujaitvā maggasac‍caniraudhasac‍cāni yathārahaṃ nīharitvā vibhajitabbadhammasabhāvau ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitau| sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca tipukkhalanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati| tassa aulaukanā ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitā, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca disālaucananayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati| tathā aulaukaitvā disāvisaisabhūtassa dhammassa puggalānaṃ nayanañ‍ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina nayati, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca aṅkusanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati|

‘‘kathaṃ sīhavik‍kīḷitanayasamuṭṭhāna’’nti cai pucchaiyya, ‘‘cattārau āhārā, cattārau vipal‍lāsā, cattāri upādānāni, cattārau yaugā, cattārau ganthā, cattārau āsavā, cattārau aughā, cattārau sal‍lā, catassau viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau, cattāri agatigamanānī’’ti (naitti॰ 87) catuk‍kacatuk‍kavasaina daisitau ayaṃ disābhūtau catuk‍kau catuk‍kau akusaladhammau ‘‘dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa mudindriyassa taṇhācaritassa ca dukkhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa tikkhindriyassa taṇhācaritassa ca sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa mudindriyassa diṭṭhicaritassa ca sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa tikkhindriyassa diṭṭhicaritassa cā’’ti catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ saṃkilaisauti saṃkilaisapakkhai saṃkilaisasāmañ‍ñabhāvaina yaujaitvā ‘‘imaisu catuk‍kacatuk‍kavasaina daisitaisu dasasu  catuk‍kaisu dhammaisu katamau katamau akusaladhammau katamassa katamassa puggalassa disā’’ti manasāva saṃkilaisapakkhai disādhammabhāvaina aulaukaitvā ‘‘ayaṃ ayaṃ paṭhamau paṭhamau akusaladhammau dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa mudindriyassa taṇhācaritassa puggalassa paṭhamā disā nāma| ayaṃ ayaṃ dutiyau dutiyau akusaladhammau dukkhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa tikkhindriyassa taṇhācaritassa puggalassa dutiyā disā nāma| ayaṃ  ayaṃ tatiyau tatiyau akusaladhammau sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa mudindriyassa diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa tatiyā disā nāma| ayaṃ ayaṃ catutthau catutthau akusaladhammau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa tikkhindriyassa diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa catutthā disā nāmā’’ti visuṃ visuṃ disābhāvaina yaujaitvā, samudayasac‍cadukkhasac‍cāni yathārahaṃ nīharitvā, vibhajitabbadhammasabhāvau ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitau| sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca sīhavik‍kīḷitanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati| tassa aulaukanā ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitā, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca disālaucananayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati| tathā aulaukaitvā disāvisaisabhūtassa dhammassa puggalānaṃ nayanañ‍ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina nayati, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca aṅkusanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati|

‘‘catassau paṭipadā, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattāri jhānāni, cattārau vihārā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni, catassau samādhibhāvanā, cattārau sukhabhāgiyā dhammā, catassau appamāṇā’’ti (naitti॰ 86) catuk‍kacatuk‍kavasaina daisitaṃ idaṃ disābhūtaṃ kusalasamūhadhammajātaṃ ‘‘dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa mudindriyassa taṇhācaritassa ca dukkhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa tikkhindriyassa taṇhācaritassa ca sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa mudindriyassa diṭṭhicaritassa sa sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa tikkhindriyassa diṭṭhicaritassa cā’’ti catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ vaudānaṃ hautīti vaudānapakkhai vaudānasāmañ‍ñabhāvaina yaujaitvā ‘‘imaisu catuk‍kacatuk‍kavasaina daisitaisu dasasu catuk‍kaisu kusaladhammaisu katamau  katamau kusaladhammau katamassa katamassa puggalassa disā’’ti manasāva disādhammabhāvaina aulaukaitvā, ‘‘ayaṃ ayaṃ paṭhamau paṭhamau kusaladhammau dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa mudindriyassa taṇhācaritassa puggalassa paṭhamā disā nāma| ayaṃ ayaṃ dutiyau dutiyau kusaladhammau dukkhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa tikkhindriyassa taṇhācaritassa puggalassa dutiyā disā nāma| ayaṃ ayaṃ tatiyau tatiyau kusaladhammau sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa mudindriyassa diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa tatiyā disā nāma| ayaṃ ayaṃ catutthau catutthau kusaladhammau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyakassa tikkhindriyassa diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa catutthā disā nāmā’’ti visuṃ visuṃ disābhāvaina yaujaitvā, maggasac‍caniraudhasac‍cāni yathārahaṃ nīharitvā, vibhajitabbadhammasabhāvau ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitau| sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca sīhavik‍kīḷitanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavati| tassa aulaukanā ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina dassitā| sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca disālaucananayasamuṭṭhānaṃ  bhavati| tathā aulaukaitvā disāvisaisassa dhammassa puggalānaṃ nayanañ‍ca yaina saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaina nayati, sau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau ca aṅkusanayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavatīti nayak‍kamaina saṅkhaipatau nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ bhavatīti viñ‍ñātabbanti|

iti nayasamuṭṭhānai sattibalānurūpā racitā

vibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

paṇḍitaihi pana aṭṭhakathāṭīkānusāraina gambhīratthau vitthāratau vibhajitvā gahaitabbauti|

sāsanapaṭṭhānavibhāvanā

89. ‘‘tattha katamaṃ nayasamuṭṭhāna’’ntiādinā ācariyaina sabbathā nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ ṭhapitaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘sauḷasahārapañ‍canayaaṭṭhārasamūlapadaisu aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kathaṃ vibhattā, kuhiṃ amhaihi daṭṭhabbā’’ti vattabbabhāvatau  ‘‘tattha aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu sauḷasahārapañ‍canayaaṭṭhārasamūlapadaisu aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kaina padaina ācariyaina vibhattā, kuhiṃ amhaihi vitthāratau daṭṭhabbāti pucchati| aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā sāsanapaṭṭhānai mayā vibhattā, tumhaihi ca vitthāratau sāsanapaṭṭhānai daṭṭhabbāti vissaj‍jaiti| vibhattāyaiva hi aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā daṭṭhabbā bhavanti| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 89) pana –

‘‘aivaṃ sabbathā nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ vibhajitvā idāni sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ vibhajantau yasmā saṅgahavārādīsu mūlapadaihaiva paṭṭhānaṃ saṅgahaitvā sarūpatau na dassitaṃ, tasmā yathā mūlapadaihi paṭṭhānaṃ niddhāraitabbaṃ, aivaṃ paṭṭhānataupi mūlapadāni niddhāraitabbānīti dassanatthaṃ ‘aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbā? sāsanapaṭṭhānai’ti āhā’’ti –

vuttaṃ| sāsanapaṭṭhānai aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā daṭṭhabbāti ācariyaina vuttā, ‘‘katamaṃ taṃ sāsanapaṭṭhāna’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhāna’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu aṭṭhārasamūlapadasāsanapaṭṭhānaisu katamaṃ taṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānanti idāni mayā niddhāriyamānaṃ bhagavatā daisitaṃ  saṃkilaisabhāgiyādisuttaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ nāmāti vissaj‍jaiti| sāsananti pariyattisāsanaṃ| paṭṭhānanti tassa pariyattisāsanassa saṃkilaisabhāgiyatādīhi pakāraihi ṭhānaṃ pavattanaṃ paṭṭhānaṃ, taṃdīpanasuttaṃ pana idha paṭṭhānaṃ nāma| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’ntiādi| atha vā sāsananti adhisīlaadhicittaadhipañ‍ñāsikkhattayaṃ sāsanaṃ nāma, taṃ sikkhattayaṃ patiṭṭhahati aitaina saṃkilaisādināti paṭṭhānaṃ, sikkhattayassa sāsanassa paṭṭhānanti sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ| tadādhārabhūtaṃ suttampi ṭhānyūpacāratau sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ nāma| taṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttaṃ sarūpatau dassaituṃ –

‘‘saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca  nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, taṇhāsaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, diṭṭhisaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, duc‍caritasaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, taṇhāvaudānabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, diṭṭhivaudānabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, duc‍caritavaudānabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’nti –

vuttaṃ| ‘‘taisu suttaisu yai saṃkilaisādayau bhagavatā vuttā, taisu saṃkilaisādīsu saṃkilaisau kittakau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha saṃkilaisau tividhau taṇhāsaṃkilaisau diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau’’ti vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu suttantaisu saṃkilaisādīsu dhammaisu| ‘‘tividhai tasmiṃ saṃkilaisai taṇhāsaṃkilaisau katamaina kusalaina visujjhatī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha taṇhāsaṃkilaisau samathaina visujjhatī’’ti vuttaṃ| tatthāti tasmiṃ tividhai taṇhāsaṃkilaisādikai| ‘‘sau samathau khandhaisu katamau khandhau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘sau samathau samādhikkhandhau’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau kaina visujjhatī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau vipassanāya visujjhatī’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘sā vipassanā katamau khandhau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘sā vipassanā pañ‍ñākkhandhau’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau kaina visujjhatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau sucaritaina visujjhatī’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘taṃ sucaritaṃ katamau khandhau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taṃ sucaritaṃ sīlakkhandhau’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘tasmiṃ sīlai ṭhitassa puggalassa kiṃ bhavatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tassa sīlai patiṭṭhitassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| sīlai sucaritasaṅkhātai sīlakkhandhai patiṭṭhitassa tassa sīlavantassa puggalassa bhavaisu kāmabhavarūpārūpabhavaisu āsatti bhavapatthanā yadi uppaj‍jati, aivaṃsāyanti aivaṃ assa ayanti padacchaidau| aivaṃ sati assa sīlai  patiṭṭhitassa ayaṃ āsatti bhavapatthanā samathavipassanābhāvanāmayapuñ‍ñakiriyavatthu ca bhavati, ca-saddaina dānamayasīlamayapuñ‍ñakiriyavatthu ca bhavatīti atthau saṅgahitau| tatrāti taisu kāmabhavarūpārūpabhavaisu upapattiyā saṃvattatīti|

‘‘saṃkilaisādayau yaihi suttaihi dassitā, tāni suttāni kittakānī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘imāni cattāri suttānī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| asādhāraṇāni saṃkilaisabhāgiyasuttavāsanābhāgiyasuttanibbaidhabhāgiyasuttaasaikkhabhāgiyasuttāni cattāri suttāni bhavanti, sādhāraṇāni  saṃkilaisabhāgiyavāsanābhāgiyasutta, saṃkilaisabhāgiyanibbaidhabhāgiyasutta, saṃkilaisabhāgiyaasaikkhabhāgiyasutta, vāsanābhāgiyanibbaidhabhāgiyasuttāni katāni missitāni cattāri bhavanti| iti aṭṭha suttāni bhavanti| tāniyaiva vuttappakārāni aṭṭha suttāni bhavanti| sādhāraṇāni vāsanābhāgiyaasaikkhabhāgiyasuttanibbaidhabhāgiyaasaikkhabhāgiyasutta- saṃkilaisabhāgiyavāsanābhāgiyanibbaidhabhāgiyasuttasaṃkilaisa- bhāgiyavāsanābhāgiyaasaikkhabhāgiyasutta- saṃkilaisabhāgiyanibbaidhabhāgiyaasaikkhabhāgiyasutta- vāsanābhāgiyanibbaidhabhāgiyaasaikkhabhāgiyasutta- saṃkilaisabhāgiyavāsanābhāgiyanibbaidhabhāgiyaasaikkhabhāgiyasutta- naivasaṃkilaisabhāgiyanavāsanā- bhāgiyananibbaidhabhāgiyanaasaikkhabhāgiyasuttāni katāni missitāni aṭṭha suttāni bhavantīti sauḷasa suttāni bhavanti| taisu sauḷasasuttaisu cattāri aikakāni suttāni ca cattāri dukāni suttāni ca dvai tikāni ca pāḷiyaṃ āgatāni, dvai dukāni suttāni ca dvai tikāni ca dvai catuk‍kāni suttāni ca aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 89) āgatāni|

‘‘yadi paṭṭhānanayaina vuttappakārāni sauḷasa suttāniyaiva vibhattāni, aivaṃ sati suttagaiyyādinavavidhaṃ sakalaṃ pariyattisāsanaṃ paṭṭhānanayaina avibhattaṃ bhavaiyyā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘imaihi sauḷasahi suttaihī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| paṭṭhānanayaina vibhattaihi sauḷasahi imaihi suttaihi navavidhaṃ sakalaṃ pariyattisuttaṃ paṭṭhānanayaina vibhattaṃyaiva hutvā bhin‍naṃ bhavati| saṃkilaisabhāgiyādipabhaidāya gāthāya gāthā anuminitabbā, saṃkilaisabhāgiyādipabhaidaina vaiyyākaraṇaina vaiyyākaraṇaṃ anuminitabbaṃ| saṃkilaisabhāgiyādipabhaidaina suttaina suttaṃ anuminitabbaṃ bhavatiyaivāti|

90. ‘‘taisu sauḷasasu saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīsu suttaisu katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu sauḷasasu saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīsu suttaisu katamaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāmāti pucchati|

‘‘kāmandhā  jālasañchan‍nā, taṇhāchadanachāditā|

pamattabandhanābaddhā, macchāva kumināmukhai|

jarāmaraṇamanvainti, vacchau khīrapakauva mātara’’nti|| –

idaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| yai sattā kāmandhā kāmaina andhā jālasañchan‍nā taṇhāchadanaina chāditā, pamattabandhanāya baddhā bandhitabbā, tai sattai jarāmaraṇaṃ anvaiti, yathā taṃ kumināmukhai yai macchā gahitā, tai macchai jarāmaraṇaṃ anvaiti iva, aivaṃ tai sattai jarāmaraṇaṃ anvaiti| khīrapakau vacchau mātaraṃ anvaiti iva, aivaṃ tai sattai jarāmaraṇaṃ anvaitīti yaujanā kātabbā| atha vā khīrapakau vacchau mātaraṃ anvaiti iva, kumināmukhai gahitā macchā jarāmaraṇaṃ anvainti iva ca, aivaṃ yai sattā kāmandhā pamattabandhanāya bandhitabbā, tai sattā jarāmaraṇaṃ anvaintīti yaujanā|

‘‘idaṃ suttaṃyaivā’’ti vattabbattā –

‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, agatigamanāni| katamāni cattāri? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri agatigamanāni| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau, athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘chandā dausā bhayā mauhā, yau dhammaṃ ativattati|

nihīyati tassa yasau, kāḷapakkhaiva candimā’’ti|| –

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttanti –

vuttaṃ| chandā chandahaitunā dausā dausahaitunā bhayā bhayahaitunā mauhā mauhahaitunā yau rājādikau yau vinayadharādikau vā dhammaṃ sappurisadhammaṃ ativattati atik‍kamitvā vattati, tassa rājādinau vā tassa vinayadharādinau vā yasau kitti ca parivārau ca bhaugau ca nihīyati| candimā kāḷapakkhai pabhāya nihīyati iva, aivaṃ nihīyatīti yaujanā|

‘‘aittakaṃyaivā’’ti  vattabbattā ‘‘manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā…pai॰… cak‍kaṃva vahatau padanti idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’nti vuttaṃ| atthau pākaṭau| aṭṭhakathāyampi vibhattau|

‘‘middhī yadā hauti mahagghasau ca, niddāyitā samparivattasāyī|

mahāvarāhauva nivāpapuṭṭhau, punappunaṃ gabbhamupaiti mandau’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| nivāpapuṭṭhau mahāvarāhau gāmasūkarau niddāyitā supanasīlau samparivattasāyī hauti iva, aivaṃ yau mandau sattau yadā mahagghasau hauti, sau mandau sattau middhī thinamiddhābhibhūtau hutvā niddāyitā muduphassasayanai muduhatthaihi parāmasitau samparivattasāyī punappunaṃ gabbhaṃ upaitīti yaujanā|

‘‘ayasāva malaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ, tatuṭṭhāya tamaiva khādati|

aivaṃ atidhaunacārinaṃ, sāni kammāni nayanti duggati’’nti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| ayasā ayatau samuṭṭhitaṃ jātamalaṃ tatuṭṭhāya tatau ayatau uṭṭhahitvā tamaiva ayaṃ khādati iva, aivaṃ atidhaunacārinaṃ atik‍kamitvā dhaunacāripuggalaṃ sāni sayaṃ katāni akusalakammāni duggatiṃ nayantīti yaujanā|

‘‘caurau yathā sandhimukhai gahītau, sakammunā hañ‍ñati bajjhatai ca|

aivaṃ ayaṃ paic‍ca pajā parattha, sakammunā hañ‍ñati bajjhatai cā’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| sandhimukhai rājapurisādīhi gahitau caurau sakammunā attanā kataina caurakammaina hañ‍ñati ca bajjhatai ca yathā, aivaṃ ayaṃ pāpakārinī pajā parattha paralaukai paic‍ca sakammunā sayaṃ kataina akusalakammunā satthādīhi hañ‍ñati ca addubandhanādīhi bajjhatai cāti yaujanā|

‘‘sukhakāmāni  bhūtāni, yau daṇḍaina vihiṃsati|

attanau sukhamaisānau, paic‍ca sau na labhatai sukha’’nti|| –

idaṃ  suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| attanau sukhaṃ aisānau aisamānau yau sattau añ‍ñāni sukhakāmāni bhūtāni daṇḍaina vihiṃsati, sau hiṃsakau sattau paralaukai paic‍ca sukhaṃ na labhatīti yaujanā|

‘‘gun‍naṃ cai taramānānaṃ, jimhaṃ gacchati puṅgavau|

sabbā tā jimhaṃ gacchanti, naittai jimhaṃ gatai sati||

‘‘aivamaiva manussaisu, yau hauti saiṭṭhasammatau|

sau cai adhammaṃ carati, pagaiva itarā pajā|

sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti adhammikau’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| nadiṃ taramānānaṃ gun‍naṃ puṅgavau jimhaṃ cai gacchati, aivaṃ naittai jimhaṃ gatai sati sabbā tā gāviyau jimhaṃ gacchanti yathā, aivamaiva manussaisu yau rājā saiṭṭhasammatau, sau rājā adhammaṃ carati, aivaṃ rañ‍ñai adhammaṃ carantai sati itarā pajā pagaiva paṭhamamaiva adhammaṃ carati| rājā adhammikau cai hauti, aivaṃ rañ‍ñai adhammikai sati sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṃ saitīti yaujanā|

‘‘sukiccharūpā vatimai manussā, karaunti pāpaṃ upadhīsu rattā|

gacchanti tai bahujanasan‍nivāsaṃ, nirayaṃ avīciṃ kaṭukaṃ bhayānaka’’nti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| yai manussā upadhīsu kāmaguṇūpadhīsu rattā rāgābhibhūtā hutvā pāpaṃ akusalakammaṃ karaunti, imai pāpakammakarā manussā sukiccharūpā vata suṭṭhu kicchāpan‍narūpā vata bhavanti, tai pāpakammakarā manussā kaṭukaṃ bhayānakaṃ bahujanasan‍nivāsaṃ nirayaṃ avīciṃ gacchantīti yaujanā|

‘‘phalaṃ vai kadaliṃ hanti, phalaṃ vaiḷuṃ phalaṃ naḷaṃ|

sak‍kārau kāpurisaṃ hanti, gabbhau assatariṃ yathā’’ti|| –

idaṃ  suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| phalaṃ kadaliyā phalaṃ kadaliṃ vai aikantaina hanti yathā, phalaṃ vaiḷuṃ vai aikantaina hanti yathā, phalaṃ naḷaṃ vai aikantaina hanti yathā, gabbhau assatariṃ  mātaraṃ vai aikantaina hanti yathā, aivaṃ sak‍kārau kāpurisaṃ vai aikantaina hantīti yaujanā|

‘‘kaudhamakkhagaru bhikkhu, lābhasak‍kāragāravau|

sukhaittai pūtibījaṃva, saddhammai na virūhatī’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| sukhaittai sundarai khaittaipi khittaṃ pūtibījaṃ na ruhati iva, aivaṃ yau bhikkhu lābhasak‍kāragāravau kaudhaṃ kujjhanalakkhaṇaṃ kaudhaṃ, makkhaṃ paraguṇasīlamakkhanalakkhaṇaṃ makkhañ‍ca garuṃ katvā carati, sau carantau bhikkhu saddhammasmiṃ na ruhatīti yaujanā|

91. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ paduṭṭhacittaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi…pai॰… iti mai suta’’nti idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| bhikkhavai idha sāsanai, laukai vā ahaṃ aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ paduṭṭhacittaṃ mama caitasā tassa caitau cittaṃ paric‍ca paricchinditvā buddhacakkhunā aivaṃ pajānāmi| ‘‘kathaṃ pajānāmī’’ti cai pucchaiyya, yañ‍ca paṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau, yañ‍ca maggaṃ samāruḷhau ayaṃ puggalau yathā yāya duppaṭipadāya yaina dummaggaina iriyati pavattati, tāya duppaṭipadāya taina dummaggaina imamhi imasmiṃ samayai duppaṭipaj‍janakālai ayaṃ duppaṭipan‍naṃ paṭipan‍nau dummaggasamāruḷhau puggalau cai kālaṃ karaiyya, aivaṃ sati ābhataṃ vatthu nikkhittaṃ yathā, aivaṃ nirayai nikkhittau| taṃ kissa haitu? bhikkhavai assa puggalassa cittaṃ paduṭṭhaṃ padausitaṃ hi yasmā hauti, tasmā nikkhittau| aivaṃ idha sāsanai, laukai vā caitaupadausahaitu ca pana aikac‍cai sattā puggalā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ upapaj‍jantīti pajānāmīti| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca| tattha tasmiṃ suttai aitaṃ ‘‘paduṭṭhacittaṃ ñatvāna…pai॰… nirayaṃ sau upapaj‍jatī’’ti gāthāvacanaṃ iti aivaṃ vuc‍cati|

satthā idha sāsanai, laukai vā paduṭṭhacittaṃ aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ ñatvāna bhikkhūnaṃ santikai aitamatthaṃ byākāsi| imamhi imasmiṃ samayai ayaṃ puggalau cai kālaṃ kayirātha, aivaṃ sati paduṭṭhacittasamaṅgī hi nirayasmiṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, puggalassa cittaṃ padūsitaṃ hi yasmā hauti, tasmā upapaj‍jaiyya, caitaupadausahaitu  sattā duggatiṃ gacchanti ābhataṃ vatthuṃ nikkhipaiyya yathā, aivamaivaṃ  tathāvidhau duppañ‍ñau sau padausacittau puggalau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatīti ayampi atthau bhagavatā vuttau, iti mai mayā sutanti yaujanā|

‘‘sacai bhāyatha dukkhassa, sacai vau dukkhamappiyaṃ|

mākattha pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, āvi vā yadi vā rahau||

‘‘sacai ca pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, karissatha karautha vā|

na vau dukkhā pamutyatthi, upaic‍capi palāyata’’nti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| sappurisā tumhai dukkhassa jātidukkhajarādukkhabyādhidukkhamaraṇadukkhaapāyadukkha- atītavaṭṭamūlakadukkhaanāgatavaṭṭamūlakadukkhapac‍cuppan‍nāhāramūlakadukkhāti aṭṭhavidhassa dukkhassa sacai bhāyatha, vau tumhaihi dukkhaṃ tathā aṭṭhavidhaṃ dukkhaṃ sacai appiyaṃ, aivaṃ sati āvi vā yadi rahau vā pāpakaṃ kammaṃ mākattha mā akattha| sappurisā tumhai āvi vā yadi rahau vā pāpakaṃ kammaṃ sacai karissatha vā sacai karautha vā, aivaṃ sati upaic‍capi sañ‍cic‍cāpi palāyataṃ palāyantānaṃ vau tumhākaṃ dukkhā aṭṭhavidhā dukkhatau pamutti muc‍canaṃ natthaivāti yaujanā|

‘‘adhammaina dhanaṃ laddhā, musāvādaina cūbhayaṃ|

mamaiti bālā mañ‍ñanti, taṃ kathaṃ nu bhavissati||

‘‘antarāyā su bhavissanti, sambhatassa vinassati|

matā saggaṃ na gacchanti, nanu aittāvatā hatā’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| yai bālā adhammaina ca musāvādaina ca dhanaṃ saviñ‍ñāṇāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ sabbaṃ labhitabbaṃ dhanaṃ laddhā ubhayaṃ dhanaṃ ‘‘mama dhana’’nti mañ‍ñanti, taisaṃ bālānaṃ taṃ ubhayaṃ dhanaṃ kathaṃ kaina nu pakāraina bhavissati, adhammaina sambhatattā ciraṭṭhitikaṃ na hauti| antarāyā rājantarāyādayau antarāyā taisaṃ bālānaṃ bhavissanti| yaina adhammavauhārādikaina yaṃ dhanaṃ sambhataṃ, assa adhammavauhārādikassa taṃ sambhataṃ dhanaṃ vinassati| matā marantā tai bālā saggaṃ sugatiṃ na gacchanti| sugati hi saubhanaihi bhaugaihi aggauti ‘‘saggau’’ti  adhippaitā| aittāvatā aittakaina diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikānaṃ attahitānaṃ hāyanaina tai bālā hatā vinaṭṭhā bhavanti nanūti yaujanā|

‘‘kathaṃ khaṇati attānaṃ, kathaṃ mittaihi jīrati|

kathaṃ vivaṭṭatai dhammā, kathaṃ saggaṃ na gacchati’’||

‘‘laubhā khaṇati attānaṃ, luddhau mittaihi jīrati|

laubhā vivaṭṭatai dhammā, laubhā saggaṃ na gacchatī’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| yaujanatthau pākaṭau|

‘‘caranti bālā dummaidhā, amittainaiva attanā|

karauntā pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, yaṃ hauti kaṭukapphalaṃ||

‘‘na taṃ kammaṃ kataṃ sādhu, yaṃ katvā anutappati|

yassa assumukhau raudaṃ, vipākaṃ paṭisaivatī’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| dummaidhā nippañ‍ñā bālā amittaina pāpakaṃ kammaṃ kataṃ iva, aivaṃ attanā kaṭukapphalaṃ yaṃ kammaṃ kataṃ hauti, taṃ pāpakaṃ kataṃ kammaṃ karauntā caranti| yaṃ kammaṃ katvā karauntau pacchā anutappati, taṃ kataṃ kammaṃ na sādhu| yassa kammassa vipākaṃ raudaṃ rudantau assumukhau paṭisaivati, taṃ kataṃ kammaṃ na sādhūti yaujanā|

‘‘duk‍karaṃ duttitikkhañ‍ca…pai॰… avītarāgau’’ti idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| suttatthau aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vitthāratau vuttau|

‘‘appamaiyyaṃ paminantau, kaudha vidvā vikappayai|

appamaiyyaṃ pamāyinaṃ, nivutaṃ taṃ mañ‍ñai akissava’’nti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| idha sāsanai appamaiyyaṃ appamaiyyaguṇaṃ khīṇāsavaṃ puggalaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ khīṇāsavau puggalau aittakasīlau aittakasamādhi aittakapañ‍ñau’’ti paminantau kau puthuj‍janau  vikappayai| appamaiyyaṃ khīṇāsavapuggalaṃ pamāyinaṃ pamāyantaṃ taṃ puthuj‍janaṃ ayaṃ nivutaṃ avakuj‍japañ‍ñaṃ akissavaṃ apañ‍ñanti mañ‍ñai mañ‍ñāmīti yaujanā|

‘‘purisassa  hi jātassa, kuṭhārī jāyatai mukhai|

yāya chindati attānaṃ, bālau dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ||

‘‘na hi satthaṃ sunisitaṃ, visaṃ halāhalaṃ iva|

aivaṃ viraddhaṃ pātaiti, vācā dubbhāsitā yathā’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| dubbhāsitaṃ ariyūpavādasaṅkhātaṃ pharusavācaṃ bhaṇaṃ bhaṇantau bālau duṭṭhau purisau yāya kuṭhārīsadisiyā dubbhāsitavācāya attānaṃ chindati, sā kuṭhārīsadisī dubbhāsitavācā jātassa purisassa mukhai jāyatai jāyati, sā dubbhāsitavācā mukhai jāyati iva, aivaṃ sunisitaṃ satthaṃ mukhai na jāyati, yathā halāhalaṃ visaṃ mukhai na jāyati, dubbhāsitā vācā apāyaisu viraddhaṃ puggalaṃ pātaiti yathā, aivaṃ sunisitaṃ satthaṃ apāyaisu na pātaiti, halāhalaṃ visaṃ apāyaisu na pātaitīti yaujanā|

92.

 ‘‘yau nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati, taṃ vā nindati yau pasaṃsiyau|

vicināti mukhaina sau kaliṃ, kalinā taina sukhaṃ na vindati||

‘‘appamattau ayaṃ kali, yau akkhaisu dhanaparājayau|

sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā, ayamaiva mahantatarau kali|

yau sugataisu manaṃ padausayai||

‘‘sataṃ sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ, chattiṃsatī pañ‍ca ca abbudāni|

yamariyagarahī nirayaṃ upaiti, vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya pāpaka’’nti|| –
