idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| yau puggalau nindiyaṃ duc‍caraṃ dussīlaṃ puggalaṃ pasaṃsati, sau pasaṃsakau puggalau mukhaina kaliṃ vicināti upacināti, taina kalinā sukhaṃ na vindati| yau sucārī sīlavā puggalau pasaṃsiyau hauti, taṃ vā sucāriṃ vā sīlavantaṃ puggalaṃ  yau puggalau  nindati, sau nindantau puggalau mukhaina kaliṃ vicināti upacināti, taina kalinā sukhaṃ na vindati|

attanā sahāpi sabbassa dhanassa vasainapi akkhaisu yau dhanaparājayau bhavati, ayaṃ kali ayaṃ dhanaparājayau appamattau hauti| yau puggalau sugataisu manaṃ padausayai, tassa puggalassa yau kali bhavati, ayamaiva kali mahantatarau hauti|

kasmā? vācañ‍ca manañ‍ca paṇidhāya ariyagarahī puggalau yaṃ kālaṃ pāpakaṃ nirayaṃ upaiti, sau kālau ‘‘sataṃ sahassānaṃ nirabbudānañ‍ca chattiṃsa nirabbudāni ca pañ‍ca abbudāni ca yasmiṃ kālai gaṇīyantī’’ti taina kālaina samau hauti, tasmā mahantatarau hautīti yaujanā|

‘‘yau laubhaguṇai anuyuttau…pai॰…

gacchasi khau papataṃ ciraratta’’nti|| –

idaṃ suttampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| yau puggalau laubhaguṇai anuyuttau anu punappunaṃ yuttau hauti, sau puggalau añ‍ñai puggalai vacasā paribhāsati, assaddhau kadariyau avadañ‍ñū buddhānaṃ auvādañ‍ñū na hauti, maccharī paisuṇiyaṃ paisuṇiyasmiṃ anuyuttau hauti|

mukhadugga mukhavisama vibhūta vigatabhūta anariya bhūnahu buddhivināsaka pāpaka duk‍kaṭakāri purisanta purisalāmaka kali alakkhi avajātaputta tvaṃ nairayikau asi| idha idāni bahubhāṇī mā hauhi|

ahitāya rajaṃ attani mā ākirasi mā pakkhipasi| kibbisakāri tvaṃ santai khīṇāsavai puggalai garahasi, bahūni duc‍caritāni kammāni carasi, caritvā tvaṃ cirarattaṃ racanavirahitaṃ papataṃ narakaṃ nirayaṃ gacchasi khau aikaṃsaināti yaujanā|

nānāvidhaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ sutta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu sauḷasasu saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīsu suttaisu katamaṃ suttaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāmāti  pucchati| ‘‘manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā…pai॰… chāyāva anapāyinī’’ti idaṃ vāsanābhāgai puñ‍ñabhāgai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| atthau pākaṭau|

93. ‘‘mahānāmau  sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca…pai॰… apāpikā kālaṅkiriyā’’ti idaṃ suttampi vāsanābhāgai puñ‍ñabhāgai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| atthau pākaṭau|

‘‘sukhakāmāni bhūtāni, yau daṇḍaina na hiṃsati|

attanau sukhamaisānau, paic‍ca sau labhatai sukha’’nti|| –

idaṃ suttampi vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| atthau pākaṭau|

‘‘gun‍naṃ cai taramānānaṃ…pai॰… rājā cai hauti dhammikau’’ti idaṃ suttampi vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| atthau pākaṭau|

94. ‘‘bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… aivaṃ pajānātī’’ti idaṃ suttampi vāsanā…pai॰… suttaṃ nāma| atthau pākaṭau|

‘‘kasmā bhagavā janapadacārikaṃ caratī’’ti pucchaiyya, sattahi kāraṇaihi buddhā bhagavantau janapadacārikaṃ caranti| katamaihi sattahi? daisantaragatānaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ vinayanatthaṃ, tatra ṭhitānaṃ ussuk‍kasamuppādanatthaṃ, sāvakānaṃ aikasmiṃ ṭhānai nibaddhavāsanivāraṇatthaṃ, attanau ca tattha nibaddhavāsai anāsaṅgadassanatthaṃ, sambuddhavasitaṭṭhānatāya daisānaṃ caitiyabhāvasampādanatthaṃ, bahūnaṃ sattānaṃ dassanūpasaṅkamanādīhi puñ‍ñaughappasavanatthaṃ, avuṭṭhiādiupaddavūpasamanatthañ‍cāti imaihi sattahi kāraṇaihi buddhā bhagavantau janapadacārikaṃ carantīti janapadacaraṇakāraṇaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

‘‘aikapupphaṃ cajitvāna, sahassaṃ kappakauṭiyau|

daivai caiva manussai ca, saisaina parinibbutau’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| sahassaṃ kappakauṭiyauti sahassaṃ attabhāvatau kauṭiyau|

‘‘assatthai  haritaubhāsai, saṃvirūḷhamhi pādapai|

aikaṃ buddhagataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, alabhiṃhaṃ patissatau||

‘‘aj‍ja tiṃsaṃ tatau kappā, nābhijānāmi duggatiṃ|

tissau vij‍jā sacchikatā, tassā sañ‍ñāya vāsanā’’ti|| –

idaṃ suttampi vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| haritaubhāsaiti haritaaubhāsai| alabhiṃhanti ahaṃ alabhiṃ|

‘‘piṇḍāya  kausalaṃ puraṃ…pai॰… vipākau hauti acintiyau’’ti idaṃ suttampi vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| aggapuggalau anukampakau taṇhānighātakau muni sambuddhau puraibhattaṃ piṇḍāya piṇḍaṃ paṭiggaṇhituṃ kausalaṃ puraṃ pāvisi|

yassa purisassa hatthai sabbapupphaihi alaṅkatau vaṭaṃsakau pupphavaṭaṃsakauva atthi, sau ayaṃ purisau rājamaggaina kausalapuraṃ pavisantaṃ bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ daivamānusapūjitaṃ sambuddhaṃ addasa, disvā haṭṭhau cittaṃ pasādaisi; pasādaitvā sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkami|

upasaṅkamitvā sau ayaṃ pasan‍nau hutvā surabhiṃ vaṇṇavantaṃ manauramaṃ taṃ vaṭaṃsakaṃ sambuddhassa saihi pāṇībhi upanāmaisi|

tatau buddhassa lapanantarā lapanassa vadanassa antarā aggisikhā vaṇṇā sahassaraṃsi auk‍kā pabhā nikkhami, abbhā vij‍ju nikkhamati iva, aivaṃ ānanā sahassaraṃsi nikkhamitvā ādic‍cabandhunau sīsai tikkhattuṃ padakkhiṇaṃ karitvāna parivaṭṭaitvā muddhani antaradhāyatha|

ānandau acchariyaṃ abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ idaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ disvā cīvaraṃ aikaṃsaṃ karitvā aitaṃ abravi – ‘‘mahāmuni, sitakammassa haitu kau? taṃ haituṃ byākarauhi, dhammālaukau bhavissatī’’ti|

yassa bhagavatau sabbadhammaisu ñāṇaṃ sadā pavattati, kaṅkhāvitaraṇau muni sau bhagavā kaṅkhiṃ vaimatikaṃ  ānandaṃ thairaṃ aitaṃ abravi| ānanda, yau saupurisau mayi cittaṃ pasādayi, sau purisau caturāsītikappāni duggatiṃ na gamissati, daivaisu daivasaubhaggaṃ dibbaṃ raj‍jaṃ pasāsitvā manujaisu raṭṭhai sakalaraṭṭhai manujindau rājā bhavissati| sau purisau carimaṃ pabbajitvā, dhammataṃ sacchikatvā ca dhutarāgau vaṭaṃsakau nāma pac‍caikabuddhau bhavissati|

tathāgatai vā sammāsambuddhai vā pac‍caikasambuddhai vā tassa tathāgatassa sāvakai vā cittai pasan‍namhi dakkhiṇā appakā nāma natthi|

buddhā aivaṃ aittakāti acintiyā bhavanti| buddhadhammā buddhaguṇā aivaṃ aittakāti acintiyā bhavanti, acintiyai pasan‍nānaṃ vipākau puñ‍ñavipākau aivaṃ aittakauti acintiyau hautīti aitaṃ abravīti yaujanā|

96. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ…pai॰… ayampi atthau vuttau bhagavatā iti mai suta’’nti idaṃ suttampi vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| bhikkhavai, idha imasmiṃ sāsanai ahaṃ aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ mama caitasā aikac‍cassa puggalassa caitau cittaṃ paric‍ca buddhacakkhunā aivaṃ pajānāmi, yathā yaina pakāraina ayaṃ puggalau yañ‍ca dānādipaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau, yañ‍ca dassanādimaggaṃ samāruḷhau hutvā taṃ paṭipadaṃ, maggañ‍ca iriyati pavattaiti, imamhi imasmiñ‍ca samayai ayaṃ puggalau cai kālaṃ karaiyya, aivaṃ sati ābhataṃ vatthuṃ nikkhipati yathā, aivaṃ tāya paṭipadāya taina maggaina saggai attanikkhittau bhavai| taṃ kissa haitu? bhikkhavai, assa puggalassa cittaṃ hi yasmā pasan‍naṃ pasāditaṃ, tasmā nikkhittau bhavai| idha sāsanai, laukai vā aikac‍cai sattā puggalā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ caitaupasādahaitu khau pana upapaj‍jantīti aivaṃ ahaṃ pajānāmīti bhagavā aitamatthaṃ avauca| tattha tasmiṃ atthai saṅgahavasaina pavattaṃ aitaṃ ‘‘pasan‍nacittaṃ ñatvāna…pai॰… saggaṃ sau upapaj‍jatī’’ti gāthāvacanaṃ vuc‍cati| ayampi atthau bhagavatā vuttau, iti mai mayā sutanti yaujanā|

‘‘suvaṇṇacchadanaṃ nāvaṃ…pai॰… aitādisaṃ katapuñ‍ñā labhi’’nti idaṃ suttampi vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| nāri daivadhītā suvaṇṇacchadanaṃ suvaṇṇālaṅkāraihi chāditaṃ nāvaṃ āruyha tiṭṭhasi, paukkharaṇiṃ daivapaukkharaṇiṃ augāhasi, padumaṃ pāṇinā chindasi|

daivatai  kaina kammaina tai tava tādisau vaṇṇau tādisau ānubhāvau tādisī juti bhavati, daivatai tai tava yai kaici bhaugā manasā icchitā bhavanti, tai bhaugā ca kaina kammaina uppaj‍janti| daivatai mai pucchitā tvaṃ saṃsa saṃsāhi idaṃ sabbaṃ kissa kammassa ca phalanti sak‍kau pucchati|

daivarājaina pucchitā sā daivadhītā attamanā hutvā pañhaṃ puṭṭhā sak‍kassa byākāsi| daivarājā addhānaṃ dīghamaggaṃ paṭipan‍nā ahaṃ yasassinau kassapassa bhagavatau manauramaṃ thūpaṃ addassaṃ, disvā tattha thūpai cittaṃ pasādaisiṃ| pasan‍nāhaṃ saihi pāṇīhi padumapupphaihi pūjaisiṃ| tassaiva kammassa idaṃ sabbaṃ phalaṃ vipākau bhavai| katapuñ‍ñāhaṃ aitādisaṃ phalaṃ alabhinti sak‍kassa byākāsi| iti mai sutanti mahāmauggal‍lānau vadatīti yaujanā|

‘‘yathāniddhāritasuttāniyaiva  vāsanābhāgiyasuttāni paripuṇṇānī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘dānakathā sīlakathā saggakathā puñ‍ñakathā puñ‍ñavipākakathāti idaṃ vāsanābhāgiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha yāya daisanāya dānañ‍ca dānaphalañ‍ca dassitaṃ, sā daisanā dānakathā nāma| yāya daisanāya sīlañ‍ca sīlaphalañ‍ca dassitaṃ, sā daisanā sīlakathā nāma| yāya daisanāya saggā ca saggaisu nibbattāpakañ‍ca kammaṃ dassitaṃ, sā saggakathā nāma| yāya daisanāya dānasīlabhāvanādivasaina dasavidhaṃ puñ‍ñakammaṃ dassitaṃ, sā puñ‍ñakathā nāma| yāya daisanāya tādisassa puñ‍ñakammassa vividhau ayaṃ vipākau imassa puñ‍ñassa vipākauti niyamaitvā dassitau, sā puñ‍ñavipākakathā nāma|

dasabaladharānaṃ sammāsambuddhānaṃ uddissakataisu sarīradhātuṃ abbhantarai ṭhapaitvā paṃsūhi kataisu thūpaisu yai narā pasan‍nā, tai narā tattha thūpai kāraṃ puñ‍ñaṃ katvā saggaisu uppaj‍jitvā pamaudantīti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ vā…pai॰… suttaṃ|

97. daivaputtasarīravaṇṇā daivaputtasarīrasadisavaṇṇā subhagasaṇṭhiti saubhaggayuttasaṇṭhānā sabbai janā udakaina paṃsuṃ taimaitvā thūpaṃ vaḍḍhaitha, sau ayaṃ thūpau kassa puggalassa thūpauti pucchati|

sugattai sundaragattai daivatai tasmiṃ thūpai pasan‍nā imai daivamanujā kāraṃ puñ‍ñaṃ karauntā hutvā jarāmaraṇatau  pamuc‍carai| sau ayaṃ thūpau mahaisinau dasabaladhammadhārinau sugatassa thūpauti vaiditabbauti āhāti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ vā…pai॰… suttaṃ|

yāhaṃ yā ahaṃ mahaisinau thūpaṃ cattāri uppalāni ca mālañ‍ca abhiraupayiṃ, taina mayā kataṃ taṃ puñ‍ñaṃ uḷāraṃ vata āsi ahausi| tatau kappatau aj‍ja kappā tiṃsaṃ dharanti satthunau thūpaṃ pūjaitvā tattakāni duggatiṃ na jānāmi, vinipātaṃ na gacchāmīti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ vā…pai॰… suttaṃ|

ahaṃ bāttiṃsalakkhaṇadharassa bāttiṃsalakkhaṇadharaina sampan‍nassa vijitavijayassa laukanāthassa thūpaṃ apūjaisiṃ, pūjaitvā satasahassaṃ kappai āyukappai pamuditau āsiṃ| mayā yaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasutaṃ, taina puñ‍ñaina vinipātaṃ anāgantuna anāgantvā daivasaubhaggaṃ sampattiṃ ca daivaraj‍jāni ca tāni akāriṃ| atha vā daivasaubhaggañ‍ca mayā kāritaṃ, raj‍jāni ca mayā kāritāni|

adantadamakassa sāsanai yaṃ cakkhu pañ‍ñācakkhu paṇihitaṃ, tathā cittaṃ yaṃ vimuttacittaṃ paṇihitaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ pañ‍ñācakkhu vimuttacittaṃ mai mayā laddhaṃ, ahaṃ vidhutalatāsaṅkhātataṇhā hutvā vimuttacittā phalavimuttacittasampan‍nā amhīti avaucāti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ vā…pai॰… suttaṃ|

98. vimuttacittai phalavimuttacittasampan‍nai akhilai pañ‍cacaitaukhīlarahitai anāsavai araṇavihārimhi araṇavihārasīlai asaṅgamānasai alaggamānasai pac‍caikabuddhasmiṃ sāmākapatthaudanamattamaiva dakkhiṇaṃ adāsiṃ|

tasmiṃ pac‍caikabuddhai uttamaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍caikabaudhidhammaṃ aukappayiṃ ‘‘sau uttamau dhammau atthī’’ti saddahiṃ| aivaṃ ariyavihāraina vihārīhi pac‍caikabuddhaihi mai mama saṅgamau katau siyā bhavai, kudāsupi ca ahaṃ apaikkhavā mā bhavaiyyanti mānasaṃ tasmiñ‍ca dhammai paṇidhaisiṃ ‘‘iminā pac‍caikabuddhaina laddhadhammaṃ ahampi sacchikaraiyya’’nti cittaṃ paṇidahiṃ|

tassaiva pac‍caikabuddhai katassaiva kammassa vipākatau ahaṃ dīghāyukaisu amamaisu ‘‘mama pariggahau’’ti pariggahābhāvaina apariggahaisu visaisagāmīsu ahīnagāmīsu kurūsu uttarakurūsu pāṇīsu sattaisu sahassakkhattuṃ upapaj‍jatha upapaj‍jiṃ|

tassaiva  kammassa vipākatau vicitramālābharaṇānulaipīsu yasassīsu parivāravantaisu tidasau daivau ahaṃ visiṭṭhakāyūpagatau hutvā sahassakkhattuṃ upapaj‍jatha|

tassaiva kammassa vipākatau ahaṃ vimuttacittau akhīlau anāsavau hutvā hitāhitāsīhi kusalākusalavītivattaihi antimadaihadhāribhi imaihi pac‍caikabuddhaihi, buddhasāvakaihi vā mai mama samāgamau āsi|

‘‘sīlavatau yaṃ icchitaṃ, taṃ samijjhatai’’ti imaṃ vacanaṃ tathāgatau jinau pac‍cakkhaṃ katvā avaca khau, yathā yathā yaina yaina pakāraina mai manasā vicintitaṃ, tathā tathā taina taina pakāraina samiddhaṃ bhavati| ayaṃ bhavau antimau bhavauti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ vā…pai॰… suttaṃ|

aikatiṃsamhi  kappamhi jinau anaijau anantadassī ‘‘sikhī’’ti itināmakau uppaj‍ji| tassāpi bhagavatau rājā bhātā sikhiddhai ca sikhī itināmakai buddhai ca tassa bhagavatau dhammai ca abhippasan‍nau laukavināyakamhi parinibbutai sati daivātidaivassa naruttamassa mahaisinau vipulaṃ mahantaṃ samantatau gāvutikaṃ thūpaṃ akāsiṃ|

tasmiṃ thūpai baliṃ pūjābaliṃ abhihārī manussau jātisumanaṃ paggayha pahaṭṭhau pūjaisi| assa manussassa aikaṃ pupphaṃ vātaina paharitaṃ hutvā patitaṃ| ahaṃ taṃ patitaṃ aikaṃ pupphaṃ gahaitvā tassaiva pupphasāmikassa adāsiṃ|

sau manussau pupphasāmikau abhippasan‍nacittau hutvā maṃ ‘‘tvamaiva aitaṃ aikaṃ pupphaṃ pūjā’’ti adāsi| dadāsīti aittha da-kārau āgamau| ahaṃ taṃ aikaṃ pupphaṃ gahaitvā buddhaṃ buddhaguṇaṃ punappunaṃ anussarantau yasmiṃ kappai abhiraupayiṃ, tatau kappatau aj‍ja kappā tiṃsaṃ ahaisuṃ| taisu kappaisu duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vinipātañ‍ca na gacchāmi, idaṃ phalaṃ thūpapūjāya phalanti avaucāti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ vā…pai॰… suttaṃ|

brahmadattassa brahmadattanāmakassa rājinau kapilaṃ nāma nagaraṃ suvibhattaṃ bhāgatau suṭṭhu vibhattaṃ, mahāpathaṃ mahāpathasampan‍naṃ ākiṇṇaṃ, nānājātikaihi manussaihi paripuṇṇaṃ, iddhaṃ phītañ‍ca āsi|

pañ‍cālānaṃ  tattha puruttamai ahaṃ kummāsaṃ vik‍kiṇiṃ, sau ahaṃ yasassinaṃ upariṭṭhaṃ upasamīpai ṭhitaṃ ariṭṭhaṃ nāma sambuddhaṃ pac‍caikabuddhaṃ addasiṃ, disvā haṭṭhau cittaṃ pasādaitvā naruttamaṃ ariṭṭhaṃ mai gaihasmiṃ yaṃ dhuvabhattaṃ vij‍jatha vij‍ji, taina dhuvabhattaina nimantaisiṃ|

yatau ca yasmiṃ kālai ca kattikau yassaṃ pan‍narasīpuṇṇau, sā puṇṇamāsī pan‍narasī upaṭṭhitā, tatau ca tasmiṃ kālai ca ahaṃ navaṃ dussayugaṃ gayha ariṭṭhassa ariṭṭhanāmakassa pac‍caikabuddhassa upanāmaisiṃ|

naruttamau anukampakau kāruṇikau taṇhānighātakau muni pac‍caikabuddhau pasan‍nacittaṃ maṃ ñatvāna paṭiggaṇhi|

ahaṃ  kalyāṇaṃ buddhavaṇṇitaṃ kammaṃ karitvāna daivai ca manussai ca sandhāvitvā tatau cutau bārāṇasiyaṃ nagarai aḍḍhai kulasmiṃ saiṭṭhissa aikaputtakau uppaj‍jiṃ, pāṇaihi ca piyatarau āsiṃ|

tatau ca tasmiṃ kālai viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau hutvā daivaputtaina cauditau ahaṃ pāsādā auruhitvāna sambuddhaṃ bhagavantaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ|

sau sambuddhau bhagavā gautamau anukampāya mai dhammaṃ adaisaisi| dukkhaṃ dukkhasac‍cañ‍ca dukkhasamuppādaṃ samudayasac‍cañ‍ca dukkhassa atik‍kamaṃ niraudhasac‍cañ‍ca ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ maggaṃ maggasac‍cañ‍ca iti cattāri sac‍cāni daisitāni, taduppādakaṃ dhammaṃ muni bhagavā gautamau adaisayi|

ahaṃ tassa bhagavatau gautamassa vacanaṃ sutvā sāsanai ratau hutvā vihariṃ, ahaṃ rattindivaṃ atanditau hutvā samathaṃ paṭivijjhiṃ|

ajjhattañ‍ca yai āsavā, bahiddhā ca yai āsavā maggaina samucchin‍nā āsuṃ, sabbai tai āsavā mai mama vij‍jiṃsu, puna na ca uppaj‍jarai|

dukkhaṃ ‘‘pariyantakataṃ yassa dukkhassā’’ti pariyantakataṃ āsi, ayaṃ samussayau jātimaraṇasaṃsārau  carimau antimau āsi, idāni imassa attabhāvassa anantaraṃ punabbhavau mama natthīti avaucāti yaujanā| yaṃ nānāvidhaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ udāharaṇavasaina niddhāritaṃ, idaṃ nānāvidhaṃ suttaṃ vāsanābhāgai puñ‍ñakauṭṭhāsai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma|

nānāvidhaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha taisu sauḷasasu saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīsu suttaisu katamaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāmāti pucchati|

uddhaṃ brahmalaukai adhau kāmāvacarai bhavai sabbadhi sabbaisu bhavaisu vippamuttau arahā ‘‘ayaṃ nāma dhammau ahaṃ asmī’’ti anānupassī, aivaṃ vimuttau arahā atiṇṇapubbaṃ aughaṃ apunabbhavāya udatāri uttiṇṇauti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgai saikkhadhammai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma|

‘‘aittakamaivā’’ti  vattabbattā ‘‘sīlavatau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ānanda, sīlavatau puggalassa ‘‘kinti mai mama avippaṭisārau jāyaiyya pavattaiyyā’’ti caitanā na karaṇīyā na kātabbā| ānanda, sīlavatau avippaṭisārau yaṃ jāyaiyya pavattaiyya, aisā avippaṭisārassa jāyanā pavattanā dhammatā bhavati| ānanda, avippaṭisārinā puggalaina ‘‘kinti mai mama pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyaiyya pavattaiyyā’’ti caitanā na karaṇīyā, ānanda, avippaṭisārinau puggalassa pāmauj‍jaṃ yaṃ jāyaiyya pavattaiyya, aisā pāmauj‍jassa jāyanā pavattanā dhammatā| saisaisupi imassa yaujanānayānusāraina yaujanānayau gahaitabbau| idaṃ suttampi nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma|

ātāpinau kilaisānaṃ ātāpaina sammappadhānaina saman‍nāgatassa jhāyatau jhāyantassa brāhmaṇassa bāhitapāpassa khīṇāsavassa dhammā anulaumapac‍cayākārapaṭivaidhasādhakā baudhipakkhiyadhammā yadā yasmiṃ kālai havai aikantaina pātubhavanti uppaj‍janti| atha vā dhammā catuariyasac‍cadhammā pātubhavanti pakāsayanti abhisamayavasaina pākaṭā haunti| atha vā pātubhavanakālai assa ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa khīṇāsavassa sabbā kaṅkhā vapayanti apagacchanti nirujjhanti| kasmā? sahaitudhammaṃ avij‍jādikaina haitunā saha pavattaṃ saṅkhārādikaṃ sukhaina asammissaṃ dukkhakkhandhadhammaṃ yatau yasmā pajānāti añ‍ñāsi paṭivijjhati, tatau tasmā vapayanti apagacchanti nirujjhantīti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma|

dutiyagāthāyaṃ pana pac‍cayānaṃ khayaṃ khayasaṅkhātaṃ nibbānaṃ yatau yasmā avaidi añ‍ñāsi paṭivijjhi, tatau tasmā sabbāpi kaṅkhā vapayantīti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

tissa, tvaṃ kiṃ nu kujjhasi? mā kujjhi, tissa, tai tava ak‍kaudhau akujjhanaṃ varaṃ uttamaṃ, hi sac‍caṃ, tissa, tayā kaudhamānamakkhavinayatthaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vussati nūti bhagavā avaucāti yaujanā|

ārañ‍ñaṃ ārañ‍ñakaṃ paṃsukūlikaṃ añ‍ñātuñchaina añ‍ñātaanabhilakkhitagharapaṭipāṭiyā ṭhatvā uñchaina piṇḍapātacaraṇavīriyaina laddhaina missakabhaujanaina yāpaintaṃ nandaṃ kadā kālai ahaṃ passaiyyanti avaucāti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

gautama, kiṃsu katamaṃ chaitvā vadhitvā vadhantau kaudhapariḷāhaina aparidayhamānau hutvā sukhaṃ saiti sayati, kiṃsu katamaṃ chaitvā vadhitvā vadhantau kaudhavināsaina vinaṭṭhadaumanassau hutvā na saucati, gautama, tvaṃ kissa aikadhammassa vadhaṃ vadhanaṃ raucaisīti brāhmaṇau pucchati|

brāhmaṇa, kaudhaṃ kujjhanaṃ chaitvā vadhitvā vadhantau kaudhapariḷāhaina aparidayhamānau hutvā sukhaṃ saiti sayati, kaudhaṃ kujjhanaṃ chaitvā vadhitvā vadhantau kaudhavināsaina vinaṭṭhadaumanassau hutvā na saucati, visamūlassa visasadisassa dukkhassa mūlabhūtassa madhuraggassa madhurasaṅkhātassa sukhapariyausānassa kaudhassa kujjhanassa vadhaṃ vadhanaṃ ariyā buddhādayau puggalā pasaṃsanti| hi sac‍caṃ taṃ kaudhaṃ kujjhanaṃ chaitvā vadhitvā vadhantau kaudhavināsaina vinaṭṭhadaumanassau hutvā saiti sayatīti yaujanā| madhuraggassāti ca madhuraṃ caitasikasukhaṃ assādaaggaṃ pariyausānaṃ assa kaudhassāti madhuraggauti samāsau vaiditabbau| kujjhantassa hi ak‍kausitvā paribhāsitvā paharitvā pariyausānai caitasikasukhassādau uppaj‍jatīti| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

gautama, hanantau dhīrau uppatitaṃ kiṃsu katamaṃ hanai hanaiyya| vinaudaintau dhīrau jātaṃ kiṃsu  katamaṃ vinaudayai vinaudayaiyya| pajahantau dhīrau kiṃ ca katamaṃ pajahai pajahaiyya| dhīrassa kissa dhammassa abhisamayau sukhauti daivatā pucchati|

daivaputta, hanantau dhīrau uppatitaṃ kaudhaṃ kujjhanaṃ hanai hanaiyya| vinaudaintau dhīrau jātaṃ rāgaṃ vinaudayai vinaudayaiyya| pajahantau dhīrau avij‍jaṃ pajahai pajahaiyya| sac‍cadhammassa abhisamayau sukhauti bhagavā avaucāti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

101. bhagavā sattiyā aumaṭṭhau uparitau yāva haiṭṭhā viddhau purisau sattippahānāya vīriyaṃ ārabhati viya, agginā matthakai ḍayhamānau ādittasirau purisau agginibbāpanatthāya vīriyaṃ ārabhati iva, aivaṃ kāmarāgaina ḍayhamānau bhikkhu kāmarāgappahānāya kāmarāgavikkhambhanāya appamattau vāyamamānau satau satisampan‍nau hutvā paribbajai viharaiyyāti daivatā kathaisi|

bhagavā  pana ‘‘samucchaidappahānāya vīriyaṃ ārabhīyatī’’ti dassaituṃ ‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau’’tiādimāha| daivaputta, sattiyā aumaṭṭhau purisau sattippahānāya vīriyaṃ ārabhati viya, agginā matthakai ḍayhamānau ādittasirau purisau agginibbāpanatthāya vīriyaṃ ārabhati iva, aivaṃ sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā abhibhūtau bhikkhu sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā pahānāya maggaina samucchaidappahānāya appamattau vāyamamānau satau satisampan‍nau hutvā paribbajai viharaiyyāti bhagavā avaucāti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma|

sabbai nicayā bhaugā khayantā khayapariyausānā bhavanti, sabbai samussayā dhammā patanantā patanapariyausānā bhavanti, sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ maraṇamāgamma sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ jīvitaṃ addhuvaṃ bhavati, iti aitaṃ vuttappakāraṃ bhayaṃ bhayahaituṃ maraṇaṃ sammutimaraṇaṃ apaikkhamānau paṇḍitau sukhāvahāni diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikasukhāvahāni puñ‍ñāni dānasīlabhāvanāmayapuñ‍ñāni kayirāthāti daivatā avauca|

daivaputta, sabbai nicayā bhaugā khayantā khayapariyausānā, sabbai samussayā dhammā patanantā patanapariyausānā, sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ maraṇamāgamma sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ jīvitaṃ addhuvaṃ, iti aitaṃ vuttappakāraṃ bhayaṃ bhayahaituṃ maraṇaṃ apaikkhamānau santipaikkhau sabbasaṅkhārupasamaṃ nibbānaṃ apaikkhamānau  paṇḍitau laukāmisaṃ kāmaguṇaṃ pajahai pajahaiyyāti bhagavā avaucāti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma|

māvidha yaisaṃ munīnaṃ cittaṃ jhānarataṃ jhānai rataṃ hauti, tai munayau sukhaṃ sayanti na saucanti| pañ‍ñavā maggapañ‍ñavā susamāhitau āraddhavīriyau pahitattau nibbānaṃ paisitacittau puggalau duttaraṃ aughaṃ saṃsāraughaṃ tarati|

kāmasañ‍ñāya viratau vigatacittau yau khīṇāsavau sabbasaṃyaujanātītau arahattamaggaina sabbai saṃyaujanai atītau nandibhavaparikkhīṇau ahausi, sau khīṇāsavau gambhīrai saṃsāraṇṇavai na sīdatīti yaujanā| nandisaṅkhātā taṇhā ca kāmabhavarūpabhavaarūpabhavā ca nandibhavā, nandibhavā parikkhīṇā yassa khīṇāsavassāti nandibhavaparikkhīṇauti samāsau vaiditabbau| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma|

arahataṃ  arahantānaṃ buddhapac‍caikabuddhasāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ sucaritādibhaidañ‍ca sattatiṃsabaudhipakkhiyabhaidañ‍ca dhammaṃ yau paṇḍitau saddahānau saddahantau hutvā nibbānappattiyā appamattau vicakkhaṇau hutvā sussūsaṃ sussūsantau bhavai, sau paṇḍitau pañ‍ñaṃ laukiyalaukuttarapañ‍ñaṃ labhatai labhati|

yau vīriyavā puggalau patirūpadaisakārī daisakālādīni ahāpaitvā laukiyalaukuttaradhammapatirūpaṃ adhigamūpāyaṃ karauti, dhuravā caitasikavīriyaina anikkhittadhurau uṭṭhātā kāyikavīriyavasaina uṭṭhānasampan‍nau hauti, sau vīriyavā puggalau dhanaṃ laukiyalaukuttaradhanaṃ vindatai adhigacchati| sac‍caina vacīsac‍caina ca paramatthasac‍caina ca buddhādikau sac‍cadhammai ṭhitau sappurisau kittiṃ pappauti| dadaṃ dadantau yaṃ kiñ‍ci icchitaṃ patthitaṃ catusaṅgahavatthuṃ dadantau saṅgahantau sappurisau mittāni aikantamittāni ganthati sampādaiti, aivaṃ catūhi sac‍cadhammadhiticāgaihi saman‍nāgatau sau sappurisau asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ paic‍ca gantvā aikantaina saukakāraṇassa abhāvatau na saucatīti yaujanā|

gautama, sabbaganthappahīnau tīhi bhavaihi vippamuttau satau satisampan‍nau tvaṃ, samaṇau, añ‍ñaṃ daivamanussādikaṃ yaṃ anusāsasi yaṃ anusāsanaṃ karausi, taṃ anusāsanaṃ sabbaganthappahīnassa  tīhi bhavaihi vippamuttassa samaṇassa tai tava na sādhūti sak‍kanāmakau mārapakkhikau yakkhau gāthāya ajjhabhāsi|

‘‘sak‍ka sak‍kanāmaka yakkha anukampitaina purisaina saddhiṃ yaina kainaci vaṇṇaina kāraṇaina saṃvāsau aikasmiṃ ṭhānai sahavāsau jāyati, taṃ anukampitabbaṃ sahavāsagataṃ purisaṃ sappañ‍ñau manasā anukampituṃ na arahati anukampituṃyaiva arahati| yā anukampā karuṇā, yā anuddayā maittā, muditā ca uppan‍nā, tāya anukampāya karuṇāya, tāya anuddayāya maittāya muditāya ca samussāhitaina pasan‍naina manasā yau sappañ‍ñau sappurisau añ‍ñaṃ daivamanussādikaṃ yaṃ anusāsati yaṃ anusāsanaṃ karauti, sau sappañ‍ñau sappurisau taina anusāsanaina saṃyuttau kāmacchandādīnaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ vasaina ananulaumasaṃyaugaina saṃyuttau na hautīti bhagavā avaucāti yaujanā|

102. samaṇa, rāgau ca dausau ca imai dvai dhammā kutaunidānā, kiṃnidānā, kiṃpac‍cayā bhavanti, arati ca rati ca laumahaṃsau ca imai tayau kutaujā kutau bhavanti? kumārakā dhaṅkaṃ kākaṃ gahaitvā pādai dīghasuttakaina bandhitvā suttakauṭiṃ aṅguliyaṃ vaiṭhaitvā ausajanti iva, aivaṃ manauvitak‍kā kutau samuṭṭhāya cittaṃ ausajantīti sūcilaumayakkhau bhagavantaṃ pucchi|

rāgau ca dausau ca imai dvai dhammā itaunidānā itau attabhāvatau nidānā jāyanti; arati ca rati ca laumahaṃsau ca imai tayau itaujā itau attatau bhavanti; kumārakā dhaṅkaṃ kākaṃ gahaitvā pādai dīghasuttakaina bandhitvā suttakauṭiṃ aṅguliyaṃ vaiṭhaitvā ausajanti iva, aivaṃ manauvitak‍kā itau attabhāvatau samuṭṭhāya cittaṃ ausajanti|

nigraudhassa khandhajā pārauhā sākhāsu jāyanti iva, vanai rukkhaṃ nissāya jātā māluvā latā taṃ rukkhaṃ ajjhauttharitvā vitatā autatavitatā tiṭṭhati iva, aivaṃ, yakkha, tvaṃ suṇauhi snaihajā taṇhāsnaihatau jātā attasambhūtā attani sambhūtā puna anaikappakārā manauvitak‍kā pāpamanauvitak‍kā caiva taṃsampayuttakilaisā ca kāmaisu vatthukāmaisu visattā laggā saṃsibbitā ṭhitā|

yai paṇḍitā ‘‘yaṃ nidānaṃ assa attabhāvassā’’ti yataunidānaṃ naṃ kilaisagahanaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ  pajānanti, tai paṇḍitā attabhāvasaṅkhātassa dukkhasac‍cassa nidānabhūtaṃ naṃ kilaisagahanaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ maggasac‍caina vinaudainti| apunabbhavāya apunabhavasaṅkhātāya niraudhasac‍catthāya atiṇṇapubbaṃ anamataggai saṃsārai supinaināpi atiṇṇapubbaṃ duttaraṃ imaṃ aughaṃ catubbidhaṃ saṃkilaisaughaṃ tarantīti bhagavā avaucāti yaujanā|

‘‘bhagavā, samaṇadhammassa karaṇaṃ nāma duk‍karaṃ, bhagavā, samaṇadhammassa karaṇaṃ nāma suduk‍karaṃ suṭṭhutaraṃ duk‍kara’’nti aikau kulaputtau pabbajitvā samaṇadhammaṃ katvā ariyabhūmiṃ appatvā kālaṃ katvā daivalaukai nibbattau, sau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā āha| kāmada, sīlasamāhitā ṭhitattā ṭhitasabhāvā satta saikkhā puggalā duk‍karaṃ vāpi samaṇadhammaṃ karaunti| anagāriyupaitassa anagāriyaṃ niggaihabhāvaṃ upagatassa pabbajitassa  tuṭṭhi catupac‍cayasantausau sukhāvahā hautī’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘bhagavā, yadidaṃ yā aisā tuṭṭhi sukhāvahā, aisā tuṭṭhi dul‍labhā’’ti sau daivaputtau āha| ‘‘kāmada, yaisaṃ saikkhānaṃ manau divā ca rattau ca bhāvanāya ratau cittavūpasamai ratau, tai saikkhā dul‍labhaṃ vāpi tussanaṃ labhantī’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘bhagavā, yadidaṃ yaṃ idaṃ cittaṃ bhāvanāya rataṃ, taṃ cittaṃ dussamādaha’’nti sau daivaputtau āha| ‘‘kāmada, yai ariyā indriyūpasamai rattindivaṃ ratā, tai ariyā dussamādahaṃ vāpi cittaṃ samādahanti, kāmada, tai ariyā mac‍cunau jālaṃ kilaisajālaṃ chaitvā maggaṃ gacchantī’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘bhagavā, yau maggau pubbabhāgapaṭipadāvasaina visamau, sau maggau duggamau’’ti sau daivaputtau āha| ‘‘kāmada, ariyā duggamai visamai vāpi maggaṃ gacchanti, anariyā visamai maggai avaṃsirā papatanti, ariyānaṃ sau maggau samauva bhavai, na asamau| hi sac‍caṃ visamai visattakāyai ariyā samā bhavantī’’ti bhagavā avaucāti yaujanā|

103. yaṃ jaitavanaṃ isisaṅghanisaivitaṃ dhammarājaina āvutthaṃ, idaṃ taṃ jaitavanaṃ mama pītisañ‍jananaṃ pītiyā sañ‍jananaṃ karaṃ hi karaṃ aiva|

kammaṃ maggacaitanākammañ‍ca vij‍jā maggapañ‍ñā ca dhammau samādhi caiva samādhipakkhikau ca  dhammau sīlaṃ, sīlai ṭhitassa jīvitaṃ uttamaṃ, aitaina aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina sattā sujjhanti, gauttaina vā dhanaina vā sattā na sujjhanti|

tasmā maggainaiva sattānaṃ visujjhanatau attanau atthaṃ sampassaṃ passantau paṇḍitau pausau yaunisau upāyaina dhammaṃ baudhipakkhiyadhammaṃ vicinai vicinaiyya, aivaṃ vicinanai sati tattha ariyamaggai vicinantau puggalau sujjhati|

sāriputtau sīlaina ca upasamaina ca pāraṅgatau iva, aivaṃ yaupi bhikkhu sīlaina ca upasamaina ca pāraṅgatau, sau bhikkhu aitāva paramau sāriputtasadisauva siyāti anāthapiṇḍikanāmau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā āhāti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma|

atītaṃ  atītakkhandhapañ‍cakaṃ taṇhādiṭṭhīhi nānvāgamaiyya, anāgataṃ anāgatakkhandhapañ‍cakaṃ taṇhādiṭṭhīhi nappaṭikaṅkhai na patthaiyya| yaṃ yasmā atītaṃ pahīnaṃ niruddhaṃ atthaṅgataṃ, tasmā atītassa pahīnattā niruddhattā atthaṅgatattā nānvāgamaiyya| yaṃ yasmā anāgataṃ appattaṃ, tasmā na paṭikaṅkhai|

pac‍cuppan‍naṃ khandhapañ‍cakaṃ vayadhammaṃ yattha yattha santānai vā, yattha yattha arañ‍ñādīsu vā uppan‍naṃ, tattha tattha santānai vā, tattha tattha arañ‍ñādīsu vā naṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadhammaṃ yāhi anic‍cānupassanādīhi vipassati, tāhi anic‍cānupassanādīhi nibbānaṃ rāgādīhi asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ bhavati, taṃ nibbānaṃ vipassakau puggalau vidvā nibbānārammaṇaṃ phalasamāpattiṃ appaintau hutvā anubrūhayai vaḍḍhaiyya|

ātappaṃ saṃkilaisānaṃ ātapantaṃ vīriyaṃ aj‍jaiva kic‍caṃ kātabbaṃ, suvai jīvitaṃ vā maraṇaṃ vā kau jañ‍ñā jānaiyya, ‘‘suvai vā dānādipuñ‍ñaṃ jānissāmī’’ti cittaṃ anuppādaitvā ‘‘aj‍jaiva karissāmī’’ti aivaṃ vīriyaṃ kātabbaṃ| hi sac‍caṃ maraṇakāraṇabhāvatāya ahivicchikavisasatthādianaikāya saināya vasaina mahāsainaina taina mac‍cunā saddhiṃ mittasanthavākāraina vā lañ‍jadānaina vā saṅgaraṃ natthīti|

aivaṃ  manasi katvā vihāriṃ viharantaṃ ātāpiṃ ahaurattaṃ atanditaṃ analasaṃ uṭṭhāhakaṃ sappurisaṃ ‘‘bhaddaikarattau’’ti santau muni vai aikantaina ācikkhatai ācikkhatiyaivāti yaujanā|

‘‘cattārimāni bhikkhavai’’tyādisuttaṃ pāḷitau ca aṭṭhakathātau ca pākaṭaṃ|

104. nānāvidhaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu sauḷasasu saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīsu suttaisu katamaṃ suttaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāmāti pucchati|

yassa uttamapurisassa cittaṃ sailūpamaṃ ṭhitaṃ laukadhammavātaihi nānukampati, rajanīyaisu lābhādīsu virattaṃ bhavai, sau uttamapurisau kaupanaiyyai  alābhādikai na kuppati, tassa uttamapurisassa cittaṃ aivaṃ anic‍catādinā bhāvitaṃ, naṃ bhāvitacittaṃ uttamapurisaṃ vītik‍kantalaukadhammahaitukaṃ dukkhaṃ kutau aissatīti yaujanā| idaṃ suttaṃ asaikkhabhāgai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma| aisa nayau itau paraisupi vaiditabbau|

‘‘asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ idamaivā’’ti vattabbabhāvatau ‘‘āyasmatau cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

yau brāhmaṇau bāhitapāpadhammattā bāhitapāpadhammau bhavai, sau brāhmaṇau niggatahuṃhuṃkattā nihuṃhuṅkau bhavai, niggatakilaisakasāvattā nik‍kasāvau bhavai, sīlasaṃvaraina saṃyatacittatāya yatattau bhavai, yau brāhmaṇau catumaggañāṇavaidaihi antaṃ nibbānaṃ gatattā vaidantagū bhavai, dhammaina vusitabrahmacariyattā vusitabrahmacariyau bhavai| yassa brāhmaṇassa kuhiñ‍ci laukai ussadā rāgussadau dausussadau mauhussadau mānussadau diṭṭhussadau natthi, sau brāhmaṇau brahmavādaṃ ‘‘ahaṃ brāhmaṇaumhī’’ti vācaṃ vadaiyyāti bhagavā avaucāti yaujanā|

yai buddhā pāpakai akusalai dhammai bāhitvā sadā caranti satā satisampan‍nā khīṇasaṃyaujanā, tai buddhā laukasmiṃ brāhmaṇāti vai aikantaina kathīyantīti avaucāti yaujanā|

yattha nibbānai āpau ca pathavī ca taijau ca vāyau ca na gādhati na patiṭṭhahati, tattha  nibbānai suk‍kā gahā caiva tārakā ca na jautanti, tattha nibbānai ādic‍cau nappakāsati, tattha nibbānai candimā na bhāti, tattha nibbānai tamau na vij‍jati|

yau brāhmaṇau attanā sayaṃ muni maunaina yadā taṃ nibbānaṃ avaidi vindati paṭilabhati paṭivijjhati, atha paṭivijjhanakkhaṇai sau brāhmaṇau rūpā rūpadhammatau ca arūpā arūpadhammatau ca sukhadukkhā sukhadukkhatau ca pamuc‍catīti avaucāti yaujanā|

yakkha, yau brāhmaṇau sakaisu sakaattabhāvaisu dhammaisu upādānakkhandhaisu sac‍caisu, dhammaisu ca pāragū hauti, atha pāragamanakkhaṇai sau brāhmaṇau aitaṃ ajakalāpakaṃ tayā vuttaṃ aitaṃ pisācaṃ kilaisapisācañ‍ca, tayā kataṃ ak‍kulañ‍ca ak‍kulaṃ, pak‍kulakaraṇaṃ ativattatīti avaucāti yaujanā|

yau bhikkhu āyantiṃ āgacchantiṃ purāṇadutiyikaṃ bhariyaṃ vā, añ‍ñaṃ āgacchantiṃ itthiṃ vā cittaina na abhinandati, pak‍kamantiṃ purāṇadutiyikaṃ bhariyaṃ vā, añ‍ñaṃ pak‍kamantiṃ itthiṃ vā cittaina na saucati, saṅgā pañ‍cavidhataupi saṅgatau muttaṃ saṅgāmajiṃ taṃ bhikkhuṃ ‘‘brāhmaṇa’’nti ahaṃ vadāmīti avaucāti yaujanā|

aittha nadiyaṃ bahujanau nhāyati, sau bahujanau nhāyakau udakaina udakanhānaina sucī na hauti| yamhi puggalai sac‍caṃ, sac‍catau saisadhammau ca atthi, sau puggalau sucī ca hauti, sau puggalau brāhmaṇau ca hautīti avaucāti yaujanā; suciasucibhāvau ṭīkāyaṃ vitthāratau vuttauva|

ātāpinau jhāyatau yassa brāhmaṇassa dhammā sac‍cadhammā yadā havai aikantaina pātubhavanti, tadā dhammānaṃ pātubhavanakkhaṇai sau brāhmaṇau mārasainaṃ vidhūpayaṃ vidhūpayantau tiṭṭhati| ‘‘kimivā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘sūriyauva aubhāsayamantalikkha’’nti vuttaṃ| sūriyau antalikkhaṃ aubhāsayantau tiṭṭhati iva, aivaṃ tiṭṭhatīti yaujanā|

yau paṃsukūlikau bhikkhu sabbāni cattāri yaugāni upātivattau sakiñ‍canai laukai akiñ‍canau iriyati catubbidhairiyāpathaṃ vattaiti| apahānadhammaṃ kainaci maggaina appahānasabhāvaṃ appattakāyaina appattaṃ taivij‍japattaṃ, iriyamānaṃ santindriyaṃ taṃ paṃsukūlikaṃ bhikkhuṃ tumhai passatha|

ājāniyaṃ  purisaājānīyaṃ jātibalanisaidhaṃ ‘‘ahaṃ jātibrāhmaṇau’’ti jātimattakaina pavattamānabalanisaidhakaṃ taṃ paṃsukūlikaṃ bhikkhuṃ sambahulā uḷārā daivatā brahmaṃ vimānaṃ upasaṅkamitvā idha sāsanai, brahmavimānai vā pasan‍nacittā hutvā namassanti| nidhāti ca aittha na-kārau āgamau|

purisājañ‍ña tai tava amhākaṃ namau atthu, purisuttama tai tava amhākaṃ namau atthu, yassa tai tava nissayaṃ mayaṃ nābhijānāma, sau tvaṃ kiṃ puggalaṃ nissāya jhāyasīti avaucunti yaujanā|

yai  bhikkhū kālaina kālaṃ dhammassavanavasaina cirarattaṃ samaitikā bhavanti, imai bhikkhū sahāyā haunti vata| naisaṃ sahāyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammai buddhappavaiditai dhammai saddhammau samaiti|

kappinaina ariyappavaiditai dhammai suvinītā tai sahāyakā bhikkhū savāhiniṃ māraṃ jaitvā antimaṃ daihaṃ attabhāvaṃ dhāraintīti avaucāti yaujanā|

sithilaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabbha sabbadukkhappamaucanaṃ idaṃ nibbānaṃ yaugāvacaraina na adhigantabbaṃ, appaina appakaina thāmasā idaṃ nibbānaṃ na adhigantabbaṃ|

ayañ‍ca yaugāvacarau bhikkhu daharau, yau purisau savāhiniṃ māraṃ jaitvā antimaṃ daihaṃ attabhāvaṃ dhāraiti, sau ayaṃ purisau sau uttamapurisauvāti avaucāti yaujanā| ‘‘purisau’’ti vattabbai chandānurakkhaṇavasaina ‘‘paurisau’’ti vuttaṃ|

maugharāja, dubbaṇṇakau lūkhacīvarau sadā satau satisampan‍nau khīṇāsavau ca visaṃyuttau ca katakic‍cau ca anāsavau ca taivij‍jau ca iddhippattau ca caitaupariyāyakauvidau ca sau bhikkhu savāhiniṃ māraṃ jaitvā antimaṃ daihaṃ dhāraitīti avaucāti yaujanā|

105. ‘‘tathāgatau’’tiādīsu yaujanā pākaṭā| idaṃ suttaṃ asaikkhabhāgai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ nāma|

106. nānāvidhaṃ  asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca sutta’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca sutta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu sauḷasasu saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīsu suttaisu katamaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nāmāti pucchati|

chan‍naṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā chan‍naṃ chādaintaṃ bhikkhuṃ duk‍kaṭādivassau ativassati, vivaṭaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā vivaṭaṃ daisaintaṃ ācikkhantaṃ bhikkhuṃ duk‍kaṭādivassau nātivassati, tasmā chan‍nassa ativassanatau ca vivaṭassa nātivassanatau ca chan‍naṃ chāditabbaṃ āpattiṃ vivaraitha daisaitha āraucaitha, aivaṃ vivaraṇai sati taṃ vivarantaṃ bhikkhuṃ duk‍kaṭādivassau nātivassatīti yaujanā|

‘‘imasmiṃ suttai kittakaina saṃkilaisau dassitau, kittakaina vāsanā dassitā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘chan‍namativassatī’’ti saṃkilaisau, ‘vivaṭaṃ nātivassatī’ti vāsanā, ‘tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraitha, aivaṃ taṃ nātivassatī’ti ayaṃ saṃkilaisau ca vāsanā cā’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘chan‍namativassatī’’ti aittakaina saṃkilaisau dassitau| ‘‘vivaṭaṃ nātivassatī’’ti aittakaina vāsanā dassitā| ‘‘tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraitha, aivaṃ taṃ nātivassatī’’ti aittakaina ayaṃ saṃkilaisau ca dassitau, ayaṃ vāsanā ca dassitā| idaṃ ‘‘chan‍naṃ…pai॰.. vassatī’’ti suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgai visayai ca vāsanābhāgai visayai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nāma|

‘‘saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ aittakamaivā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘cattāraumai mahārājā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘taisu catūsu puggalaisu katamai puggalā saṃkilaisabhāgiyā, katamai puggalā vāsanābhāgiyā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yau ca puggalau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu catūsu tamautamaparāyaṇādīsu puggalaisu| tassatthau pākaṭau| idaṃ ‘‘cattāraumai’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaisu dvīsu puggalaisu ca vāsanābhāgiyaisu dvīsu puggalaisu ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nāma|

nānāvidhaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nāmā’’ti pucchitabbattā tattha katamaṃ  saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca sutta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tassatthau vuttanayaina vaiditabbau|

ayasaṃ yaṃ bandhanañ‍ca dārujaṃ yaṃ bandhanañ‍ca pabbajaṃ yaṃ bandhanañ‍ca laukai atthi, taṃ ayasādibandhanaṃ ‘‘daḷhaṃ bandhana’’nti dhīrā buddhādayau paṇḍitapurisā na āhu| maṇikuṇḍalaisu ca puttaisu ca dāraisu ca yā sārattarattā balavarāgarattā apaikkhā laukai vij‍jati, taṃ sārattarattaapaikkhāsaṅkhātaṃ rāgabandhanaṃ ‘‘daḷhaṃ bandhana’’nti dhīrā paṇḍitapurisā āhu| iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ sārattarattaapaikkhāsaṅkhātau akusaladhammau saṃkilaisau dassitau|

‘‘kaina  nibbaidhau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘aita’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| dhīrā paṇḍitapurisā aitaṃ rāgabandhanaṃ ‘‘daḷhaṃ bandhana’’nti āhu| aitaṃ rāgabandhanaṃ auhārinaṃ haiṭṭhā apāyaṃ avaharaṇaṃ hauti, sithilaṃ bandhanaṭṭhānai chaviādīni akaupaitattā sithilaṃ hauti, duppamuñ‍caṃ laubhavasaina aikavārampi uppan‍nassa rāgabandhanassa dummaucayattā duppamuñ‍caṃ hauti, paṇḍitapurisā aitampi vuttappakāraṃ rāgabandhanampi maggaina chaitvāna anapaikkhinau hutvā kāmasukhaṃ pahāya paribbajanti| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ maggau nibbaidhau dassitau| idaṃ ‘‘ayasa’’ntiādikaṃ suttaṃ rāgādisaṃkilaisabhāgai visayai ca vāsanābhāgai visayai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nāma|

107. bhikkhavai, yañ‍ca caitaiti yañ‍ca caitanaṃ nibbattaiti, yañ‍ca pakappaiti yañ‍ca pakappanaṃ karauti, yañ‍ca anusaiti yañ‍ca anusayanaṃ bhavati, aitaṃ caitanaṃ aitaṃ pakappanaṃ aitaṃ anusayanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā ārammaṇaṃ pac‍cayau hauti| ārammaṇai pac‍cayai sati tassa abhisaṅkhāra viñ‍ñāṇassa patiṭṭhā hauti| tasmiṃ abhisaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇai patiṭṭhitai viruḷhai sati āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti viñ‍ñāṇādinibbatti hauti| āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā sati āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ sambhavati| āyatiṃ sauka…pai॰… samudayau hauti|

bhikkhavai, cai nau caitaiti, cai nau pakappaiti, atha tathāpi cai anusaiti anusayanaṃ bhavati, aivaṃ sati aitaṃ anusayanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa abhisaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ pac‍cayau hauti…pai॰… samudayau hauti| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ vuttappakārau caitayanādikau akusaladhammau saṃkilaisau dassitau|

‘‘kaina  nibbaidhau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘yatau cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhikkhavai, yatau ca ariyamaggikau naiva caitaiti, nau ca pakappaiti, nau ca anusaiti, aitaṃ acaitayanaṃ aitaṃ akappanaṃ aitaṃ ananusayanaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa abhisaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ pac‍cayau na hauti…pai॰… āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ nirujjhati, āyatiṃ sauka…pai॰… yāsā nirujjhanti…pai॰… niraudhau hauti| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ ariyamaggau nibbaidhau dassitau| idaṃ ‘‘yañ‍ca bhikkhavai’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ  saṃkilaisabhāgai visayai ca nibbaidhabhāgai visayai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nāma|

108. nānāvidhaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca sutta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tassatthau vuttanayaina vaiditabbau|

bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau samuddau jalasāgarasamuddau ‘‘samuddau samuddau’’ti bhāsati| kainaṭṭhaina bhāsati? duppūraṇaṭṭhaina ca saṃsaraṇaṭṭhaina ca duratik‍kamanaṭṭhaina ca samuddauti bhāsati| aivaṃ sati, bhikkhavai, aisau jalasāgarasamuddau ariyassa bhagavatau vinayai vuttappakāraṭṭhaina samuddau na hauti| bhikkhavai, aisau jalasāgarasamuddau mahā udakarāsi mahā udakaṇṇavau hauti| bhikkhavai, cakkhu purisassa samuddau hauti, tassa cakkhussa rūpamayau rūpāyatanamayau vaigau| kainaṭṭhaina? pathavitau yāva akaniṭṭhabrahmalaukā nīlādirūpārammaṇaṃ samausarantampi duppūraṇaṭṭhaina ca anamataggai saṃsārai saṃsaraṇaṭṭhaina ca duratik‍kamanaṭṭhaina ca cakkhumaiva samuddau hauti| nīlādirūpāyatanassa appamaiyyassa appamaiyyaina ūmimayaina vaigaina saṃsaraṇaṭṭhaina nīlādirūpamaiva vaigau hauti| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ cakkhu samuddau rūpamayau vaigau ca saṃkilaisau saṃkilaisahaitu dassitau|

‘‘kaina asaikkhau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘yau ta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| yasmiṃ arahattaphalai ṭhitau yau arahā rūpamayaṃ taṃ vaigaṃ sahati manāpai rūpai rāgaṃ, amanāpai rūpai dausaṃ, asamapaikkhanai mauhaṃ anuppādaintau hutvā upaikkhakabhāvaina sahati, ayaṃ arahā, bhikkhavai, saūmiṃ sāvaṭṭaṃ sagahaṃ sarakkhasaṃ cakkhusamuddaṃ atarīti vuc‍cati, tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇauti vuc‍cati| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ arahattaphalabhūtau asaikkhau dassitau|

‘‘sautaṃ  bhikkhavai’’tiādīsupi iminā nayaina yathāsambhavaṃ atthau vaiditabbau| idaṃ ‘‘samuddau’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgai visayai ca asaikkhabhāgai visayai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nāma|

‘‘aittakamaivā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘chayimai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhikkhavai, laukasmiṃ sattānaṃ anayāya anatthāya pāṇīnaṃ byābādhāya imai mayā vuc‍camānā baḷisā cha bhavanti| katamai cha? bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaina viñ‍ñaiyyā iṭṭhā kantā kāmanīyā manāpā manavaḍḍhakā piyarūpā piyasabhāvā kāmūpasaṃhitā kilaisakāmasahitā rajanīyā rūpā nīlādirūpārammaṇā santi, taṃ vuttappakāraṃ rūpaṃ bhikkhu cai abhinandati sappītikataṇhāya abhimukhau nandati, cai abhivadati ‘‘ahau sukhaṃ ahau sukha’’nti vadāpaintiyā taṇhāyanavasaina abhivadati, cai ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati gilitvā viya pariniṭṭhapaitvā tiṭṭhati, aivaṃ sati, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ bhikkhu ‘‘gilitabaḷisau…pai॰… pāpimatau’’ti vuc‍cati| aittha ca nīlādibhaidaina anaikavidhattā ‘‘rūpā iṭṭhā…pai॰… rajanīyā’’ti bahuvacananiddaisau kataupi rūpāyatanarūpārammaṇabhāvaina ca cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyabhāvaina ca aikavidhataṃ anativattanatau ‘‘ta’’nti aikavacananiddaisau katauti vaiditabbau| ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā’’tiādīsupi iminā nayaina yathāsambhavaṃ atthau vaiditabbau| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyādikau chabbidhau baḷisau saṃkilaisau kilaisahaitu dassitau|

‘‘kaina asaikkhau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘santi cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

abhaidi bhindi, paribhaidi parisamantatau bhindi| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ arahattaphalabhūtau asaikkhau dassitau| idaṃ ‘‘chayimai’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ vuttanayaina saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nāma|

109. nānāvidhaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca sutta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

ayaṃ  laukau sattalaukau santāpajātau ñātibyasanādivasaina jātasaukasantāpau caiva rāgādivasaina  jātapariḷāhasantāpau ca phassaparaitau anaikaihi dukkhasamphassaihi abhibhūtau raudaṃ raudantau vadati| kinti vadati? attanā phuṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṃ abhāvitakāyatāya adhivāsaituṃ asak‍kauntau hutvā ‘‘ahau dukkhaṃ, īdisaṃ dukkhaṃ mayhaṃ sattunaupi mā hautū’’tiādinā raudantau vilapantau vadati, ‘‘kasmā aivaṃ vadatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘attatau yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñantī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aitai sattā yaina yaina kāmajjhausādinā pakāraina attatau dukkhassa paṭikāraṃ mañ‍ñanti, tatau pakāratau añ‍ñathā añ‍ñaina pakāraina taṃ dukkhaṃ tikicchitabbaṃ hi yasmā hauti|

añ‍ñathābhāvīti yasmā raudaṃ raudantau vadati, yaina yaina vā paravihiṃsādipakāraina attanau vaḍḍhiṃ mañ‍ñanti āsīsanti, tatau pakāratau añ‍ñathā avaḍḍhi aiva hauti| taṃ āsīsitabbaṃ añ‍ñathābhāvi avaḍḍhitabhāvi aiva hi yasmā hauti, tasmā mañ‍ñitabbassa āsīsitabbassa añ‍ñathā bhavanasīlattā raudaṃ raudantau vadati, ayaṃ sattalaukau raudantau ca hutvā vadati| ‘‘ki’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘bhavasattau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhavasattau kāmabhavādīsu sattau visattau laukau bhavamaiva kāmabhavādibhavamaiva abhinandati| yaṃ bhavaṃ abhinandati, taṃ jarāmaraṇādianaikabyasanānubandhattā bhayānakaṭṭhaina bhayaṃ hauti| yassa yatau jarāmaraṇāditau bhāyati, taṃ jarāmaraṇādidukkhassa adhiṭṭhānabhāvatau dukkhaṃ dukkhādhiṭṭhānaṃ hauti| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ santāpādikau saṃkilaisau saṃkilaisahaitu dassitau|

saṃkilaisau dassitau, ‘‘kaina nibbaidhau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘bhavavippahānāya khau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhavavippahānāya khau pana kāmabhavādikassa bhavassa pajahanatthāya aiva idaṃ mayā adhigataṃ maggabrahmacariyaṃ vussati| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ brahmacariyabhūtau maggau nibbaidhau dassitau|

‘‘bhavavippahānāyā’’tiādinā aikantaina niyyānikamaggau nibbaidhau ācariyaina dassitau, amhaihi ca ñātau, ‘‘katamau aniyyānikamaggau’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘yai hi kaici samaṇā vā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| hi-saddau vācāsiliṭṭhatthau| yai kaici samaṇā vā yai kaici brāhmaṇā vā bhavaina  rūpabhavaina bhavassa kāmabhavassa vippamaukkhaṃ āhaṃsu, bhavaina arūpabhavaina bhavassa kāmabhavassa caiva rūpabhavassa ca vippamaukkhaṃ āhaṃsu, sabbai tai samaṇā vā sabbai tai brāhmaṇā vā bhavasmā vuttappakārabhavatau avippamuttā bhavantīti ahaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘rūpabhavādinā  yai ca kāmabhavādibhavassa vippamaukkhaṃ āhaṃsū’’ti vattabbattā vibhavaina bhavassa nissaraṇaṃ āhaṃsūti dassaituṃ ‘‘yai vā panā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yai vā pana kaici samaṇā vā yai vā pana kaici brāhmaṇā vā vibhavaina ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā bhavassa saṃsārabhavassa nissaraṇaṃ āhaṃsu, sabbai tai samaṇā vā sabbai tai brāhmaṇā vā bhavasmā saṃsārabhavatau anissaṭāva hauntīti ahaṃ vadāmi| ‘‘kasmā anissaṭā’’ti vattabbattā anissaṭakāraṇaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘upadhiṃ hī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ dukkhaṃ saṃsāradukkhaṃ upadhiṃ khandhūpadhikilaisūpadhiabhisaṅkhārūpadhayau paṭic‍ca hi yasmā sambhauti, tasmā anissaṭā haunti| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ micchādiṭṭhisaṅkhātau saṃkilaisau dassitau|

yai hi ‘‘kaicī’’tiādinā saṃkilaisau dassitau, ‘‘kaina nibbaidhau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘sabbupādānakkhayā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| sabbupādānakkhayā ariyamaggatau dukkhassa saṃsāradukkhassa sambhavau natthi| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ ariyamaggau nibbaidhau dassitau|

‘‘vuttappakārā añ‍ñasuttappadaisainapi dassitau’’ti dassaituṃ ‘‘laukamima’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| puthū visuṃ visuṃ avij‍jāya paraitaṃ abhibhūtaṃ bhūtarataṃ bhūtaisu itthipurisaisu añ‍ñamañ‍ñarataṃ bhūtaṃ khandhapañ‍cakaṃ bhavaihi aparimuttaṃ imaṃ laukaṃ mama citta tvaṃ passa| yai kaici bhavā ittarakhaṇā vā bhavā, dīghāyukā vā bhavā, sātavantau vā bhavā, asātavantau vā bhavā pañ‍cakkhandhā sabbadhi ‘‘uddhaṃ adhau tiriya’’nti imaisu sabbaisu sabbatthatāya sabbatthabhāvaina ahaisuṃ, sabbai tai vuttappakārā bhavā nic‍cadhuvarahitattā anic‍cā sampīḷitattā dukkhā vipariṇāmabhāvatau vipariṇāmadhammā ahaisuṃ| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ avij‍jādikau saṃkilaisau dassitau|

‘‘laukamima’’ntiādinā saṃkilaisau dassitau, ‘‘kaina nibbaidhau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘aivamaita’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| aivaṃ vuttappakāraina aitaṃ khandhapañ‍cakaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sassa attanau maggapañ‍ñāya, vipassanāpañ‍ñāya vā passatau passantassa puggalassa bhavataṇhā pahīyati, sammappañ‍ñāya yathābhūtaṃ khandhapañ‍cakaṃ passantau vibhavaṃ ucchaidadiṭṭhiṃ nābhinandati na patthaiti| tassa puggalassa sabbasau taṇhānaṃ khayā asaisavirāganiraudhau asaisavirāgasaṅkhātaina maggaina niraudhau nirujjhanaṃ nibbānaṃ nibbuti hauti| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ maggau nibbaidhau dassitau|

‘‘aivamaita’’ntiādinā  nibbaidhau dassitau, ‘‘kaina asaikkhau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tassa nibbutassā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taṇhādiṭṭhinibbutassa tassa bhikkhunau anupādā kilaisābhisaṅkhārānaṃ anuppādanatau aggahaṇatau punabbhavau na hauti, aivaṃbhūtaina ariyapuggalaina pubbai attānaṃ abhibhūtau pañ‍cavidhau mārau vijitau ahausi, anaina ariyapuggalaina pañ‍cahi māraihi saṅgāmau vijitau, saṅgāmai, iṭṭhāniṭṭhādīsu vā tādī tādilakkhaṇappattau ariyapuggalau sabbabhavāni upac‍cagā atik‍kantauva jātau| iti iminā suttappadaisaina ayaṃ asaikkhau dassitau| idaṃ vuttappakāraṃ ‘‘ayaṃ laukau’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgai visayai ca nibbaidhabhāgai visayai ca asaikkhabhāgai visayai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina aikadaisavasaina pavattanatau saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nāma|

‘‘aittakamaivā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘cattāraumai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| andhaputhuj‍janau saṃsārasautassa anukūlabhāvaina gacchanatau anusautagāmī nāma, kalyāṇaputhuj‍janau saṃsārasautassa nibbidānupassanādīhi paṭik‍kūlavasaina pavattanatau paṭisautagāmī nāma, saikkhau acalappasādādisaman‍nāgamaina ṭhitasabhāvattā ṭhitattau nāma, asaikkhau saṃsārapāraṅgatavasaina tiṭṭhanatau ‘‘thalai tiṭṭhatī’’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘taisu catūsu puggalaisu katamau puggalau saṃkilaisabhāgiyādī’’ti vattabbabhāvatau ‘‘tattha yauya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

110. nānāvidhaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katama’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

abhijātiyau  puggalā cha saṃvij‍janti laukasmiṃ, kaṇhai nīcai kulai nibbattau kaṇhābhijātikau, kaṇhadhammasaman‍nāgatattā vā kaṇhau kaṇhābhijātikau hutvā kaṇhaṃ kāḷakaṃ dasavidhaṃ dussīlyadhammaṃ abhijāyati pasavati, aisau puggalau atthi| vuttappakāraina kaṇhau kaṇhābhijātikau hutvā suk‍kaṃ dasavidhaṃ kusaladhammaṃ abhijāyati, aisau puggalau atthi| kaṇhau kaṇhābhijātikau hutvā akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ ac‍cantadiṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārādhaiti, aisau puggalau atthi| vuttavipariyāyaina tayau puggalā jānitabbā|

‘‘taisu  katamai puggalā saṃkilaisabhāgiyā’’tiādinā vattabbattā ‘‘tattha yau cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

‘‘aittakamaivā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘cattārimānī’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

111. nānāvidhaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

mānusattaṃ manussabhāvaṃ laddhāna kic‍caṃ, akic‍cañ‍ca dvai bhavanti, dvai kic‍cāniyaiva kattabbāni| taināha aṭṭhakathācariyau (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 111) ‘‘kattabba’’nti dassaiti| ‘‘katamaṃ kattabbaṃ kic‍ca’’nti vattabbattā kattabbakic‍caṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘sukic‍caṃ caivā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| puñ‍ñāni ca kattabbattā sukic‍caṃ aiva, saṃyaujanavippahānaṃ vā kattabbattā sukic‍caṃ nāmāti yaujanā|

‘‘tattha suttai katamaina katamau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘sukic‍caṃcaiva puñ‍ñānīti vāsanā, saṃyaujanavippahānaṃ vāti nibbaidhau’’ti vuttau|

‘‘aittakamaivā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘puñ‍ñāni karitvānā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| puñ‍ñāni karitvāna katapuñ‍ñā puggalā saggā saggatau saggaṃyaiva vajanti| saṃyaujanappahānā ariyā jarāmaraṇā jarāmaraṇatau vippamuc‍canti|

‘‘tattha suttai katamaina katamau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘puñ‍ñāni karitvāna, saggā saggaṃ vajanti katapuñ‍ñā’ti vāsanā, ‘saṃyaujanappahānā, jarāmaraṇā vippamuc‍cantī’ti nibbaidhau’’ti vuttaṃ|

‘‘aittakamaivā’’ti  vattabbattā ‘‘dvaimānī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘katamaina katamau dassitau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha yau…pai॰… ayaṃ nibbaidhau’’ti vuttaṃ|

nānāvidhaṃ vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ taṇhāsaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha taṇhāsaṃkilaisabhāgiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu sauḷasasu saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīsu suttaisu taṇhāsaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ taṇhāpakkhainaiva niddisitabbaṃ, bahuvisayattā niyamaitvāna niddhāraissāmīti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘kaina pakāraina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘tīhi taṇhāhī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhavataṇhāyāti rūpabhavataṇhāya| vibhavataṇhāyāti arūpabhavataṇhāya| yaina yaina vā pana vatthunā taṇhāpabhaidaucchaidādivatthunā ajjhausitā bhavataṇhādivasaina ajjhausitā, taina taina pakāraina taṇhādinā vā taṇhāpabhaidaucchaidādivatthunā vā taṇhāsaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ niddisitabbaṃ|

taṇhāsaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ taṇhāpakkhainaiva niddisitabbanti ācariyaina vuttaṃ, amhaihi ca lakkhitaṃ, ‘‘diṭṭhisaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ kaina pakkhaina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha diṭṭhisaṃkilaisabhāgiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| yaina yaina vā pana vatthunāti diṭṭhippabhaidaamarāvikkhaipādivatthunā|

diṭṭhisaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ diṭṭhipakkhainaiva niddisitabbanti ācariyaina vuttaṃ, amhaihi ca lakkhitaṃ, ‘‘duc‍caritasaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ kaina pakāraina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha duc‍caritasaṃkilaisabhāgiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tathaiva vattabbattā ‘‘tattha taṇhāvaudānabhāgiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ, ‘‘diṭṭhivaudānabhāgiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ, ‘‘duc‍caritavaudānabhāgiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

112. yasmiṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānai aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā daṭṭhabbā, taṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ sauḷasahi saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīhi suttaihi aikadaisaniddhāraṇavasaina vibhajitaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘kiṃ pana taṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ taihi sauḷasahi aiva saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīhi vibhajitabbaṃ, udāhu añ‍ñaihi suttaihipi vibhajitabba’’nti vattabbattā añ‍ñaihi aṭṭhavīsasuttaihipi vibhajituṃ ‘‘tattha katamai aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā? laukiyaṃ laukuttara’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘yadi añ‍ñaihipi aṭṭhavīsasuttaihi vibhajitabbaṃ, aivaṃ sati ‘laukiya’ntiādivacanamaiva vattabbaṃ, kasmā ‘tattha katamai aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā’ti vuttā’’ti cai? tassa sāsanapaṭṭhānavibhāgau aṭṭhārasahi mūlapadaihi saṅgahitau, aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāpi vibhajitai sāsanapaṭṭhānai daṭṭhabbā, tasmā mūlapadā vibhattāyaiva| tāni mūlapadāni vibhajituṃ ‘‘tattha katamai aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā’’ti vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 112) pana –

‘‘aivaṃ  sauḷasavidhaina sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ nānāsuttaihi udāharaṇavasaina vibhajitvā idāni aṭṭhavīsatividhaina sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ dassaintaina yasmā ayampi paṭṭhānavibhāgau mūlapadaihi saṅgahitau, na imassāpi taihi asaṅgahitau padaisau atthi, tasmā mūlapadaṃ, vibhajitabbatañ‍ca dassaituṃ ‘tattha katamai aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā’ti pucchāya vasaina mūlapadāni uddharitvā ‘laukiyaṃ laukuttara’ntiādinā nava tikā, thavau cāti aṭṭhavīsatividhaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ uddiṭṭha’’nti –

vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu sauḷasahārapañ‍cakanayaaṭṭhārasamūlapadaisu katamāni padāni mūlapadāni hauntīti pucchati| laukai niyuttau sabhāvadhammauti laukiyau, laukai vā vidūhi viditau sabhāvautipi laukiyau, laukiyau sabhāvadhammau assa visaisasuttassa atthīti taṃ visaisasuttaṃ laukiyaṃ nāma| aisa nayau ‘‘laukuttara’’ntiādīsupi vaiditabbau| jānātīti ñāṇaṃ, ñāṇaṃ assa visaisasuttassa atthīti ñāṇaṃ| ñātabbāti ñaiyyā, ñaiyyā assa visaisasuttassa atthīti ñaiyyaṃ| aisaiva nayau – ‘‘ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍cā’’ti aitthāpi vaiditabbau| nibbānaṃ paṭhamaṃ passatīti dassanaṃ, paṭhamamaggañāṇaṃ, dassanaṃ assa visaisasuttassa atthīti dassanaṃ| bhāvanā assa visaisasuttassa pāḷiyā atthīti bhāvanā| ‘‘dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā cā’’ti aitthāpi aisa nayau vaiditabbau| sassa attanau vacananti sakaṃ, sakaṃ vacanaṃ sakavacanaṃ, bhagavatau vacananti atthau| parassa vacanaṃ paravacanaṃ| vissaj‍janīyau assa visaisasuttassa atthīti vissaj‍janīyaṃ| natthi vissaj‍janīyau assa visaisasuttassāti avissaj‍janīyaṃ| kammaṃ assa visaisasuttassa atthīti kammaṃ| vipākau assa pāṭhassa atthīti vipākau| saisaisupi assatthiatthau gahaitabbau| atha vā laukiyādiatthau mukhyatthau, taṃvācakasuttampi ṭhānyūpacāraina vuttaṃ| buddhādīnaṃ guṇai abhitthavati aitaina suttappadaisaināti thavau, suttappadaisau|

‘‘taisu aṭṭhavīsatividhaisu laukiyādīsu sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttaisu katamaṃ suttaṃ laukiyaṃ sutta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ laukiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu aṭṭhavīsatividhaisu laukiyādīsu sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttaisu katamaṃ suttaṃ laukiyaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ suttanti pucchati|

bhikkhavai, dhainuyā thanaihi nikkhantaṃ saj‍jukhīraṃ nikkhantakkhaṇai na muc‍cati na pariṇamati khīrabhāvaṃ pajahitvā dadhibhāvaṃ na pāpuṇāti, tak‍kādiambilasamāyaugatau pacchā khīrabhāvaṃ pajahati dadhibhāvaṃ pāpuṇāti iva, aivaṃ yaina bālaina pāpaṃ yaṃ kammaṃ kataṃ, taṃ kammaṃ karaṇakkhaṇai  tassa bālassa apāyadukkhādinibbattāpanavasaina na vipac‍cati| dutiyai pana vā tatiyādimhi vā attabhāvai vipac‍cati| bhasmacchan‍nau chārikāya paṭicchan‍nau pāvakau aggi ak‍kantaṃ janaṃ ak‍kamanakkhaṇai na ḍahati| chārikaṃ pana tāpaitvā kālantarai ḍahati iva, aivaṃ yaina bālaina pāpaṃ yaṃ kammaṃ kataṃ, taṃ kammaṃ karaṇakkhaṇai taṃ bālaṃ apāyadukkhādinibbattāpanavasaina ḍahāpaintaṃ hutvā na anvaiti| dutiyai vā tatiyādimhi vā attabhāvai apāyadukkhādinibbattāpanavasaina ḍahāpaintaṃ hutvā ḍahantaṃ taṃ bālaṃ taṃ anvaitīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘na hi…pai॰… pāvakau’’ti suttaṃ laukiyai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau laukiyaṃ nāma|

‘‘aittakamaiva laukiya’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘cattārimānī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| atthau pākaṭau| idaṃ ‘‘cattārimāni…pai॰… kāḷapakkhaiva candimā’’ti suttaṃ laukiyai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau laukiyaṃ nāma|

‘‘aivaṃ duvidhaṃyaiva laukiya’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, laukadhammā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| atthau pākaṭau| idaṃ ‘‘aṭṭhimai’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ laukiyaisu aṭṭhavidhaisu atthaisu vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau laukiyaṃ nāma|

nānāvidhaṃ laukiyaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ suttaṃ laukuttara’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ laukuttara’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| itau paraisupi aisa nayau vaiditabbau| tatthāti taisu aṭṭhavīsatividhaisu laukiyādīsu suttaisu|

chaikaina sārathinā sudantā assā samathaṅgatā yathā, aivaṃ pahīnamānassa pahīnanavavidhamānassa yassa anāsavassa bhikkhunau indriyāni chabbidhāni cakkhundriyādīni samathaṅgatāni| tādinau tādilakkhaṇaina saman‍nāgatassa anāsavassa tassa bhikkhunau daivāpi manussāpi pihayantīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘yassindriyānī’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ laukuttarai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau laukuttaraṃ nāma|

‘‘aittakamaivā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘pañ‍cimāni bhikkhavai indriyānī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ ‘‘pañ‍cimānī’’tiādikaṃ suttampi laukuttarai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau laukuttaraṃ nāma| (1)

‘‘laddhāna  mānusattaṃ dvai, kic‍caṃ akic‍camaiva cā’’tiādikā dvai gāthā vuttā| iha gāthāsu ‘‘sukic‍caṃ caiva puñ‍ñānī’’ti yaṃ gāthāpadañ‍ca ‘‘puñ‍ñāni karitvāna, saggā saggaṃ vajanti katapuñ‍ñā’’ti yaṃ gāthāpadañ‍ca vuttaṃ, idaṃ gāthāpadaṃ laukiyai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau laukiyaṃ nāma|

iha gāthāsu ‘‘saṃyaujanavippahānaṃ vā’’ti yaṃ gāthāpadañ‍ca ‘‘saṃyaujanavippahānā, jarāmaraṇā vippamuc‍cantī’’ti yaṃ gāthāpadañ‍ca vuttaṃ, idaṃ gāthāpadaṃ vuttanayaina laukuttaraṃ nāma| idaṃ ‘‘laddhānā’’tiādikaṃ vuttappakāraina laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca|

bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai āhārai āhārapaṭibaddhai chandarāgai sati nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti hauti| ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇai…pai॰… hautī’’ti idaṃ vuttanayaina laukiyaṃ nāma| ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇai…pai॰… niraudhau’’ti idaṃ suttaṃ laukuttaraṃ nāma| idaṃ ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇai cai bhikkhavai’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ laukiyai atthai ca laukuttarai atthai ca aikadaisavasaina vācakañāpakavasaina pavattanatau laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca| (2)

113. sattalaukai sabbā disā anuparigamma k‍vaci disāyaṃ caitasā attanā attatau piyataraṃ añ‍ñaṃ naiva ajjhagā, attāva piyatarau yathā, aivaṃ paraisaṃ sattānaṃ puthu visuṃ visuṃ attāva piyau piyatarau, tasmā attanauva piyatarattā attakāmau attanau hitakāmau paṇḍitau sattalaukau attānaṃ upamaṃ katvā paraṃ na hiṃsai na hiṃsaiyyāti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘sabbā disā’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ sattaisu vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ nāma|

yai kaici khīṇāsavā puggalā bhūtāva na bhavissanti, sabbai tai khīṇāsavā puggalā daihaṃ attabhāvaṃ pahāya nibbānaṃ gamissanti| yai ca puthuj‍janādayau sattā punabbhavaisu bhavissanti, sabbai tai puthuj‍janādayau sattā daihaṃ attabhāvaṃ pahāya paralaukaṃ gamissanti, taṃ sabbajāniṃ sabbassa sattassa hāniṃ maraṇaṃ, vināsaṃ vā kusalau yau puggalau vijānāti, sau kusalau puggalau taṃ sabbajāniṃ viditvā ātāpiyau brahmacariyaṃ caraiyyāti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘yai kaicī’’tiādikaṃ vuttanayaina sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

sattahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kalyāṇamittaṃ yāvajīvaṃ kusalaina puggalaina na vijahitabbaṃ| katamaihi sattahi? parisuddhasīlasampattiparisuddhadiṭṭhisampattīhi saman‍nāgatattā piyau ca piyāyitabbau  ca hauti, pāsāṇachattaṃ viya garu ca hauti, sambhāvaitabbatāya bhāvanīyau ca hauti, ‘‘kālaina vadāmi, nau akālainā’’tiādikai pañ‍cadhammai attani upaṭṭhāpaitvā sabrahmacārīnaṃ vā sissānaṃ vā vinicchayaul‍lumpanaauvādadānabhāvai ṭhatvā vattā ca hauti, sabrahmacārīhi vā sissādīhi vā vuc‍camānau suvacau hutvā taisaṃ vacanakkhamau ca hauti, sac‍capaṭic‍casamuppādādigambhīraṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ gambhīraṃ vā kathaṃ kattā ca hauti, dhammavinayādivasainaiva dīpanatau aṭṭhānai ca na niyaujakau hauti| imaihi sattahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kalyāṇamittaṃ yāvajīvaṃ na vijahitabbaṃ| idaṃ ‘‘sattahī’’tiādivacanaṃ bhagavā avauca| idaṃ ‘‘sattahī’’tiādikaṃ vacanaṃ sugatau vatvā athāparaṃ aitaṃ gāthāvacanaṃ satthā avauca| kiṃ avauca?

‘‘piyau garu bhāvanīyau, vattā ca vacanakkhamau|

gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā, na caṭṭhānai niyaujakau|

taṃ mittaṃ mittakāmaina, yāvajīvampi saiviya’’nti|| –

aitaṃ  gāthāvacanaṃ satthā avaucāti yaujanā| ‘‘na ca aṭṭhānayaujakau’’ti pāṭhau atthi| idaṃ ‘‘sattahī’’tiādikaṃ vacanaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

laukai yaṃ kāmasukhañ‍ca yaṃ idaṃ diviyaṃ sukhañ‍ca atthi, aitai kāmasukhadiviyasukhā taṇhākkhayasukhassa sauḷasiṃ kalaṃ na agghantīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘yañ‍cā’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

yattha nibbānai dukkhaṃ nirujjhati, sammāsambuddhadaisitaṃ asaukaṃ virajaṃ khaimaṃ taṃ nibbānaṃ susukhaṃ vatāti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘susukha’’ntiādikaṃ suttampi dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ| (3)

tīsu bhavaisu sattānaṃ jananatau taṇhāsaṅkhātaṃ mātarañ‍ca, pitaraṃ nissāya mānassa uppaj‍janatau mānasaṅkhātaṃ pitarañ‍ca, raṭṭhai laukau raṭṭhissaraṃ rājānaṃ bhajati viya dvin‍naṃ sassatucchaidadiṭṭhīnaṃ sabbadiṭṭhigataihi bhajanīyattā sassatucchaidadiṭṭhisaṅkhātai khattiyai dvai rājānau ca, āyasādhakau purisau raṭṭhai atthaṃ anucarati iva nandirāgassa dvādasāyatanai anucaraṇatau nandirāgasaṅkhātaina anucaraṇaina saha pavattanaṭṭhānaṃ dvādasāyatanasaṅkhātaṃ raṭṭhañ‍ca khīṇāsavau yau brāhmaṇau hanati, sau brāhmaṇau hantvā anīghau niddukkhau hutvā yātīti yaujanā| iha ‘‘mātara’’ntiādigāthāyaṃ  ‘‘mātaraṃ…pai॰… hantvā’’ti idaṃ gāthāvacanaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘anīghau yāti brāhmaṇau’’ti idaṃ gāthāvacanaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ| idaṃ ‘‘mātara’’ntiādikaṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca|

‘‘cattāraumai bhikkhavai iddhipādā’’ti idaṃ suttappadaisavacanaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ| sau catūhi iddhipādaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau kāyaipi karajakāyaipi cittaṃ pādakajjhānacittaṃ samaudahati pakkhipati, sau puggalau dissamānakāyaina gantukāmau karajakāyagatikaṃ pādakajjhānacittaṃ adhiṭṭhahati| cittaipi pādakajjhānacittaipi kāyaṃ karajakāyaṃ samaudahati pakkhipati, sau puggalau sīghaṃ añ‍ñaṃ gantukāmau pādakajjhānacittagatikaṃ karajakāyaṃ adhiṭṭhahati| kāyai karajakāyai sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca sukhavihārasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca lahugamanasañ‍ñañ‍ca auk‍kamitvā añ‍ñaṃ gamanaiyyaṃ icchitaṭṭhānaṃ aikacittakkhaṇainaiva ca gantvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ ‘‘sau’’tiādikaṃ suttappadaisavacanaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ| idaṃ ‘‘cattāraumai’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca| (4)

114. yaṃ  sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ laukuttaraṃ laukaṃ uttaritvā abhibhavitvā ṭhitaṃ, yaina sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina bhagavā ‘‘sabbañ‍ñū’’ti vuc‍cati, tassa sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassa parihānaṃ natthi, taṃ sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ sabbakālai jānituṃ āvaj‍janakālai pavattatīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘yaṃ ta’’ntiādikaṃ ñāṇai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvainapi pavattanatau ñāṇaṃ nāma|

yāya nibbānagāminiyā maggapañ‍ñāya jātimaraṇasaṅkhayaṃ pajānāti, sā nibbānagāminī maggapañ‍ñā sabbāhi laukiyāhi pañ‍ñāhi saiṭṭhā pasatthāti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘pañ‍ñā hī’’tiādikaṃ vuttanayaina ñāṇaṃ nāma|

‘‘dhautaka, vau tumhākaṃ santiṃ ahaṃ kittayissāmī’’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘‘diṭṭhai dhammai dukkhādidhammai vā attabhāvai vā satau anic‍cānupassanādisatisampan‍nau hutvā caraṃ carantau yaugāvacarau anītihaṃ yaṃ santiṃ yaṃ nibbānaṃ ariyamaggaina viditvā laukai saṃsāralaukai visattikaṃ visappakaṃ taṇhaṃ tarai taraiyyā’’ti bhagavā avauca|

dhautakau bhagavantaṃ vadati ‘‘mahaisi mahantai sīlakkhandhādī aisanasīla, gautama, satau ‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’tiādisaraṇasampan‍nau hutvā caraṃ carantau yaugāvacarau uttamaṃ yaṃ santiṃ yaṃ  nibbānaṃ ariyamaggaina viditvā laukai saṃsāralaukai visattikaṃ taṇhaṃ tarai taraiyyāti tañ‍ca vacanaṃ tañ‍ca santiṃ nibbānaṃ ahaṃ abhinandāmi abhipatthayāmi, mahaisi tvaṃ, yañ‍ca sampajānāsī’’ti dhautakau bhagavantaṃ vadati|

‘‘dhautakā’’ti ālapitvā bhagavā dhautakaṃ avauca| ‘‘uddhaṃ anāgataṃ upari adhau atītaṃ haiṭṭhā ca tiriyañ‍cāpi majjhai pac‍cuppan‍naṃ paritau ca laukai saṃsāralaukai aitaṃ taṇhaṃ bhavābhavāya khuddakabhavamahantabhavatthāya saṅgau laggauti viditvā vicarantau tvaṃ taṇhaṃ mākāsi mā akāsī’’ti bhagavā dhautakaṃ avaucāti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘kittayissāmī’’tiādikaṃ ñaiyyai visayai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau ñaiyyaṃ nāma|

‘‘aittakamaivā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘catun‍naṃ bhikkhavai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ ariyabhāvakarānaṃ sac‍cānaṃ ananubaudhā abujjhanaina appaṭivaidhā appaṭivijjhanaina aivaṃ iminā kāraṇaina mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca dīghamaddhānaṃ idaṃ sandhāvitaṃ sandhāvanaṃ, idaṃ saṃsaritaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ ahausīti, bhikkhavai, aj‍ja  tayidaṃ taṃ idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ mayā anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ…pai॰… tayidaṃ taṃ idaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ mayā anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, mama bhavataṇhā ucchin‍nā, bhavanaitti taṇhā khīṇā, idāni mama punabbhavau natthi, iti idaṃ ‘‘catun‍na’’ntiādikaṃ bhagavā avauca, sugatau idaṃ ‘‘catun‍na’’ntiādikaṃ vatvā athāparaṃ aitaṃ ‘‘catun‍na’’ntiādigāthāvacanaṃ satthā avaucāti yaujaitvā gāthāyañ‍ca tathaiva yaujanā kātabbā| idaṃ ‘‘catun‍na’’ntiādikaṃ vuttanayaina ñaiyyaṃ nāma| (5)

‘‘rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍ca’’nti idaṃ suttaṃ ñaiyyai rūpādidhammai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau ñaiyyaṃ nāma|

aivaṃ ‘‘rūpaṃ anic‍ca’’ntiādinā pakāraina jānaṃ jānantau aivaṃ ‘‘rūpaṃ anic‍ca’’ntiādinā pakāraina passaṃ passantau ariyasāvakau ariyassa bhagavatau sāvakau ‘‘idaṃ rūpaṃ anic‍ca’’nti rūpaṃ passati, ‘‘ayaṃ vaidanā anic‍cā’’ti vaidanaṃ passati, ‘‘ayaṃ sañ‍ñā anic‍cā’’ti sañ‍ñaṃ passati, ‘‘imai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti saṅkhārai passati, ‘‘idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍ca’’nti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ passati| iti idaṃ suttaṃ rūpādipassanai ñāṇai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau ñāṇaṃ nāma|

sau  ‘‘rūpaṃ anic‍ca’’ntiādinā pakāraina passantau ariyasāvakau rūpaina rūparāgaina parimuc‍cati…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇamhā viñ‍ñāṇarāgamhā parimuc‍catīti dukkhasmā parimuc‍catīti ahaṃ vadāmīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘sau parimuc‍catī’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ ñāṇai ca ñaiyyai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca hauti|

sabbai pañ‍cakkhandhā pac‍cayaihi saṅkharitattā saṅkhārā ādiantavantabhāvatau, anic‍cantikabhāvatau, tāvakālikabhāvatau ca khaṇaparittabhāvatau anic‍cā bhavanti| idaṃ ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti suttappadaisavacanaṃ vuttanayaina ñaiyyaṃ nāma| yadā vipassanākaraṇakālai pañ‍ñāya vipassanāpañ‍ñāya passati anic‍catādikaṃ passati| idaṃ ‘‘yadā pañ‍ñāya passatī’’ti suttappadaisavacanaṃ ñāṇaṃ nāma| atha passanakkhaṇai dukkhai pañ‍cakkhandhai vipassakau nibbindati, nibbindantau puggalau dukkhādijānanādivasaina cattāri sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati, aisau catusac‍capaṭivaidhau visuddhiyā visuddhatthāya maggauti| idaṃ ‘‘athā’’tiādikaṃ suttappadaisavacanaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca hauti|

sauṇāti  sauṇaṃ ālapati| samaṇā bāhirakasamaṇā brāhmaṇā jātibrāhmaṇā anic‍caina rūpaina, dukkhaina rūpaina, vipariṇāmadhammaina rūpaina ‘‘ahaṃ paraihi saiyyau uttamau asmī’’ti vā samanupassanti, ‘‘ahaṃ paraina sadisau samānau asmī’’ti vā samanupassanti, ‘‘ahaṃ paratau hīnau lāmakau asmī’’ti vā samanupassanti, yathābhūtassa adassanā añ‍ñatra vaj‍jaitvā añ‍ñaṃ kiṃ nāma kāraṇaṃ siyā, yathābhūtaṃ adassanatau tāva samanupassanassa añ‍ñaṃ kāraṇaṃ natthi, yathābhūtaṃ adassanamaiva kāraṇanti vaiditabbaṃ| ‘‘anic‍cāya vaidanāyā’’tiādīsupi iminā vuttanayaina vuttanayānusāraina yaujanā kātabbā| idaṃ ‘‘yai hi kaicī’’tiādikaṃ suttappadaisavacanaṃ ñaiyyaṃ nāma| ‘‘yai ca khau kaicī’’tiādikau suk‍kapakkhau pana vuttavipariyāyaina vaiditabbau| idaṃ ‘‘yai ca khau’’tiādikaṃ suttappadaisavacanaṃ ñāṇaṃ nāma| idaṃ ‘‘yai hi kaicī’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ ñāṇai ca ñaiyyai ca atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca hauti| (6)

nānāvidhaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ dassana’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ dassana’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu laukiyādīsu aṭṭhavīsatividhaisu sāsanapaṭṭhānaisu suttaisu katamaṃ suttaṃ dassanaṃ nāmāti pucchati|

115. gambhīrapañ‍ñaina  sabbañ‍ñubuddhaina sudaisitāni saṅkhaipavitthārādīhi taihi taihi nayaihi suṭṭhu daisitāni ariyasac‍cāni yai bhāvitabhāvanā ariyapuggalā pañ‍ñāaubhāsaina vibhāvayanti, tai bhāvitabhāvanā ariyapuggalā daivaraj‍jacak‍kavattiraj‍jādipamādaṭṭhānaṃ āgamma bhusaṃ pamattā kiñ‍cāpi haunti, tathāpi tai bhāvitabhāvanā ariyapuggalā sautāpattimaggañāṇaina abhisaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhaina aṭṭhamabhavādīsu uppaj‍janārahānaṃ nāmarūpānaṃ niruddhattā aṭṭhamakkhattuvasaina aṭṭhamaṃ bhavaṃ na ādiyantīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘yai ariyasac‍cānī’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ dassanai paṭhamamaggañāṇai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau dassanaṃ nāma|

nagaradvārabāhathirakaraṇatthaṃ ummārabbhantarai pathaviyaṃ aṭṭhahatthadasahatthappamāṇaṃ āvāṭaṃ khaṇitvā tasmiṃ āvāṭai ussāpitattā pathavissitau pathavinissitau antaupathavinissitau indakhīlau sāradārumayau thambhau  catubbhi catūhi disāhi āgataihi vātaihi mahāvātaihi asampakampiyau sampakampituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyau siyā yathā, yau sappurisau ariyasac‍cāni avaic‍ca passati, taṃ sappurisaṃ sabbatitthiyavādavātaihi asampakampiyattā tathūpamaṃ ahaṃ vadāmīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘yathindakhīlau’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ vuttanayaina dassanaṃ nāma|

bhikkhavai, catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ariyassa bhagavatau sammāsambuddhassa sāvakau ācikkhituṃ ākaṅkhamānau hutvā attanāva sayamaiva attānaṃ byākaraiyya ‘‘bhau, mama atta ahaṃ idāni khīṇanirayau amhi, khīṇatiracchānayauni amhi…pai॰…. dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’’ti byākaraiyya| caturaṅgasarūpaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘katamaihi catūhī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ ‘‘catūhī’’tiādikaṃ dassanaṃ nāma|

nānāvidhaṃ dassanaṃ niddhāritaṃ, ‘‘katamā bhāvanā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamā bhāvanā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

idha sāsanai yassa ariyasāvakassa ajjhattaṃ kāmabhavai nibbattāpakānaṃ aurambhāgiyasaṃyaujanānaṃ pajahanavasaina ca bahiddhā rūpārūpabhavaisu nibbattāpakānaṃ uddhambhāgiyasaṃyaujanānaṃ pajahanavasaina ca indriyāni saddhindriyādīni indriyāni subhāvitāni ariyamaggabhāvanāvasaina suṭṭhu bhāvitāni bhavanti, bhāvitau bhāvitamaggau sa dantau sau ariyasāvakau imaṃ laukañ‍ca paraṃ laukañ‍ca nibbijjha nibbijjhitvā paṭivijjhitvā kālaṃ maraṇakālai, kālaṃkiriyaṃ vā kaṅkhati patthaitīti yaujanā| ayaṃ ‘‘yassindriyānī’’tiādikā pāḷi bhāvanāya vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau bhāvanā nāma|

dhammapadāni jhānavipassanāmaggaphalanibbānadhammakauṭṭhāsāni| anabhijjhā dhammapadaṃ anabhijjhāsīsaina adhigatajhānavipassanāmagganibbānadhammapadaṃ kauṭṭhāsaṃ| aisa nayau saisaisupi| ayaṃ ‘‘cattārimānī’’tiādikā pāḷi vuttanayaina bhāvanā nāma| (7)

daivaputta chindantau puggalau pañ‍ca aurambhāgiyasaṃyaujanāni haiṭṭhā maggattayaina chindai chindaiyya, pajahantau puggalau pañ‍ca uddhambhāgiyasaṃyaujanāni arahattamaggaina jahai pajahaiyya, bhāvayantau puggalau pañ‍ca  saddhindriyādīni ca uttari bhāvayai bhāvaiyya| pañ‍casaṅgātigau rāgasaṅgadausasaṅgamauhasaṅgamānasaṅgadiṭṭhisaṅgātigau bhikkhu aughatiṇṇauti kāmaughabhavaughadiṭṭhaughaavij‍jaughatiṇṇauti vuc‍cati kathīyatīti yaujanā| ‘‘pañ‍ca chindai pañ‍ca jahai’’ti idaṃ vacanaṃ dassanaṃ nāma| ‘‘pañ‍ca cuttari…pai॰… vuc‍catī’’ti ayaṃ pāḷi bhāvanā nāma| idaṃ ‘‘pañ‍cā’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā ca hauti|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai’’tiādīsu yaujanā pākaṭā| (8)

116. ‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ…pai॰… buddhāna sāsana’’nti idaṃ suttaṃ sammāsambuddhassa vacanabhāvatau sakavacanaṃ nāma| atthau haiṭṭhā vuttauva|

bhikkhavai, bālassa bālalakkhaṇāni bālaupalakkhaṇakāraṇāni bālanimittāni ‘‘ayaṃ bālau’’ti gahaṇakāraṇāni bālāpadānāni bālassa apadānāni paurāṇāni viruḷhāni kammāni yaihi bālalakkhaṇādīhi bālaṃ ‘‘bālau’’ti parai paṇḍitā sañ‍jānanti, imāni bālalakkhaṇāni mayā vuc‍camānāni tīṇi| katamāni tīṇi? bhikkhavai, bālau duc‍cintitacintī ca duc‍cintitaṃ abhijjhābyāpādamicchādassanaṃ cintī ca hauti, dubbhāsitabhāsī dubbhāsitaṃ musāvādādiṃ bhāsī ca hauti. duk‍kaṭakammakārī ca duk‍kaṭaṃ pāṇātipātādikammaṃ kārī ca hauti| bhikkhavai, bālassa…pai॰… bālāpadānāni iminā mayā vuttāni tīṇi khau bhavanti| suk‍kapakkhau pana vuttavipariyāyaina vaiditabbau| idaṃ ‘‘tīṇimāni bhikkhavai’’tiādikaṃ vacanaṃ sammāsambuddhassa vacanabhāvatau sakavacanaṃ nāma|

pathavīsamau  vitthatau sambaudhau nāma natthi| pātaṃ vuṭṭhaṃ udakaṃ ābhusau lābhi gaṇhātīti pātālau, pātālaina samau pātālasamau nin‍nau na vij‍jati| mairusamau un‍natau natthi, cak‍kavattisadisau paurisau natthīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘pathavīsamau’’tiādikaṃ vacanaṃ parassa daivassa vacanabhāvatau paravacanaṃ hauti|

‘‘daivānaṃ inda, tava subhāsitaina jayau hautī’’ti vatvā ‘‘vaipacitti, tava subhāsitaina jayau hautū’’ti vatvā ‘‘vaipacitti, tvaṃ gāthaṃ bhaṇa bhaṇāhī’’ti avauca| bhikkhavai, atha khau asurindau vaipacitti imaṃ vuc‍camānaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi ‘‘tai paṭisaidhakau nau cai assa nau cai bhavaiyya, aivaṃ sati bālā bhiyyau pakujjhaiyyuṃ, tasmā dhīrau paṇḍitau bhusaina daṇḍaina bālaṃ nisaidhayai’’ti|

gāthāya, bhikkhavai, asurindaina vaipacittinā bhāsitāya asurā anumaudiṃsu, daivā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| bhikkhavai, atha khau asurindau vaipacitti daivānaṃ indaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitaṃ vacanaṃ avauca ‘‘daivānaminda, tvaṃ gāthaṃ bhaṇa bhaṇāhī’’ti aitaṃ vacanaṃ avauca| bhikkhavai, atha khau daivānamindau sak‍kau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi ‘‘bālassa paṭisaidhanaṃ paraṃ pac‍catthikaṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā satau satimā yau paṇḍitau kaudhatau upasammati, tassa paṇḍitassa aitadaiva upasamaṃ varanti ahaṃ mañ‍ñai’’ti|

gāthāya, bhikkhavai, daivānamindaina sak‍kaina bhāsitāya daivā anumaudiṃsu, asurā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| bhikkhavai, atha khau daivānamindau sak‍kau asurindaṃ vaipacittiṃ aitaṃ vacanaṃ avauca ‘‘vaipacitti, tvaṃ gāthaṃ bhaṇāhī’’ti aitaṃ vacanaṃ avauca| bhikkhavai, atha khau asurindau vaipacitti imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi ‘‘vāsava yadā titikkhati, tadā naṃ titikkhantaṃ puggalaṃ bālau ‘‘ayaṃ mai bhayā titikkhatī’’ti mañ‍ñati, titikkhāya aitadaiva vaj‍jaṃ ahaṃ passāmi| vāsava gaugaṇau palāyinaṃ gavaṃ ajjhauttharati iva, aivaṃ dummaidhau khamantaṃ bhiyyau ajjhāruhati ajjhauttharati|

gāthāya, bhikkhavai, asurindaina vaipacittinā bhāsitāya asurā anumaudiṃsu, daivā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| jāyamānai ca gauyuddhai paṭhamaṃ dvaiyaiva gauṇā yujjhanti, gaugaṇau pana yāva aikau gauṇau na palāyati, tāva aulaukaintauva tiṭṭhati| yadā ca aikau gauṇau palāyati, tadā sabbau gauṇau taṃ palāyinaṃ gavaṃ bhiyyau ajjhauttharatīti gauyuddhasabhāvau vaiditabbau| ‘‘atha khau’’tiādīnaṃ yaujanatthau pākaṭau|

vaipacitti, yau puggalau titikkhati, taṃ khamantaṃ puggalaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ mai bhayā titikkhatī’’ti kāmaṃ mañ‍ñatu vā, mā mañ‍ñatu vā, taṃ mañ‍ñanaṃ nipphalamaiva| atthā sadatthaparamā bhavanti| khantyā khantitau bhiyyau sadatthau nāma na vij‍jati|

yau balavā santau dubbalassa havai titikkhatīti tassa balavantassa taṃ titikkhanaṃ paramaṃ khantinti sappurisā āhu, dubbalau nic‍caṃ khamati aiva|

yassa  bālassa bālabalaṃ atthi, tassa bālassa bālabalaṃ ‘‘abala’’nti sappurisā āhu, yassa dhammaguttassa yaṃ balaṃ atthi, tassa dhammaguttassa tassa balassa paṭivattā paṭippharitvā vattā na vij‍jati|

vaipacitti, yau puggalau kuddhaṃ paṭhamaṃ kujjhantassa paṭikujjhati, sau paṭikujjhantau puggalau taina paṭikujjhanaina tassa paṭhamaṃ kujjhantassa pāpapuggalassa pāpapuggalatau pāpiyau aiva pāpatarau hīnatarau aiva bhavai|

vaipacitti, yau sappurisau satau satimā paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā paṭhamaṃ kujjhantassa appaṭikujjhantauva bhavai, sau sappurisau duj‍jayaṃ saṅgāmaṃ jaiti nāma, attanau ca parassa ca ubhin‍naṃ atthaṃ carati nāma|

attanau ca parassa ca tikicchantānaṃ ubhin‍naṃ kaudhau upasammati, yai janā dhammassa khantidhammassa atthai, catusac‍cadhammai vā akauvidā bhavanti, tai janā khamantaṃ sappurisaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ bālau’’ti mañ‍ñanti, taisaṃ akauvidānaṃ janānaṃ taṃ mañ‍ñanaṃ nipphalanti|

gāthāsu, bhikkhavai, daivānamindaina sak‍kaina bhāsitāsu daivā anumaudiṃsu, asurā tuṇhī ahaisunti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘bhiyyau bālā’’tiādikaṃ vacanaṃ sak‍kavaipacittīnaṃ vacanabhāvatau paravacanaṃ nāma| (9)

117. pattaṃ  aitarahi adhigataṃ yañ‍ca kāmūpakaraṇaṃ, āyatiṃ pattabbaṃ adhigataṃ yañ‍ca kāmūpakaraṇaṃ atthi, aitaṃ ubhayaṃ rajānukiṇṇaṃ rāgarajādikiṇṇaṃ iti āturassa āturānaṃyaiva puggalānaṃ santikai anusikkhatau anusikkhantassa sikkhāsārā hutvā upaṭṭhahanti, sīlaṃ vataṃ jīvitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ imai sikkhāsārā hutvā upaṭṭhahantīti yai upaṭṭhānasārā puggalā sāratau gahaitvā ṭhitā| aittha sīlaṃ nāma ‘‘na karaumī’’ti auramaṇaṃ| vataṃ nāma bhaujanakic‍cakaraṇādi| jīvitaṃ nāma ājīvau| brahmacariyaṃ nāma maithunavirativisaisabhāvau vaiditabbau| taisaṃ upaṭṭhānasārānaṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ vādau aikau paṭhamau antau lāmakau| yai ca puggalā ‘‘kāmaisu dausau natthī’’ti aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau hutvā ṭhitā, taisaṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ vādau aikau dutiyau antau  lāmakau| ic‍caitai ubhau antā antavādā puggalā kaṭasivaḍḍhanā kaṭasiyau diṭṭhiṃ vaḍḍhainti| aitai ubhau antai attakilamathānuyaugai kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugai aikai puggalā anabhiñ‍ñāya aulīyanti, aikai puggalā atidhāvantīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘yañ‍ca patta’’ntiādikaṃ vacanaṃ paraisaṃ puggalānaṃ vacanabhāvatau paravacanaṃ nāma|

yai ca sammādiṭṭhipuggalā tai ubhau antai attakilamathānuyaugakāmasukhal‍likānuyaugai abhiñ‍ñāya tatra ca antai na ahaisuṃ| taina ca abhijānanaina tai ubhau antai na amañ‍ñiṃsu, taisaṃ sammādiṭṭhipuggalānaṃ vaṭṭaṃ tividhaṃ vaṭṭaṃ pañ‍ñāpanāya natthi| iti aivaṃ idaṃ ‘‘yai cā’’tiādikaṃ vacanaṃ bhagavatau vacanabhāvatau sakavacanaṃ nāma| ayaṃ udānau ‘‘yañ‍ca pattaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñāpanāyā’’ti ayaṃ udānau vuttanayaina sakavacanañ‍ca paravacanañ‍ca hauti|

pasainadi nāma kausalau kausalissarau rājā bhagavantaṃ aitaṃ ‘‘idha mayhaṃ…pai॰… taisaṃ piyau attā’’ti vacanaṃ avauca| bhantai, idha rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa mayhaṃ mama caitasau cittassa aivaṃ parivitak‍kau udapādi, kaisaṃ sattānaṃ attā piyau nu khau, kaisaṃ sattānaṃ attā appiyau nu khau iti aitaṃ parivitak‍kanaṃ udapādi| bhantai, tassa mayhaṃ aitaṃ parivitak‍kanaṃ ahausi, yai ca kaici sattā kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caranti khau…pai॰… manasā duc‍caritaṃ caranti khau, taisaṃ sattānaṃ attā appiyau khau| tai duc‍caritaṃ carantā sattā ‘‘nau attā piyau’’ti aivaṃ kiñ‍cāpi vadaiyyuṃ, atha khau taisaṃ duc‍caritaṃ carantānaṃ sattānaṃ attā appiyauva| taṃ kissa haitu? appiyau appiyassa anatthāya yaṃ duc‍caritaṃ karaiyya, taṃ duc‍caritaṃ tai duc‍caritaṃ carantā sattā attanāva. sayamaiva| attanau anatthāya hi yasmā karaunti, tasmā taisaṃ duc‍caritaṃ carantānaṃ sattānaṃ attā appiyauvāti aitaṃ parivitak‍kanaṃ ahausi|

bhantai, yai ca kaici sattā kāyaina sucaritaṃ caranti khau…pai॰… manasā sucaritaṃ caranti khau, taisaṃ sucaritaṃ carantānaṃ sattānaṃ attā piyau khau, tai sucaritaṃ carantā sattā ‘‘nau attā appiyau’’ti aivaṃ kiñ‍cāpi vadaiyyuṃ, atha khau taisaṃ sucaritaṃ carantānaṃ sattānaṃ attā piyauva| taṃ kissa haitu? piyau piyassa atthāya yaṃ sucaritaṃ caraiyya, taṃ sucaritaṃ tai sucaritaṃ carantā sattā attanāva sayamaiva attanau atthāya hi yasmā karaunti, tasmā taisaṃ sucaritaṃ carantānaṃ sattānaṃ attā piyauvāti aitaṃ parivitak‍kanaṃ ahausi| aitaṃ vacanaṃ avaucāti yaujanā|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ mahārājā’’ti vacanaṃ paṭhamaṃ vatvā ‘‘yai hi kaicī’’tiādikai bhagavatau vuttavacanaipi yaujanā tathaiva kātabbā|

gāthāsu pana yau paṇḍitau attānaṃ ‘‘piya’’nti cai jañ‍ñā, aivaṃ sati sau paṇḍitau naṃ attānaṃ pāpaina kammaina na saṃyujai na saṃyaujaiyya, taṃ vacanaṃ hi sac‍caṃ piyaṃ attānaṃ sulabhaṃ na hauti, duk‍kaṭakārinā sukhaṃ sulabhaṃ na hauti|

antakaina mac‍cunā adhipan‍nassa mānusaṃ bhavaṃ jahatau pajahantassa tassa maraṇamukhai ṭhitassa sattassa kiṃ sakaṃ hauti, maraṇamukhai ṭhitau sau sattau kiñ‍ca ādāya paralaukaṃ gacchati, chāyā gacchantaṃ sattaṃ anapāyinī iva, aivaṃ assa paralaukagatassa sattassa kiñ‍ca anugaṃ hauti|

iti bhagavā aivaṃ pucchati, pucchitvā ‘‘ubhau’’tiādivissaj‍janavacanañ‍ca āha| idha laukai yau mac‍cau yaṃ puñ‍ñañ‍ca yaṃ pāpañ‍ca ubhau kammai kurutai karauti, tassa paralaukagatassa mac‍cassa taṃ puñ‍ñapāpadvayaṃ sakaṃ hauti| paralaukaṃ gatau mac‍cau taṃva puñ‍ñapāpadvayaṃ ādāya paralaukaṃ gacchati, chāyā gacchantaṃ sattaṃ anapāyinī iva, aivaṃ assa paralaukagatassa mac‍cassa taṃva puñ‍ñapāpadvayaṃ anugaṃ hauti|

tasmā paṇḍitau samparāyikaṃ samparāyai phalanibbattāpakaṃ kalyāṇaṃ nicayaṃ nicayantau hutvā karaiyya| pāṇinaṃ paralaukasmiṃ puñ‍ñāni patiṭṭhā haunti, iti bhagavā āhāti yaujanā kātabbā| idaṃ ‘‘rājā pasainadī’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ kausalarañ‍ñau vacanabhāvatau paravacanaṃ hauti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, mahārāja, aivamaitaṃ mahārājā’’tiādikā anugīti bhagavatau vacanabhāvatau sakavacanaṃ hauti| idaṃ dvayaṃ vacanaṃ sakavacanañ‍ca paravacanañ‍ca hauti| (10)
