118. pañhai pucchitai idaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, idaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ pahātabbaṃ, idaṃ maggasac‍caṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ, idaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, phalaṃ vā sacchikātabbaṃ, imai kusalākusalā dhammā aivaṃ kusalākusalabhāvaina gahitā anavaj‍jasāvaj‍jabhāvaina vā gahitā sukhavipākadukkhavipākabhāvaina vā gahitā, idaṃ iṭṭhavipākaṃ idaṃ aniṭṭhavipākaṃ phalaṃ nibbattayaiti aivaṃgahitānaṃ taisaṃ kusalākusaladhammānaṃ ayaṃ vuḍḍhi atthau, ayaṃ hāni atthauti| iti idaṃ ‘‘pañhai pucchitai’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ vissaj‍janīyai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau vissaj‍janīyaṃ nāma|

‘‘uḷārau buddhau bhagavā’’ti iminā padaina buddhauḷārataṃ aikaṃsainaiva aikakauṭṭhāsainaiva niddisai| ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau’’ti iminā padaina dhammasvākkhātataṃ aikaṃsainaiva aikakauṭṭhāsainaiva niddisai| ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nau saṅghau’’ti iminā padaina saṅghasuppaṭipattiṃ aikaṃsainaiva aikakauṭṭhāsainaiva niddisai ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti iminā padaina saṅkhārānic‍cataṃ aikaṃsainaiva niddisai| ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti iminā padaina saṅkhāradukkhataṃ aikaṃsainaiva niddisai| ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti iminā padaina dhammānattataṃ aikaṃsainaiva niddisai| ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anattā’’ti avatvā ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti vuttaina iminā padaina saṅkhāraihi añ‍ñaṃ yaṃ vā pana maggaphalaniraudhasamāpattidhammajātaṃ atthi, taṃ maggaphalaniraudhasamāpattidhammajātampi aivaṃjātiyaṃ aivaṃ aikaṃsabyākaraṇīyanti maggaphalaniraudhasamāpattidhammajātassāpi anattataṃ aikaṃsainaiva niddisaiti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘uḷārau’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ vissaj‍janīyai buddhauḷāratādikai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau vissaj‍janīyaṃ nāma|

naradammasārathi ākaṅkhatau tai bhagavatau manasā sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇasahitādimanasā vicintitaṃ ñaiyyadhammaṃ daivā manussā sabbai pāṇinau na jañ‍ñā na jānaiyyuṃ| santaṃ araṇaṃ samādhiṃ nisaivatau tai bhagavatau manasā vicintitā kasiṇāpi sabbai pāṇinau na jañ‍ñā na jānaiyyuṃ| kasiṇāpi vā kasiṇārammaṇāya pañ‍ñāyapi na jañ‍ñā na jānaiyyuṃ| kasiṇāpīti aittha ca ‘‘kasiṇāyapī’’ti vattabbaipi ya-kāra laupavasaina ‘‘kasiṇāpī’’ti vuttaṃ| atha vā ‘‘yaṃ ākaṅkhati yaṃ ākaṅkhanaṃ karauti, taṃ ākaṅkhanaṃ kiṃ katama’’nti pucchitaṃ pañhaṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ avisayattā  avissaj‍janīyaṃ hautīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘ākaṅkhatau’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ avissaj‍janīyai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau avissaj‍janīyaṃ nāma|

bhagavā sīlakkhandhai sīlakkhandhahaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇau, bhagavā samādhikkhandhai samādhikkhandhahaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇau, bhagavā pañ‍ñākkhandhai  pañ‍ñākkhandhahaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇau, bhagavā vimuttikkhandhai vimuttikkhandhahaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇau, bhagavā vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhai vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhahaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇau, bhagavā iriyāyaṃ kāyavacīsamācārai kāyavacīsamācārahaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇau, bhagavā pabhāvai ānubhāvahaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇau, bhagavā hitaisitāyaṃ maittāhaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇau, bhagavā karuṇāyaṃ karuṇāhaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇau, bhagavā iddhiyaṃ iddhividhahaitu aittakau aitaparimāṇauti avissaj‍janīyauti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘aittakau’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ avissaj‍janīyai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau avissaj‍janīyaṃ nāma|

bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa tathāgatassa laukai uppādā uppādahaitu tiṇṇaṃ ratanānaṃ uppādau sambhavati, āyatiṃ buddharatanassa uppādau sambhavati, aikassa buddharatanassa dharamānakkhaṇaina hi añ‍ñassa buddharatanassa anuppaj‍janatau āyatinti vuttaṃ, dhammaratanassa uppādau sambhavati, saṅgharatanassa uppādau sambhavatīti tīṇi ratanāni| ‘‘tāni tīṇi ratanāni guṇatau kiṃpamāṇānī’’ti pucchitai sati tāni tīṇi ratanāni guṇatau aitaparimāṇānīti na vissaj‍jitabbānīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘tathāgatassā’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ avissaj‍janīyai visayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau avissaj‍janīyaṃ nāma|

buddhavisayau puggalaparau puggalapadhānau pañhau avissaj‍janīyau, buddhavisayāva puggalaparauparañ‍ñutā avissaj‍janīyā| bhikkhavai, avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ, sakiṃ nirayaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ sattānaṃ, sakiṃ tiracchānayauniṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ sattānaṃ, sakiṃ paittivisayaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ sattānaṃ, sakiṃ asurayauniṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ sattānaṃ, sakiṃ daivai sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ sattānaṃ, sakiṃ manussai sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ sattānaṃ pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati na dissati na upalabbhatīti| ‘‘sā pubbā kauṭi katamā’’ti kainaci kataṃ pucchanaṃ avissaj‍janīyaṃ saṃsārassa pubbakauṭiyā abhāvatau avissaj‍janīyaṃ hauti| ‘‘na pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti daisanā sāvakānaṃ ñāṇavaikal‍laina katā, na attanau ñāṇavaikal‍laina| ‘‘na pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti daisanā attanau caiva sāvakānañ‍ca ñāṇavaikal‍laina kātabbā  siyāti caudanaṃ manasi katvā ‘‘duvidhā buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ daisanā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| attā upanaitabbau aitissā daisanāyāti attūpanāyikā| parau upanaitabbau aitissā daisanāyāti parūpanāyikā| ‘‘katamā attūpanāyikā daisanā, katamā parūpanāyikā daisanā’’ti pucchitabbattā niyamaitvā dassaituṃ ‘‘na pañ‍ñāyatī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘na pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti daisanā sāvakānaṃ naitabbānaṃ vasaina daisitattā parūpanāyikā daisanā nāma, ‘‘natthi buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ avijānanā’’ti daisanā attanau naitabbassa vasaina daisitattā attūpanāyikā daisanā nāma| ‘‘natthi buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ avijānanā’’ti vuttattā pubbāya kauṭiyā abhāvatau aiva na pañ‍ñāyatīti viñ‍ñāyati, bhagavatau ñāṇassa pañ‍ñāpanaṃ kātuṃ asamatthattā na pañ‍ñāyatīti na viñ‍ñāyati taina aṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘natthi buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ avijānanāti aitaina purimāya kauṭiyā abhāvatau aiva na pañ‍ñāyati, na tattha ñāṇassa paṭighātauti dassaitī’’ti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 118) vuttaṃ|

‘‘kathaṃ pana buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ avijānanāya natthibhāvauvijānitabbau’’ti vattabbattā avijānanāya natthibhāvaṃ jānāpaituṃ ‘‘yathā bhagavā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhu’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana – ‘‘yaṃ pana atthi, taṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ appamaiyyampi bhagavatau na appamaiyyanti bhagavatau sabbattha appaṭihatañāṇataṃ dassaituṃ ‘yathā bhagavā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbhā’tiādimāhā’’ti vuttaṃ| bhagavā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha ‘‘kīva dīghaṃ nu khau, bhantai, padumai nirayai āyuppamāṇa’’nti pañhaṃ pucchitvā nisin‍naṃ añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ yathā yaina pakāraina ‘‘saiyyathāpi…pai॰… āghātaitvā’’ti aivamāha| tatau taina pakāraina avijānanāya natthibhāvau vijānitabbauti atthau gahaitabbau|

bhikkhu, tvaṃ sal‍lakkhaihi, kaukālikau vīsatikhārikau kausalakau tilavāhau rāsiṃ katvā ṭhapitau, tatau tilatau purisau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina aikamaikaṃ tilaṃ uddharaiyya, sau vīsatikhārikau kausalakau tilavāhau iminā uddharānupak‍kamaina khippataraṃ parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ saiyyathāpi gacchaiyya, aikau abbudau nirayau parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ na tvaiva gacchaiyya| vīsati abbudā nirayā tattakai kālai  parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ saiyyathāpi gacchaiyyuṃ, aivamaiva aikau nirabbudau nirayau tattakai kālai parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ na tvaiva gacchaiyya| aisa nayau saisaisupi| sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā padumaṃ nirayaṃ kaukālikau bhikkhu upapan‍nau khau, bhikkhu, tvaṃ aivaṃ sal‍lakkhaihīti bhagavā āhāti yaujanā| bhagavā ‘‘ayaṃ appamaiyyau ayaṃ asaṅkhyaiyyau’’ti vā na kiñ‍ci āha| ‘‘tasmiṃ appamaiyyai  katamau appamaiyyau, tasmiṃ asaṅkhyaiyyai katamau asaṅkhyaiyyau’’ti kainaci kataṃ pucchanaṃ buddhavisayattā avissaj‍janīyaṃ| idaṃ appamaiyyaasaṅkhyaiyyasuttaṃ vuttanayaina avissaj‍janīyaṃ| (11)

119. ‘‘yadā sau upakau’’tiādīsu yaujanā pākaṭā|

‘‘kathaṃ kaina pakāraina jinau’’ti upakaina kataṃ pucchanaṃ ‘‘kilaisappahānapakāraina jinau’’ti vissaj‍janīyattā vissaj‍janīyaṃ| ‘‘kaina pakāraina jinau’’ti upakaina kataṃ pucchanaṃ ‘‘pāpakānaṃ dhammānaṃ jitattā jinau’’ti vissaj‍janīyattā vissaj‍janīyaṃ| ‘‘katamau jinau’’ti kataṃ pucchanaṃ ‘‘rūpādikau jinau’’ti vā ‘‘rūpādikaṃ muñ‍citvā añ‍ñau jinau’’ti vā vissaj‍jaituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyattā avissaj‍janīyaṃ| ‘‘katamau āsavakkhayau, katamau rāgakkhayau, katamau dausakkhayau, katamau mauhakkhayau’’ti kataṃ pucchanaṃ ‘‘nibbānaṃ āsavakkhayau’’ti vā ‘‘arahattamaggau āsavakkhayau’’ti vā ‘‘arahattaphalaṃ rāgakkhayau’’ti vā iti aivamādinā vissaj‍janīyattā vissaj‍janīyaṃ| ‘‘kittakau āsavakkhayau, kittakau rāgakkhayau, kittakau dausakkhayau, kittakau mauhakkhayau’’ti kataṃ pucchanaṃ ‘‘aittakau aitaparimāṇau āsavakkhayau’’ti aivamādinā avissaj‍janīyattā avissaj‍janīyaṃ| idaṃ vuttappakāraṃ suttaṃ vuttanayaina vissaj‍janīyañ‍ca avissaj‍janīyañ‍ca hauti| (12)

‘‘tathāgatau sattau atthī’’ti pucchanaṃ ‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhai upādāya pañ‍ñāpaitabbau sattabhūtau atthī’’ti vissaj‍janīyattā vissaj‍janīyaṃ| ‘‘atthi rūpa’’nti kataṃ pucchanaṃ rūpassa vissaj‍jamānattā ‘‘āmantā’’ti vissaj‍janīyattā vissaj‍janīyaṃ, ‘‘rūpaṃ tathāgatau’’ti kataṃ pucchanaṃ tathābhāvatau alabbhanatau avissaj‍janīyattā avissaj‍janīyaṃ| ‘‘rūpavā tathāgatau’’tiādīsupi aisa nayau  yathāsambhavaṃ yaujaitabbau| idaṃ vuttappakāraṃ suttaṃ vuttanayaina vissaj‍janīyañ‍ca avissaj‍janīyañ‍ca hauti|

‘‘passati bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānātī’’ti kataṃ pucchanaṃ ‘‘passati bhagavā’’ti vā…pai॰… ‘‘pajānāti bhagavā’’ti vā vissaj‍janīyattā vissaj‍janīyaṃ| ‘‘katamai sattā, katamau tathāgatau’’ti kataṃ pucchanaṃ paramatthatau alabbhanatau avissaj‍janīyattā avissaj‍janīyaṃ|

‘‘atthi  tathāgatau’’ti kataṃ pucchanaṃ haiṭṭhā vuttanayaina vissaj‍janīyaṃ| ‘‘atthi tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti kataṃ pucchanaṃ ‘‘atthi tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vissaj‍jamānai sati ca idhalaukau aiva paralaukauti āpaj‍jati, ‘‘natthi tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vissaj‍jamānai sati ca idhalaukatau añ‍ñau paralaukauti āpaj‍jati, tasmā avissaj‍janīyattā avissaj‍janīyaṃ| idaṃ vuttappakāraṃ suttaṃ vissaj‍janīyai visayai ca avissaj‍janīyai visayai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau vissaj‍janīyañ‍ca avissaj‍janīyañ‍ca hauti|

120. nānāvidhaṃ vissaj‍janīyāvissaj‍janīyasuttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ suttaṃ kamma’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ kamma’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

‘‘antakainādhipan‍nassā’’tiādīsu atthau haiṭṭhā vuttanayattā pākaṭau ‘‘maraṇainābhibhūtassa…pai॰… chāyāva anapāyinī’’ti idaṃ suttaṃ puñ‍ñakammapāpakammadvayai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau kammaṃ nāma|

bhikkhavai, tumhai puna caparaṃ kammaṃ sal‍lakkhaitha| (anuṭṭhānagilānaṃ) anuṭṭhānagilānassa pīṭhasamāruḷhaṃ pīṭhasamāruḷhassa bālaṃ bālassa vā mañ‍casamāruḷhaṃ mañ‍casamāruḷhassa bālaṃ bālassa vā chamāyaṃ bhūmiyaṃ saimānaṃ saimānassa sayantassa bālaṃ bālassa vā kāyaina duc‍caritāni vācāya duc‍caritāni manasā duc‍caritāni pāpakāni yāni kammāni pubbai pubbakālai vā atītai anaikakappakauṭisatasahassai vā assa bālaina katāni, tāni pāpakāni kammāni tamhi pīṭhasamāruḷhādisamayai aulambanti viya upaṭṭhahanti ajjhaulambanti viya upaṭṭhahanti abhippalambanti viya upaṭṭhahanti| bhikkhavai, mahataṃ mahantānaṃ pabbatakūṭānaṃ chāyā sāyanhasamayaṃ pathaviyaṃ saiyyathāpi aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti yathā, bhikkhavai, aivamaiva bālaṃ…pai॰… abhippalambanti| bhikkhavai, tatra tasmiṃ upaṭṭhānākārai upaṭṭhānākārahaitu bālassa katapāpassa aivaṃ parivitak‍kau hauti ‘‘mai mayā kalyāṇaṃ akataṃ vata, mai mayā kusalaṃ akataṃ vata, bhīruttāṇaṃ kataṃ vata, mai mayā pāpaṃ kataṃ vata, mai mayā luddaṃ kataṃ vata, mai mayā kibbisaṃ kataṃ vata, bhau agilāna sappurisa akatakalyāṇānaṃ akatakusalānaṃ akatabhīruttāṇānaṃ katapāpānaṃ kataluddānaṃ katakibbisānaṃ yāvatā gati duggati atthi, taṃ gatiṃ ahaṃ paic‍ca gacchāmī’’ti aivaṃ parivitak‍kau hauti| aivaṃ vitak‍kaintau sau bālau saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jatīti yaujanā|

pabbatakūṭānaṃ  chāyā nāma sūriyuggamanakālaipi pathaviyā aulambantīpi chāyā hāyanavasaina aulambanti| sāyanhasamayaṃ pana chāyā vaḍḍhanavasaina aulambanti, tathaiva kammānipi tasmiṃ kālai vaḍḍhanavasaina upaṭṭhahanti, tasmā tamaiva vaḍḍhanupaṭṭhānaṃ sandhāya ‘‘sāyanhasamaya’’nti vuttaṃ| suk‍kapakkhaipi yaujanā kaṇhapakkhai yaujanānusāraina kātabbā| idaṃ ‘‘puna capara’’ntiādikaṃ suttadvayaṃ kusalakammaakusalakammaisu vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau kammaṃ nāma|

nānāvidhaṃ kammaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamau vipākau’’ti pucchitabbattā tattha katamau vipākau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha taisu aṭṭhavīsatividhaisu laukiyādīsu sāsanapaṭṭhānaisu katamau vipākauti pucchati| bhikkhavai, vau tumhaihi yai manussattasaddhāpaṭilābhādayau paṭiladdhā, tai manussattasaddhāpaṭilābhādayau vau tumhākaṃ lābhā bhavanti| tumhaihi pabbajitvā yaṃ catupārisuddhisīlādisampādanaṃ laddhaṃ, taṃ catupārisuddhisīlādisampādanaṃ vau tumhākaṃ suladdhaṃ bhavati| bhikkhavai, yau buddhuppādau navamau khaṇau tumhaihi laddhau, sau buddhuppādau navamau khaṇau vau tumhākaṃ brahmacariyavāsāya paṭiladdhau bhavati|

bhikkhavai, mayā chaphassāyatanikā nāma nirayā diṭṭhā, tattha taisu diṭṭhaisu chaphassāyatanikaisu nāma nirayaisu yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ cakkhunā passati, taṃ aniṭṭharūpaṃyaiva passati, nau iṭṭharūpaṃ| akantarūpaṃyaiva passati, nau kantarūpaṃ| amanāparūpaṃyaiva passati, nau manāparūpaṃ|

yaṃ  kiñ‍ci saddaṃ sautaina…pai॰… ghānaina…pai॰… jivhāya…pai॰… kāyaina…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci dhammaṃ manasā vijānāti, taṃ aniṭṭhadhammaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau iṭṭhadhammaṃ| akantadhammaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau kantadhammaṃ| amanāpadhammaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau manāpadhammanti pāṭhau yuttau| ‘‘aniṭṭharūpaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau iṭṭharūpa’’ntiādipāṭhau ayuttau, katthaci pāḷiyaṃ ayuttapāṭhau diṭṭhau| suk‍kapakkhai vuttanayavipariyāyaina yaujanā kātabbā| ayaṃ vuttappakārā ‘‘lābhā vau, bhikkhavai’’tiādikau pāṭhau vipākai vācakañāpakabhāvainaiva pavattanatau vipākau nāma|

mārisā nirayai pac‍camānānaṃ amhākaṃ sabbasau nimuj‍janaummuj‍janavasaina saṭṭhivassasahassāni paripuṇṇāni, nirayassa antau pariyausānaṃ kadā kasmiṃ kālai bhavissati|

nirayassa  antau pariyausānaṃ natthi| nirayassa antau pariyausānaṃ kutau atthi? nirayassa antau pariyausānaṃ amhākaṃ na paṭidissati| mārisā, yadā tumhai ca ahañ‍ca saiṭṭhiputtā jātā, tadā tuyhaṃ tumhākañ‍ca mayhaṃ mama ca pāpaṃ hi yasmā pakataṃ pakāraihi kataṃ, tasmā nirayassa antau pariyausānaṃ amhākaṃ na dissatīti ayaṃ pāṭhau vipākai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau vipākau nāma| (13)

121. adhammacārī narau kusaladhammaisu pamattau hi yasmā hauti, tasmā sau adhammacārī pamattau narau yahiṃ yahiṃ yaṃ yaṃ duggatiṃ gacchati, taṃ taṃ gacchantaṃ adhammacāriṃ naṃ naraṃ attanā caritau sau dhammauva hanati| kimiva hanati? sayaṃ attanā gahitau kaṇhasappau gaṇhantaṃ janaṃ hanati yathā, aivaṃ attanā caritau adhammau adhammacāriṃ naṃ hanati| ‘‘na hi dhammau adhammau cā’’tiādigāthāya atthau pākaṭau| idaṃ suttadvayaṃ kammai ca vipākai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau kammañ‍ca vipākau ca hauti|

bhikkhavai, tumhai puñ‍ñānaṃ mā bhāyittha; bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ yaṃ idaṃ ‘‘puñ‍ñānī’’ti adhivacanaṃ pavattaṃ; aitaṃ ‘‘puñ‍ñānī’’ti adhivacanaṃ iṭṭhassa kantassa piyassa manāpassa sukhassa sukhavipākajanakassa kammassa adhivacanaṃ hauti| bhikkhavai, ahaṃ dīgharattaṃ katānaṃ puñ‍ñānaṃ dīgharattaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ piyaṃ manāpaṃ abhijānāmi khau| ‘‘kathaṃ abhijānāmī’’ti cai pucchaiyya, pubbai satta vassāni maittacittaṃ maittāya sahitaṃ dutiyajjhānacittaṃ bhāvaitvā satta saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai imaṃ laukaṃ manussalaukaṃ puna na āgamāsiṃ| satta saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappaiti caittha saṃvaṭṭaggahaṇaina saṃvaṭṭaṭṭhāyī, vivaṭṭaggahaṇaina vivaṭṭaṭṭhāyīpi gahitāti vaiditabbā| saisaisupi aivamaiva gahaitabbau| bhikkhavai, saṃvaṭṭamānai kappai ahaṃ ābhassarūpagau haumi, vivaṭṭakappai suñ‍ñaṃ brahmavimānaṃ upapaj‍jāmi| bhikkhavai, tatra brahmavimānai tatra upapaj‍jamānai upapaj‍jamānahaitu ahaṃ brahmā haumi, añ‍ñai mahānubhāvaina abhibhavanatau abhibhū, añ‍ñaihi anabhibhavanatau anabhibhūtau mahābrahmā haumi, añ‍ñadatthu aikaṃsaina dasau ahaṃ vasavattī haumi|

bhikkhavai, ahaṃ daivānamindau sak‍kau chattiṃsakkhattuṃ ahausiṃ khau, dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau cak‍karatanādisattaratanasaman‍nāgatau cak‍kavattirājā anaikasatakkhattuṃ ahausiṃ, padaisaraj‍jassa rājabhāvai kau pana vādau|

bhikkhavai, tassa cak‍kavattirājabhūtassa mayhaṃ aitaṃ parivitak‍kanaṃ ahausi ‘‘yaina phalaina yaina  vipākaina ahaṃ aitarahi aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau amhi, taṃ idaṃ phalaṃ kissa kammassa phalaṃ nu khau, sau ayaṃ vipākau kissa kammassa vipākau nu khau’’ti aitaṃ parivitak‍kanaṃ ahausi| bhikkhavai, tassa vitak‍kaintassa mayhaṃ aitaṃ parivitak‍kanaṃ ahausi ‘‘yaina phalaina yaina vipākaina ahaṃ aitarahi aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau amhi, mai pavattaṃ taṃ idaṃ phalaṃ tiṇṇaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ khau, sau ayaṃ vipākau tiṇṇaṃ kammānaṃ vipākau khau, saiyyathidaṃ katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau? dānassa damassa saṃyamassāti tiṇṇaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau’’ti aitaṃ parivitak‍kanaṃ ahausīti avaucāti yaujanā|

tattha tasmiṃ ‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñānaṃ bhāyitthā’’tiādikai suttai yañ‍ca dānaṃ, yau ca damau, yau ca saṃyamau atthi, idaṃ dānādittayaṃ kammaṃ, taṃvācakañāpakaṃ suttampi kammaṃ nāma| tappac‍cayau taṃkammapac‍cayau pac‍cayuppan‍nabhūtau pac‍canubhūtau yau vipākau atthi, aitaṃ vipākai phalampi pakkhipitabbaṃ, ayaṃ vipākau taṃvācakañāpakau pāṭhaupi vipākau nāma| cūḷakammavibhaṅgau cūḷakammavipākaputhuttavibhāgau tathā vattabbau|

taudaiyyaputtassa  subhassa māṇavassa yaṃ suttaṃ bhagavatā daisitaṃ, tattha suttai vuttā yai pāṇātipātādayau dhammā appāyukadīghāyukatāya saṃvattanti, yai hiṃsanādayau dhammā bahvābādhaappābādhatāya saṃvattanti, yai usūyanādayau dhammā appaisakkhamahaisakkhatāya saṃvattanti, yai kaudhādayau dhammā dubbaṇṇasuvaṇṇatāya saṃvattanti, yai agāravādayau dhammā nīcakulikauc‍cakulikatāya saṃvattanti, yai macchairādayau dhammā appabhaugamahābhaugatāya saṃvattanti, yai asal‍lakkhaṇādayau dhammā duppañ‍ñapañ‍ñavantatāya saṃvattanti| idaṃ pāṇātipātasattayugaṃ kammaṃ, taṃvācakañāpakaṃ suttampi kammaṃ nāma| tattha subhasuttai yā appāyukadīghāyukatā vuttā…pai॰… yā duppañ‍ñapañ‍ñavantatā vuttā, sau ayaṃ appāyukadīghāyukatādikau vipākau, taṃvācakañāpakapāṭhaupi vipākau| idaṃ subhasuttaṃ kusalākusalakammai caiva vipākai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau kammañ‍ca vipākau ca hauti| (14)

122. yau puggalau vacīduc‍caritaparivaj‍janaina vācānurakkhī bhavaiyya, abhijjhādianuppādanaina manasā saṃvutau bhavaiyya, pāṇātipātādipajahanaina kāyaina akusalaṃ na kayirā, iti tayau aitai kammapathai visaudhayai, sau puggalau isippavaiditaṃ maggaṃ ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ ārādhayai ārādhayaiyyāti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘vācānurakkhī’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ kusalai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau kusalaṃ nāma|

yassa  puggalassa kāyaina duk‍kaṭaṃ duggatisaṃvattaniyakammaṃ natthi, vācāya duk‍kaṭakammaṃ natthi, manasā duk‍kaṭakammaṃ natthi, tīhi ṭhānaihi uppaj‍janaṭṭhānaihi saṃvutaṃ taṃ puggalaṃ ‘‘brāhmaṇa’’nti ahaṃ vadāmīti yaujanā| idaṃ gāthāvacanaṃ vuttanayaina kusalaṃ nāma|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai…pai॰… kusalamūlānī’’ti idaṃ vacanaṃ vuttanayaina kusalaṃ| bhikkhavai, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā vij‍jā pubbaṅgamā hauti, hirī ca auttappañ‍ca anudaivāti yaujanā| idaṃ vacanaṃ vuttanayaina kusalaṃ nāma|

māluvā sālaṃ rukkhaṃ aunataṃ bhūmiyaṃ patanaṃ karauti iva, tathā yassa janassa ac‍cantaṃ dvīsu tīsu bhavaisu dussīlyaṃ atthi, sau janau attānaṃ aunataṃ  apāyaisu pākaṭaṃ karauti| anatthakāmau janau yathā anatthaṃ icchati, tathā anatthaṃ karauti yathā, tathā īdisau dussīlau naṃ attānaṃ anatthaṃ karautīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘yassā’’tiādikaṃ vacanaṃ vuttanayaina akusalaṃ|

asmamayaṃ asmasaṅkhātaṃ pāsāṇamaṇimayaṃ vajiraṃ vajirassa uṭṭhānasaṅkhātaṃ pāsāṇamaṇiṃ abhimatthati vidhaṃsaiti iva, tathā attanā hi sayamaiva kataṃ attajaṃ attasambhavaṃ pāpaṃ dummaidhaṃ pāpaṃ karauntaṃ janaṃ abhimatthatīti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘attanā hī’’tiādikaṃ vacanaṃ vuttanayaina akusalaṃ|

daivatai kusalaihi vivaj‍jitā akusalā dasa kammapathai nisaiviya katvā garahā gārayhā bhavanti, bālamatī mandabuddhinau nirayaisu pac‍caraiti yaujanā| idaṃ ‘‘dasa kammapathai’’tiādikaṃ suttaṃ vuttanayaina akusalaṃ|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai…pai॰… akusalamūlānī’’ti idaṃ vacanaṃ vuttanayaina akusalaṃ| (15)

yādisaṃ yaṃ bījaṃ vapatai, taṃ bījaṃ tādisaṃ phalaṃ haratai iva, tathā kalyāṇakārī paṇḍitau kalyāṇaṃ phalaṃ haratai, pāpakārī bālau ca pāpakaṃ phalaṃ harataiti yaujanā| tattha ‘‘yādisa’’ntiādikai suttai ‘‘kalyāṇakārī kalyāṇa’’nti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, idaṃ vacanaṃ kusalaṃ| ‘‘pāpakārī ca pāpaka’’nti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, idaṃ vacanaṃ akusalaṃ| idaṃ dvivacanaṃ vuttanayaina kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca hauti|

kalyāṇakārī  sappurisā subhaina kammaina suggatiṃ vajanti gacchanti, pāpakārī kāpurisā asubhaina kammunā apāyabhūmiṃ vajanti gacchanti, kammassa abhisaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇasahagatakammassa khayā khayanatau vimuttacaitasā samucchaidavimuttipaṭippassaddhivimutticittā tai sappurisā asubhai nibbanti| kimiva nibbanti? indhanakkhayā jauti nibbāti iva, tathā tai sappurisā kammassa khayā anavasaisakhayanatau nibbantīti yaujanā| tattha tasmiṃ ‘‘subhainā’’tiādigāthāvacanai ‘‘subhaina…pai॰… suggati’’nti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, idaṃ ‘‘subhaina…pai॰…suggati’’nti vacanaṃ kusalai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau kusalaṃ nāma| ‘‘apāyabhūmiṃ asubhaina kammunā’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, idaṃ ‘‘apāya…pai॰… kammunā’’ti vacanaṃ akusalai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau akusalaṃ nāma| idaṃ ‘‘subhainā’’tiādikaṃ gāthāvacanaṃ vuttanayaina kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca hauti| (16)

123. ‘‘yathāpi bhamarau pupphaṃ…pai॰… munī carai’’ti idaṃ gāthāvacanaṃ anuñ‍ñātai caraṇai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau anuñ‍ñātaṃ nāma|

bhamarau nāma puppharasapivanagahaṇavasaina caraṇakau madhukarādikau bhamarau| sau puppharasaṃ gaṇhantau mandavaigau hutvā pupphañ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca gandhañ‍ca avināsaitvā yāvadatthaṃ puppharasaṃ pivitvā madhukaraṇatthāya ca puppharasaṃ gahaitvā madhukaraṇaṭṭhānaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ palaiti| pupphavaṇṇagandhā pākatikāva haunti| aivamaiva piṇḍāya gāmaṃ pavisantau muni pasādajanakaṃ ālaukanavilaukanagamanatiṭṭhanādikaṃ janaitvā pītisaumanassasahitaṃ pasādaṃ janaitvā saddhādaiyyaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ yāpanamattaṃ paṭiggahaitvā gāmatau nikkhamitvā udakaphāsukaṭṭhānai vanai bhaisaj‍jaṃ limpantau viya, kantārai puttamaṃsaṃ khādantau viya, piṇḍapātaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā paribhuñ‍jitvā bhamarau vanai madhuṃ karauti viya, kammaṭṭhānānurūpaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ pavisitvā jhānamaggaphalanibbattanatthāya samaṇadhammakaraṇatthāya gāmai carai caraiyyāti adhippāyau vaiditabbau|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ karaṇīyāni…pai॰… imāni khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ tīṇi karaṇīyānī’’ti idaṃ suttaṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātai ācārai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau anuñ‍ñātaṃ nāma| tasmiṃ suttai yau bhikkhu sīlaṃ pāti rakkhati, iti rakkhaṇatau sau bhikkhu pāti nāma| yaṃ sīlaṃ taṃ pātiṃ bhikkhuṃ apāyādidukkhatau maucaiti, iti maucanatau taṃ sīlaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ nāma| yaina sīlaina bhikkhu saṃvaritabbacakkhundriyādikaṃ saṃvarati, iti saṃvaraṇakaraṇatau taṃ sīlaṃ saṃvaraṃ nāma, pātimaukkhaṃ aiva  saṃvaraṃ pātimaukkhasaṃvaraṃ, pātimaukkhasaṃvaraina saṃvutau saman‍nāgatau hutvā saṃvuṇanatau catuiriyāpathaisu cārakau hauti, iti saṃvuṇanatau bhikkhu pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau nāma| viharati catuiriyāpathai pavattaiti| vārittacāraṃ vaj‍jaitvā cārittasīlaṃ ādāya caraṇaṃ ācārau, agaucarai vaj‍jaitvā gaucarai  caraṇaṃ gaucarauti| atthau vuc‍camānau ativitthārau bhavissati, tasmā kiñ‍cimattaṃ kathaitvā sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttabhāvaṃ kathaissāma|

‘‘aittakamaiva suttaṃ ‘anuñ‍ñāta’nti niddhāritabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ ‘‘dasā’’tiādikaṃ suttampi anuñ‍ñātai dasavidhai pac‍cavaikkhitabbai dhammai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau anuñ‍ñātaṃ nāma| ‘‘tīṇimāni…pai॰… karaṇīyānī’’ti idaṃ suttampi anuñ‍ñātai tividhai sucaritai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau anuñ‍ñātaṃ nāma|

nānāvidhaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ suttaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ suttaṃ paṭikkhitta’’nti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ paṭikkhitta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

‘‘natthi puttasamaṃ paimaṃ, natthi gausamitaṃ dhanaṃ|

natthi sūriyasamā ābhā, samuddaparamā sarā’’ti|| –

idaṃ daivaputtavacanaṃ paṭikkhipantau bhagavā –

‘‘natthi attasamaṃ paimaṃ, natthi dhañ‍ñasamaṃ dhanaṃ|

natthi pañ‍ñāsamā ābhā, vuṭṭhi vai paramā sarā’’ti|| –

gāthaṃ āha| aittha aitasmiṃ gāthādvayai yaṃ ‘‘natthi puttasamaṃ paima’’ntiādikaṃ purimakaṃ hauti| idaṃ ‘‘natthi puttasamaṃ paima’’ntiādikaṃ daivaputtavacanaṃ bhagavatā paṭikkhittattā, paṭikkhittai atthai pavattanatau ca paṭikkhittaṃ nāma|

dubbhikkhakālai vā kantārai vā mātāpitarau puttadhītarau ghātaitvāpi attānamaiva pausainti, tasmā ‘‘natthi attasamaṃ paima’’nti vuttaṃ| dubbhikkhakālādīsu hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇasārādīni, gaumahiṃsādīnipi dhañ‍ñagahaṇatthāya dhañ‍ñassāmikānaṃ datvā dhañ‍ñamaiva gaṇhanti, tasmā ‘‘natthi dhañ‍ñasamaṃ  dhana’’nti vuttaṃ| sūriyādīnaṃ ābhā pac‍cuppan‍natamaṃ aikadaisaṃva vinaudaiti, pañ‍ñā pana dasasahassilaukadhātumpi aikapaj‍jautaṃ aikaubhāsaṃ kātuṃ samatthā, atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nadhammakauṭṭhāsaisupi paṭicchādakaṃ kilaisatamampi vidhamati, tasmā ‘‘natthi pañ‍ñāsamā ābhā’’ti vuttaṃ| samuddau bhūmiyā  ca aikadaisaiyaiva tiṭṭhati, sau ca daivai avuṭṭhai sati khayanasabhāvau bhavaiyya, vuṭṭhi pana kauṭisatasahassacak‍kavāḷaisupi yāva ābhassarā brahmalaukāpi pūrā bhavati, tasmā ‘‘vuṭṭhi vai paramāsarā’’ti vuttaṃ|

‘‘idamaiva paṭikkhittaṃ niddhāritabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘tīṇimāni bhikkhavai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ ‘‘tīṇimānī’’tiādikaṃ suttampi paṭikkhittai duc‍caritai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau paṭikkhittaṃ nāma| (17)

124. nānāvidhaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamaṃ anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍cā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ anuñ‍ñātañ‍cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

bhūripañ‍ñabhūripañ‍ñavanta gautama, taṃ bhūripañ‍ñaṃ gautamaṃ ahaṃ pucchāmi| idha laukai anaikā yā janatā bhītā, sā janatā kiṃsu katamā bhavai| yau ca maggau anaikāyatanau iti pavuttau, sau ca maggau kiṃsu katamau bhavai| kismiṃ dhammai ṭhitau janau paralaukaṃ na bhāyai na bhāyaiyyāti pucchatīti yaujanā|

daivaputta yau janau sammāvācañ‍ca paṇidhāya, sammāmanañ‍ca paṇidhāya, kāyaina pāpāni akubbamānau ca bhavai, ayaṃ aikau| bahvan‍napānaṃ gharaṃ āvasantau ca bhavai, ayaṃ aikau| saddhau saddhāsampan‍nau cittamudubhāvaina mudu ca bhavai, ayaṃ aikau| vadañ‍ñū yācakānaṃ yācanavasaina vuttavacanañ‍ñū hutvā saṃvibhāgī ca bhavai, ayaṃ aikau| iti aitaisu catūsu dhammaisu ṭhitau janau dhammaisu ṭhitau hutvā paralaukaṃ na bhāyai na bhāyaiyyāti yaujanā|

‘‘tasmiṃ suttai katamaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, katamaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ nāmā’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘tattha yaṃ āhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha tasmiṃ ‘‘kiṃsūdhā’’tiādipañhāya vissaj‍janai ‘‘vācaṃ manañ‍cā’’tiādivacanai ‘‘vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya sammā’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ bhagavā āha, idaṃ ‘‘vācaṃ…pai॰… sammā’’ti vacanaṃ anuñ‍ñātai vacanīyādikai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau anuñ‍ñātaṃ nāma| ‘‘kāyaina pāpāni akubbamānau’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, idaṃ ‘‘kāyaina  …pai॰… mānau’’ti vacanaṃ pāpakubbaina paṭikkhittai vuttanayaina pavattanatau paṭikkhittaṃ nāma| ‘‘bahvan‍na…pai॰… na bhāyai’’ti yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, idaṃ ‘‘bahvan‍na…pai॰… na  bhāyai’’ti vacanaṃ vuttanayaina anuñ‍ñātaṃ nāma| idaṃ ‘‘vāca’’ntiādikaṃ vacanaṃ vuttanayadvayaina anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca hauti|

‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇa’’ntiādikau vuttatthauva| ‘‘tasmiṃ sabbapāpassātiādikai katamaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, katamaṃ paṭikkhitta’’nti vattabbabhāvatau ‘‘tattha ya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

daivānaminda, ahaṃ kāyasamācārampi duvidhaina vadāmi – saivitabbaṃ anavaj‍jaṃ kāyasamācārampi vadāmi, asaivitabbaṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ kāyasamācārampi ahaṃ vadāmi| vacīsamācārādīsupi vuttanayānusāraina yaujanā kātabbā|

‘‘kiñ‍ca vaḍḍhanahāyanaṃ āgamma kāyasamācārādikaṃ saivitabbāsaivitabbabhaidaina vutta’’nti vattabbabhāvatau ‘‘kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| akusaladhammavaḍḍhanaṃ, kusaladhammahāyanañ‍ca paṭic‍ca kāyasamācārādayau na saivitabbā, kusaladhammavaḍḍhanaṃ, akusaladhammahāyanañ‍ca paṭic‍ca kāyasamācārādayau saivitabbāti sal‍lakkhaitabbā| (18)

125. nānāvidhaṃ anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘katamau suttavisaisau thavau’’ti pucchitabbattā tathā pucchitvā ayaṃ suttavisaisau thavau nāmāti viñ‍ñāpaituṃ ‘‘tattha katamau thavau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha tatthāti taisu aṭṭhavīsatividhaisu laukiyādīsu sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttaisu katamau suttavisaisau thavau nāmāti pucchati|

maggānaṃ jaṅghamaggadiṭṭhimaggādīnaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikau sammādiṭṭhimaggaṅgādiaṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saiṭṭhau uttamau| sac‍cānaṃ vacīsac‍cakhattiyādisammutisac‍caparamatthasac‍cānaṃ caturau dukkhasamudayaniraudhaniraudhagāminipaṭipadāvasaina caturau ariyasac‍cā padā saiṭṭhā uttamā| dhammānaṃ sabbasaṅkhatasappac‍cayadhammānaṃ virāgau asaṅkhatanibbānasaṅkhātau virāgau dhammau saiṭṭhau uttamau| dvipadānaṃ sabbadaivamanussādīnaṃ dvipadānaṃ cakkhumā pañ‍cavidhacakkhumā bhagavā saiṭṭhau uttamauti yaujanā| ayaṃ ‘‘maggānaṭṭhaṅgikau’’tiādisuttavisaisau thavai atthai vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau thavau nāma|

‘‘ayamaiva  suttavisaisau thavau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tīṇimāni bhikkhavai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| apadā ahimacchādayau vā, dvipadā manussasakuṇapakkhijātikādayau vā, catuppadā hatthiassagaumahiṃsādayau vā, bahuppadā  satapadiādayau vā, rūpinau kāmarūpasattā vā, arūpinau arūpasattā vā, sañ‍ñinau sattaviñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitisattā vā, asañ‍ñinau asañ‍ñasattā vā, naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñinau bhavaggai nibbattasattā vā yāvatā yattakā sattā saṃvij‍janti, taisaṃ tattakānaṃ apadādīnaṃ sattānaṃ yadidaṃ yau ayaṃ arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau tathāgatau uppan‍nau, sau ayaṃ arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau tathāgatau aggaṃ aggauti akkhāyati, saiṭṭhaṃ saiṭṭhauti akkhāyati, pavaraṃ pavarauti akkhāyati, ayaṃ paṭhamau aggau|

saṅkhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ vā sappac‍cayasabhāvānaṃ vā, asaṅkhatānaṃ pac‍cayaihi asaṅkharitānaṃ paṇṇattimattabhūtānaṃ dhammānaṃ vā yāvatā yattakā paṇṇattī vauharīyanti, tattakaihi paṇṇattīhi pañ‍ñapaitabbānaṃ taisaṃ saṅkhatāsaṅkhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ yadidaṃ yau ayaṃ madanimmadanau…pai॰… yau ayaṃ niraudhau, yaṃ idaṃ nibbānamaggaphalānamālambaṇaṃ bhavati, sau ayaṃ madanimmadanādikau dhammau aggaṃ aggauti akkhāyati…pai॰… akkhāyati, ayaṃ dutiyau aggau|

saṅghānaṃ yāvatā paṇṇatti, gaṇānaṃ yāvatā paṇṇatti, mahājanasan‍nipātānaṃ yāvatā paṇṇatti vauharīyanti, tattakaihi paṇṇattīhi pañ‍ñapaitabbānaṃ taisaṃ saṅghagaṇādīnaṃ yāni imāni cattāri puggalāni purisayugāni, yai imai aṭṭha purisapuggalā…pai॰… laukassa yaṃ idaṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ saṃvij‍jati, sau ayaṃ catupurisayugādikau tathāgatasāvakasaṅghau aggaṃ aggauti akkhāyati…pai॰… akkhāyati, ayaṃ tatiyau aggau| imāni tīṇi tathāgatanibbānaariyasaṅgharatanāni aggāni bhavanti|

sabbalaukuttarau apadādisabbasattalaukatau uttarau satthā ca, kusalapakkhatau kusalaanavaj‍japakkhabhāvatau uttarau dhammau ca, narasīhassa satthunau gaṇau ca iti tīṇi satthudhammagaṇaratanāni aggāni, tāni tīṇi satthudhammagaṇaratanāni visissarai guṇavasaina visissanti|

samaṇapadumasañ‍cayau sarai ruhamānaṃ padumaṃ saubhanaṃ iva sāsanai saubhanasamaṇapadumasamūhau gaṇau ca, dhammavarau ca, vidūnaṃ sak‍katau naravaradamakau  naravarānaṃ brahmadaivamanussarājarājamahāmac‍cādīnaṃ  damakau anudamakau cakkhumā sambuddhau ca iti tīṇi gaṇadhammabuddharatanāni laukassa uttari bhavanti|

appaṭisamau satthā ca, nirupadāhau niggataupadāhau, sabbau dhammau ca ariyau gaṇavarau ca iti yāni tīṇi buddhadhammagaṇaratanāni aggāni, tāni tīṇi…pai॰… nāni khalu aikaṃsaina visissarai visissanti|

sac‍canāmau avitathasac‍cadaisanatau sac‍canāmau khaimau sabbābhibhū sabbai manussadaivādikai anabhibhavamānaupi guṇātiraikavasaina abhibhavamānau viya pavattanatau sabbābhibhū jinau ca, sac‍cadhammau avitathasabhāvatau sac‍cadhammau ca, tassa sac‍cadhammassa uttari uttamau añ‍ñau dhammau natthi, viñ‍ñūnaṃ nic‍caṃ pūjitau pūjārahau ariyasaṅghau ca iti tīṇi laukassa uttari uttamāni bhavanti|

aikāyanapadassa vacanatthau aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 170)  bahudhā vuttau| jātikhayantadassī hitānukampī bhagavā aikāyanaṃ maggaṃ pajānāti| ‘‘yaṃ aikāyanaṃ maggaṃ pajānāti, taina maggaina kiṃ taratī’’ti vattabbabhāvatau ‘‘aitaina maggainā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yaṃ maggaṃ bhagavā jānāti, aitaina maggaina pubbai atītamaddhānaṃ buddhādayau ariyā aughaṃ saṃsāraughaṃ tariṃsu, anāgatamaddhānaṃ tarissanti, yai cāpi buddhādayau pac‍cuppan‍nai uppaj‍janti, tai cāpi buddhādayau pac‍cuppan‍nai taranti, visuddhipaikkhā visuddhiṃ apaikkhamānā sattā daivamanussasaiṭṭhaṃ tādisaṃ yathāvuttaguṇaṃ taṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ namassanti, iti ayaṃ nānāvidhasuttavisaisaupi thavai ratanattayai, ratanattayaguṇai ca vācakañāpakabhāvaina pavattanatau thavau nāma| ic‍caitaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttavisaisadassakau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisaupi sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ nāmāti vaiditabbau|

amhākācariya tumhaihi amhākācariyaihi sauḷasappabhaidasaṃkilaisabhāgiyādisāsanapaṭṭhānasuttañ‍caiva aṭṭhavīsatividhaṃ laukiyādisāsanapaṭṭhānasuttañ‍ca niddhāritaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘taisu saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīsu sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttavisaisaisu katamaṃ suttavisaisaṃ katamaina suttavisaisaina saṃsanditvā niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha laukiyaṃ  sutta’’ntiādi āraddhaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana – ‘‘aivaṃ duvidhampi sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ nānāsuttapadāni udāharantaina vibhajitvā idāni saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīhi saṃsanditvā dassaituṃ puna ‘laukiyaṃ sutta’ntiādi āraddha’’nti (naitti॰ aṭṭha॰ 170) vuttaṃ|

tattha  tatthāti taisu sauḷasavidhaisu saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīsu sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttavisaisaisu caiva aṭṭhavīsatividhaisu laukiyādīsu sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttavisaisaisu ca akusalapakkhai pavattaṃ laukiyaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyasuttaina samānatthabhāvaina saṃsandati, kusalapakkhai pavattaṃ laukiyaṃ suttaṃ vāsanābhāgiyasuttaina samānatthabhāvaina saṃsandati, tasmā laukiyaṃ suttaṃ aikavidhampi saṃkilaisabhāgiyaina ca vāsanābhāgiyaina ca dvīhi suttaihi niddisitabbaṃ| dassanapakkhai pavattaṃ laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ dassanabhāgiyaina samānatthabhāvaina saṃsandati, bhāvanāpakkhai pavattaṃ laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ bhāvanābhāgiyaina samānatthabhāvaina saṃsandati, asaikkhapakkhai pavattaṃ laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaina samānatthabhāvaina saṃsandati, tasmā laukuttarampi suttaṃ dassanabhāgiyaina ca bhāvanābhāgiyaina ca asaikkhabhāgiyaina ca tīhi suttaihi niddisitabbaṃ| vuttanayānusāraina saisaisupi saṃsandanayaujanā kātabbā|

amhākācariya tumhaihi amhākācariyaihi nayadassanavasaina suttavisaisasaṃsandanaṃ dassitaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘kimatthāya saṃkilaisabhāgiyādibhaidaina vibhajitvā bhagavatā vutta’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyassa suttassa nigghātāyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aittha ca suttavasaina suttatthā gahitā|

‘‘yaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, taṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ kittakaihi suttaihi vibhajitvā niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ chabbisatiyā puggalaihi niddisitabba’’nti vuttaṃ| ‘‘tai chabbīsati puggalā katihi suttaihi samanvaisitabbā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tai tīhī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| dassanabhāgiyaina sattādhiṭṭhānaina, bhāvanābhāgiyaina sattādhiṭṭhānaina, asaikkhabhāgiyaina sattādhiṭṭhānaina cāti tīhi suttaihi tai chabbīsati puggalā samanvaisitabbā|

‘‘katamaihi  katamaihi katamaṃ katamaṃ suttaṃ niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha dassanabhāgiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha tatthāti taisu tīsu dassanabhāgiyādīsu suttaisu| tatthāti vā taisu chabbīsatiyā puggalaisu| sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ dassanabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ aikabījinā puggalaina ca niddisitabbaṃ…pai॰… dhammānusārinā puggalaina ca niddisitabbaṃ, iti imaihi pañ‍cahi puggalaihi sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ dassanabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ niddisitabbaṃ| aittha ca dassanaggahaṇaina sautāpattiphalaṭṭhāpi gahitā, tasmā aikabījikaulaṃkaulasattakkhattuparamā phalaṭṭhāpi gahitā|

saddhānusārī  pana yau vipassanākkhaṇai saddhaṃ dhuraṃ katvā sautāpattimaggaṃ nibbattaiti, sau puggalau nibbattaitabbasautāpattimaggakkhaṇai saddhānusārī nāma, saddhāya samāpattiṃ anussarati, iti saddhāya samāpattiyā anussaraṇatau sautāpattimaggaṭṭhau puggalau saddhānusārī nāma| sau puggalau sautāpattiphalakkhaṇai saddhāya vimuttattā saddhāvimuttau hutvā aikabījikaulaṃkaulasattakkhattuparamau bhavati| yau pana puggalau vipassanākkhaṇai pañ‍ñaṃ dhuraṃ katvā sautāpattimaggaṃ nibbattaiti, sau puggalau nibbattaitabbasautāpattimaggakkhaṇai dhammānusārī nāma, dhammaina pañ‍ñāya samāpattiṃ anussarati, iti dhammaina pañ‍ñāya samāpattiyā anussaraṇatau dhammānusārī nāma| sau puggalau phalakkhaṇai diṭṭhiyā pañ‍ñāya niraudhaṃ pattattā diṭṭhipattau hutvā aikabīji…pai॰… paramau bhavati| dhammauti caittha pañ‍ñā gahitā| iti pabhaidatau dvai maggaṭṭhā, cha phalaṭṭhāti aṭṭhahi ariyapuggalaihi, sampiṇḍitai pana pañ‍cahi ariyapuggalaihi sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ dassanabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ niddisitabbaṃ|

imaisaṃ aikabījiādīnaṃ puggalānaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisatthattā caiva dassanabhāgiyatthattā ca sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ dassanabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ aittakaihi puggalaihi niddisitabbanti niyamaitvā ācariyaina vibhattaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ dassanabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ kittakaihi puggalaihi niddisitabba’’nti pucchitabbattā –

‘‘bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ dvādasahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍naina, sakadāgāminā, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍naina, anāgāminā, antarāparinibbāyinā, upahac‍caparinibbāyinā, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyinā, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyinā, uddhaṃsautaina akaniṭṭhagāminā, saddhāvimuttaina, diṭṭhippattaina, kāyasakkhinā cāti bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ imaihi dvādasahi puggalaihi niddisitabba’’nti –

vuttaṃ| tatthāpi sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ suttanti gahaitabbaṃ| sakadā…pai॰… pan‍naina sakadāgāmimaggaṭṭhaina puggalaina, sakadāgāminā sakadāgāmiphalaṭṭhaina, anāgāmi…pai॰… pan‍naina anāgāmimaggaṭṭhaina, anāgāminā anāgāmiphalaṭṭhaina, avihādīsu pañ‍casu suddhāvāsaisu āyuvaimajjhaṃ anatik‍kamitvā arahattaṃ patvā parinibbāyanasabhāvaina antarāparinibbāyīnāmakaina anāgāminā, āyuvaimajjhaṃ atik‍kamitvā arahattaṃ patvā parinibbāyanasabhāvaina upahac‍caparinibbāyīnāmakaina anāgāminā, asaṅkhāraina appayaugaina arahattaṃ patvā parinibbāyanasabhāvaina  asaṅkhāraparinibbāyīnāmakaina anāgāminā, sasaṅkhāraina sappayaugaina arahattaṃ patvā parinibbāyanasabhāvaina sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyīnāmakaina anāgāminā, avihādīhi uddhaṃ atappādīsu upapattisautaina arahattaṃ patvā parinibbāyanasabhāvaina uddhaṃsautanāmakaina anāgāminā, akaniṭṭhaṃ gantvā arahattaṃ patvā parinibbāyanasabhāvaina akaniṭṭhagāmīnāmakaina anāgāminā, saddhāya vimuttattā saddhāvimuttanāmakaina anāgāminā, diṭṭhiyā pañ‍ñāya niraudhaṃ pattattā diṭṭhippattanāmakaina anāgāminā cāti imaihi aikādasahi ajhānalābhīpuggalaihi ca, kāyaina nāmakāyai phuṭṭhānaṃ arūpajhānānaṃ anantaraṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikarauti, iti sacchikaraṇatau kāyasakkhīnāmakaina jhānalābhinā cāti dvādasahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ|

imaisaṃ vuttappakārānaṃ puggalānaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisatthattā caiva bhāvanābhāgiyatthattā ca sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ aittakaihi puggalaihi niddisitabbanti niyamaitvā ācariyaina vibhattaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘kittakaihi puggalaihi sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā –

‘‘asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ navahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ saddhāvimuttaina, pañ‍ñāvimuttaina, suñ‍ñatavimuttaina, animittavimuttaina, appaṇihitavimuttaina, ubhataubhāgavimuttaina, samasīsinā, pac‍caikabuddhaihi, sammāsambuddhaihi cāti asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ imaihi navahi puggalaihi niddisitabba’’nti –

vuttaṃ| tattha sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ niddisitabbanti yaujaitabbaṃ| saddhāya kilaisaihi vimuttattā arahattaphalakkhaṇai saddhāvimuttau arahā, taina saddhāvimuttaina| pañ‍ñāya vimuttattā arahattaphalakkhaṇai pañ‍ñāvimuttau arahā, taina pañ‍ñāvimuttaina| suñ‍ñatavipassanāsaṅkhātaina anattānupassanaina vimuttattā suñ‍ñatavimuttau arahā, taina suñ‍ñatavimuttaina| animittānupassanāsaṅkhātaina anic‍cānupassanaina vimuttattā animittavimuttau arahā, taina animittavimuttaina| appaṇihitānupassanāsaṅkhātaina dukkhānupassanaina vimuttattā appaṇihitavimuttau arahā, taina appaṇihitavimuttaina| ubhatau rūpakāyanāmakāyatau ubhataubhāgatau vimuttattā ubhataubhāgavimuttau arahā, taina ubhataubhāgavimuttaina| purimā pañ‍ca puggalā ajhānalābhinau gahitā, ubhataubhāgavimuttau pana jhānalābhīgahitau|

samasīsī nāma iriyāpathasamasīsī, raugasamasīsī, jīvitasamasīsīti tividhā haunti| imaisu  tīsu samasīsīsu yau arahā catūsu iriyāpathaisu aikaikasmiṃ iriyāpathai arahattaṃ patvā añ‍ñaṃ iriyāpathaṃ asaṅkamitvā tasmiṃ tasmiṃ iriyāpathaiyaiva parinibbāyati, ayaṃ arahā iriyāpathasamasīsī nāma| yau arahā yasmiṃ raugai uppan‍nai arahattaṃ patvā tatau raugatau anuṭṭhahitvā tasmiṃ raugaiyaiva parinibbāyati, ayaṃ arahā raugasamasīsī nāma| yau arahā pac‍cavaikkhaṇavīthiyānantaraṃ bhavaṅgaṃ autaritvā tatau maraṇāsan‍najavanavīthiyānantaramaiva parinibbāyati, ayaṃ arahā vārasamatāya jīvitasamasīsī nāma| vārasamatāti ca pac‍cavaikkhaṇavīthi maggavithyānuvattakattā pac‍cavaikkhaṇavīthianantaraṃ pavattamānāyapi maraṇāsan‍navīthi maggavīthianantaraṃ pavattāti vattabbārahā, tasmā vīthianantaratā vārasamatā nāma| tāya vārasamatāya ca jīvitasamasīsī vuttau|

saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ pāpuṇīti aitthapi pac‍cavaikkhaṇavīthiyānantaraṃ bhavaṅgaṃ autaritvā bhavaṅgatau vuṭṭhāya pavattavīthiyā paṭisambhidāñāṇāni pavattanti| vuttanayaina vīthianantaratāya vārasamatāya ‘‘saha paṭisambhidāhī’’ti vuttaṃ| bhagavatau sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇampi pac‍cavaikkhaṇavīthiyānantaraṃ bhavaṅgaṃ autaritvā bhavaṅgatau vuṭṭhāya pavattavīthiyā paṭhamaṃ pavattatīti vaiditabbaṃ| iminā  jīvitasamasīsinā, sabbaihi pac‍caikabuddhaihi, sabbaihi sammāsambuddhaihi cāti imaihi navahi puggalaihi sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ niddisitabbaṃ|

imaisaṃ puggalānaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisatthattā caiva asaikkhabhāgiyatthattā ca aivaṃ iminā ‘‘laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhāna’’ntiādinā pakāraina vuttaihi imaihi chabbīsatiyā puggalaihi ariyaihi dassanabhāgiyavāsanābhāgiyaasaikkhabhāgiyasuttānaṃ vasaina laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ suttaṃ niddisitabbaṃ|

imaisaṃ chabbīsatiyā puggalānaṃ sakalalaukuttarasuttatthattā caiva sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisasuttatthattā ca laukuttaraṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ suttaṃ| aittakaihi puggalaihi niddisitabbanti ācariyaina niyamaitvā vibhattaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘laukiyaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ suttaṃ kittakaihi puggalaihi niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘laukiyaṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ aikūnavīsatiyā puggalaihi niddisitabba’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘tai aikūnavīsati laukiyā puggalā katamaihi dhammaihi niddiṭṭhā samanvaisitabbā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tai caritaihī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tai aikūnavīsati laukiyā puggalā caritaihi caritavisaisaihi niddiṭṭhā samanvaisitabbāti| ‘‘kathaṃ caritaihi niddiṭṭhā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘kaici rāgacaritā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| rāgacaritadausacaritādīhi caritaihi aikūnavīsati laukiyapuggalā  rāgacaritā, kaici dausacaritā…pai॰… mauhacaritau cāti niddiṭṭhā| iti niddiṭṭhaihi imaihi aikūnavīsatiyā puggalaihi laukiyasattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ suttaṃ niddisitabbaṃ| ‘‘laukiya’’nti sāmañ‍ñavasaina vuttampi ‘‘saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ laukiya’’nti visaisatau viñ‍ñātabbaṃ|

laukiyaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ suttaṃ aittakaihi puggalaihi niddisitabbanti ācariyaina niyamaitvā vibhattaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘vāsanābhāgiyaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ suttaṃ katamaihi puggalaihi niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘vāsanābhāgiya’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| vāsanābhāgiyaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ suttaṃ sīlavantaihi puggalaihi, dhammaihi ca niddisitabbanti yaujaitabbaṃ| ‘‘kittakā sīlavantapuggalā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tai sīlavantau pañ‍ca puggalā’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘kittakā dhammā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘pakatisīla’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – pakatisīlavantau ca samādānasīlavantau ca cittappasādavantau  ca samathavantau ca vipassanāvantau cāti pañ‍ca puggalā, pakatisīladhammau ca samādānasīladhammau ca cittappasādadhammau ca samathadhammau ca vipassanādhammau cāti pañ‍ca dhammāti imaihi pañ‍cahi puggalaihi, imaihi pañ‍cahi dhammaihi vāsanābhāgiyaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaikadaisadhammādhiṭṭhānaikadaisaṃ suttaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ niddisitabbanti|

laukuttaraṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ suttaṃ dassanabhāgiyavāsanābhāgiyaasaikkhabhāgiyasuttaihi niddisitabbanti ācariyaina niyamaitvā vibhattaṃ, amhaihi ca ñātaṃ, ‘‘laukuttaraṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ suttaṃ kittakaihi suttaihi niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… asaikkhabhāgiyainā’’ti vuttaṃ|

‘‘laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca kittakaihi niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘laukiyañ‍ca…pai॰… ubhayaina niddisitabba’’nti vuttaṃ| ubhayaināti laukiyalaukuttaraina, sattādhiṭṭhānadhammādhiṭṭhānaina samānatthabhāvaina niddisitabbanti|

‘‘ñāṇaṃ kittakaihi niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘ñāṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yasmiṃ yasmiṃ suttai ñāṇaṃ āgataṃ, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ suttai ñāṇaṃ ñāṇapariyāyaina pañ‍ñādinā niddisitabbanti|

‘‘ñaiyyaṃ kittakaina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘ñaiyyaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naihī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yasmiṃ yasmiṃ suttai ñaiyyaṃ āgataṃ, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ suttai ñaiyyaṃ ñaiyyapariyāyaina niddisitabbanti|

‘‘ñāṇañ‍ca  ñaiyyañ‍ca kittakaina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca tadubhayainā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yasmiṃ yasmiṃ suttai ñāṇañaiyyā āgatā, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ suttai ñāṇañaiyyā ñāṇañaiyyapariyāyaina niddisitabbanti|

dassanasuttai yathā niddiṭṭhaṃ, tathā upadhārayitvā labbhamānatau niddisitabbaṃ| bhāvanāsuttai yathā niddiṭṭhaṃ, tathā upadhārayitvā labbhamānatau niddisitabbaṃ| tadubhayaṃ dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā ca suttai yathā niddiṭṭhaṃ, tathā upadhārayitvā labbhamānatau niddisitabbaṃ|

‘‘sakavacanaṃ  paravacana’’ntiādīsupi aivamaiva visuṃ visuṃ ca aikatau ca suttai yathā niddiṭṭhaṃ, tathā upadhārayitvā labbhamānatau niddisitabbanti yaujanā kātabbā| ‘‘aittakamaiva niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘yaṃ vā panā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

‘‘vipākassa haitu kammamaivā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘duvidhau haitū’’tiādi vuttaṃ| kilaisā saṃkilaisabhāgiyasuttaina samānatthabhāvaina niddisitabbā| taṇhāsaṅkhātau samudayau vā kilaisasaṅkhātau samudayau vā akusalasaṅkhātau samudayau vā saṃkilaisabhāgiyaina suttaina samānatthabhāvaina niddisitabbau| laukiyakusalahaitusaṅkhātau samudayau vā laukiyakusalasaṅkhātau samudayau vā vāsanābhāgiyaina suttaina samānatthabhāvaina niddisitabbau|

kammañ‍ca vipākau ca yathārahaṃ labbhamānasuttaina niddisitabbauti sāmañ‍ñavasaina vibhattau, ‘‘kusalaṃ katamaina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘tattha kusala’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tatthāti taisu aṭṭhavīsatiyā sāsanapaṭṭhānasuttaisu kusalaṃ catūhi suttaihi samānatthabhāvaina niddisitabbaṃ| ‘‘katamaihi catūhī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘vāsanābhāgiyainā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| laukiyakusalaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaina niddisitabbaṃ samānatthattā, laukuttarakusalaṃ dassanabhāgiyaina, vāsanābhāgiyaina, asaikkhabhāgiyaina ca yathārahaṃ samānatthabhāvaina niddisitabbaṃ| kusalaṃ aittakaihi niddisitabbanti niyamaitvā vibhattaṃ, ‘‘akusalaṃ katamaina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘akusala’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca katamaina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca tadubhayaina niddisitabba’’nti vuttaṃ| yasmiṃ yasmiṃ suttai tadubhayaṃ āgataṃ, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ suttai āgataina tadubhayaina niddisitabbaṃ|

‘‘anuñ‍ñātaṃ katamaina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘anuñ‍ñāta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| anuñ‍ñātaṃ  bhagavatau anuñ‍ñātāya samānatāya niddisitabbaṃ| ‘‘katividhaṃ anuñ‍ñāta’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘taṃ pañ‍cavidha’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| yaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ yāsu yāsu bhūmīsu dissati, taṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ tāsu tāsu bhūmīsu āgataina samānaina kappiyānulaumaina niddisitabbaṃ|

anuñ‍ñātaṃ  iminā niddisitabbanti ācariyaina niyamaitvā vibhattaṃ, ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ katamaina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhagavatā paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā paṭikkhittakāraṇaina suttai āgataina vatthunā kāraṇaphalabhāvaina niddisitabbaṃ| yaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ yāsu yāsu bhūmīsu dissati, taṃ pana paṭikkhittaṃ tāsu tāsu bhūmīsu āgataina pākaṭaina akappiyānulaumaina niddisitabbaṃ|

‘‘anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca katamaina niddisitabba’’nti vattabbattā ‘‘anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca tadubhayaina niddisitabba’’nti vuttaṃ| yasmiṃ yasmiṃ suttai anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca āgataṃ, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ suttai āgataina tadubhayaina niddisitabbaṃ|

‘‘thavau katamaina niddisitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘thavau pasaṃsāyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| yasmiṃ yasmiṃ suttai yā yā pasaṃsā āgatā, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ suttai āgatāya tāya tāya pasaṃsāya thavau niddisitabbau| ‘‘yau thavau pasaṃsāya niddisitabbau, sau thavau katividhaina niddisitabbau’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘sau pañ‍cavidhainā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhagavatau thavau ca dhammassa thavau ca ariyasaṅghassa thavau ca ariyadhammānaṃ sikkhāya thavau ca laukiyaguṇasampattiyā thavau cāti pañ‍cavidhaina vaiditabbau| iti aivaṃ vuttappakāraina pañ‍cavidhaina thavau niddisitabbau|

amhākācariya amhākācariyaina aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā sāsanapaṭṭhānai daṭṭhabbāti vuttā, ‘‘katamāni tāni aṭṭhārasa mūlapadānī’’ti pucchitabbattā ‘‘indriyabhūmī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| sāsanapaṭṭhānai indriyabhūmi saddhindriyādiindriyabhūmi yaihi navahi padaihi niddisitabbā, sāsanapaṭṭhānai kilaisabhūmi yaihi navahi padaihi niddisitabbā, aivaṃ iminā pakāraina aitāni mūlapadāni nava padāni kusalapadāni, nava padāni akusalapadānīti aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni haunti| sāsanapaṭṭhānai daṭṭhabbā, ‘‘kaina kāraṇaina aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā sāsanapaṭṭhānai daṭṭhabbāti viñ‍ñāyatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘tathā hī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tathā hīti tatau aiva aṭṭhārasamūlapadānaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānai daṭṭhabbattā ‘‘aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbā? sāsanapaṭṭhānai’’ti yaṃ  vacanaṃ vuttaṃ, taina vacanaina viñ‍ñāyatīti| ‘‘kaina mūlapadānaṃ navakusalapadanavaakusalapadabhāvaina  aṭṭhārasabhāvau viñ‍ñāyatī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘taināhā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taina mūlapadānaṃ navakusalapadanavaakusalapadabhāvatau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau –

‘‘navahi ca padaihi kusalā, navahi ca yuj‍janti akusalapakkhā|

aitai khau mūlapadā, bhavanti aṭṭhārasa padānī’’ti|| –

yaṃ vacanaṃ āha, taina ‘‘navahi…pai॰… padānī’’ti vacanaina mūlapadānaṃ navakusalapadanavaakusalapadabhāvaina aṭṭhārasabhāvau viñ‍ñāyatīti|

‘‘yaṃ yaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyādisauḷasavidhaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānañ‍caiva yaṃ yaṃ laukiyādiaṭṭhavīsatividhaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānañ‍ca ācariyaina niddhāritaṃ, aittakamaiva paripuṇṇaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ niddhāraitvā yuttaṃ yuj‍jitabbaṃ natthī’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘niyuttaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhāna’’nti vuttaṃ| yathāniddhāritasāsanapaṭṭhānatau yaṃ yaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ niddhāritaṃ atthi, taṃ taṃ añ‍ñaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ niyuttaṃ yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā yuttaṃ yuj‍jitabbanti atthau gahaitabbauti|

‘‘yaṃ laukau pūjayatai…pai॰… niyuttaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānanti yattakau vacanak‍kamau bhāsitau, aittakaina vacanak‍kamaina kiṃ naitti samattā, udāhu asamattā’’ti vattabbattā ‘‘aittāvatā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina yā naitti bhāsitā, bhagavatā sā naitti anumauditā, mūlasaṅgītiyaṃ saṅgāyantaihi thairāsabhaihi yā naitti saṅgītā, sā naitti ‘‘yaṃ laukau pūjayatai…pai॰… niyuttaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhāna’’nti aittāvatā vacanak‍kamaina samattā paripuṇṇāva hauti|

iti samattāya āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina bhāsitāya bhagavatā anumauditāya mūlasaṅgītiyaṃ saṅgāyantaihi thairāsabhaihi saṅgītāya naittiyā atthavaṇṇanā saddhammapālanāmaina mahādhammarājagurunā mahāthairaina racitā jinaputtānaṃ hitakarā naittivibhāvanā chabbīsādhikanavasatai sak‍karājai sāvaṇamāsai suk‍kapakkhai navamadivasai sūriyuggamanasamayai samattā|

iti sāsanapaṭṭhānai sattibalānurūpā racitā vibhāvanā

niṭṭhitā|

nigamanakathā

sabbasattuttamau  nāthau, laukai uppaj‍ji nāyakau|

sambuddhau gautamau jinau, anaikaguṇalaṅkatau||

sāsanaṃ tassa saiṭṭhassa, aṭṭhavassasatādhikaṃ|

dvisahassaṃ yadā pattaṃ, nimmalaṃ vaḍḍhanaṃ subhaṃ||

tadā bhūmissarau mahādhammarājā mahiddhikau|

āṇācak‍kaina sāraiti, rājā nauanuvattakai||

laddhā saitagajai varai, laukai vimhayajānakai|

appamattau mahāvīrau, puñ‍ñaṃ katvābhimaudati||

tasmiṃ vassaiva sāvaṇai, māsai navamadivasai|

sūriyuggamanai kālai, nibbattāyaṃ vibhāvanā||

yattakaṃ sāsanaṃ ṭhitaṃ, tattakaṃ racitaṃ mayā|

ṭhātu naittivibhāvanā, jinaputtahitāvahā||

iti taṃ racayantaina, puñ‍ñaṃ adhigataṃ mayā|

hauntu tassānubhāvaina, sabbai vimuttibhāginau||

rājadaivī  puttanattā, panattā ca sajātikā|

sabbai raj‍jasukhai ṭhatvā, carantu caritaṃ sukhī||

daivau kālai suvassatu, sabbau raṭṭhajanau sukhī|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ ahiṃsantau, piyau hautu hitāvahauti||

naittivibhāvinī niṭṭhitā|
